- Published as a **pre-release** (tag `v0.1.4` on `3c876df`, `CloudStream-0.1.4-windows-x64.msi`, 377,118,924 bytes, sha256 `a1d0ffff4c03f9fd4e4ea35500610fcf3940a84a0ae00a0f2b3d04376142367d`; ProductVersion 0.1.4 read from the MSI), `make_latest=false`; release id 402880492. Process as for 0.1.3: `./gradlew :app:test :app:installerMsi` with JAVA_HOME and WiX `bin` (8aab2d1d scratchpad) on PATH (4 min), commit, `git push origin main`, then `release014.ps1` (draft -> upload with curl -> publish). Changelog entry 0.1.4 is the release notes.
- Gotcha: the PowerShell tool does not forward stdin to `git credential fill` ("refusing to work with credential missing protocol field"): fetch the token in Bash (`printf 'protocol=https\nhost=github.com\n\n' | git credential fill | sed -n 's/^password=//p'`), export it as `CS_TOKEN` for the script, never print it, unset it afterwards.
- Not done / untested: a real upgrade from an installed 0.1.3 through the in-app updater; the pacer on a second monitor with another refresh rate and on remote desktop (falls back to mpv's blocking); the real OpenSubtitles block live during playback (the blocked path was forced with `/ossub?...&block=1`).

## 25. Release 0.1.4 withdrawn, release 0.1.5 (2026-10-04): the installer deleted a user's data

- **What happened**: 0.1.4 was published at 12:07 and withdrawn (release deleted, tag `v0.1.4` left) the same hour. Reproducing a report "the downloaded setup is not opening" I ran the 0.1.4 MSI over the installed 0.1.3 and it **deleted `%LOCALAPPDATA%\CloudStream\data` (826 MB: settings, accounts, extensions, backups)**. Cause (verbose log): jpackage's MSI has a component with `util:RemoveFolderEx` on INSTALLDIR (`WixRemoveFolderEx`, property `RM_RF48431...` read by `AppSearch` from the registry). A major upgrade first removes the older product (`RemoveExistingProducts`, sequence 798, then `WixRemoveFoldersEx` 799) and the OLDER product's own table recursively wipes the install folder: `WixRemoveFoldersEx: Recursing path C:\Users\...\AppData\Local\CloudStream\`. An installed copy keeps its data in `INSTALLDIR\data` when that folder exists next to the exe (portable-mode detection, `AndroidRuntime`), which was the case for this user (the folder pre-dated the MSI era). Earlier upgrades had not wiped it (not understood why; same-version "maintenance" double-clicks and failed 1306 attempts were mixed in). My mistake: I ran an installer on a live install without copying the data first.
- **Lesson**: never run an installer against the user's real install (or any code path that deletes) without a backup of everything it may touch; test upgrades in a sandbox state (marker files), as done below.
- **The fix (0.1.5)** is a post-build patch of the MSI, `tools/patch-msi.ps1` (called by `installerMsi` after jpackage; edits the MSI tables through the Windows Installer COM API):
  1. `CsKeepData` (immediate, sequence 797, `NOT Installed`): finds the older install folder (registry uninstall entry of `JP_UPGRADABLE_FOUND`, HKCU/HKLM/WOW6432Node, default `%LOCALAPPDATA%\CloudStream\`), stops `jcef_helper.exe` processes of that folder whose parent is gone, and MOVES `data` to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\CloudStream-data-keep`; `CsRestoreData` (6650, after `InstallFinalize`, `NOT Installed AND NOT REMOVE`) moves it back into the new INSTALLDIR (and does so on the next run if an earlier run was interrupted). Both are PowerShell commands run as EXE custom actions (type 114, formatted properties, no square brackets, no double quotes in the script text); the package is 32-bit so `SystemFolder` is SysWOW64: use WMI (`Win32_Process.ExecutablePath`), `Get-Process.Path` cannot read 64-bit processes from it.
  2. The new product's `WixRemoveFolderEx` rows now target `CS_RM_APP` / `CS_RM_RUNTIME` (`[RM_RF...]app`, `...runtime`, set by two type-51 actions at 790/791 only when the install folder is known) instead of the whole install folder: the program files are still removed (every component is keyed on a registry value, the files go through this wipe, not through `RemoveFiles`) but never `data`. Leftover after an uninstall: `CloudStream.exe` and `data`.
- **Tests (all in a sandbox state with marker files, on the real machine, `%APPDATA%\CloudStream` untouched)**: (a) installed published 0.1.3, data folder (43 files) + stray file + an orphaned `jcef_helper.exe` (a copy of ping started through `cmd /c start`) -> 0.1.5 silent: exit 0, data identical (same files and sizes), the stray file outside `data` DELETED (proves the older version's wipe ran), orphan stopped, no `keep` folder left; (b) the same from the published 0.1.4 clicking through the real dialogs (UI Automation: the installer's Next / Install are *panes*, press them through `LegacyIAccessiblePattern` or `InvokePattern`): exit 0, all identical; (c) uninstall of 0.1.5 with data present: app and runtime gone, data kept. Not run: 0.1.5 -> a later release (a re-versioned copy needs a new PackageCode; the scoped wipe is the same mechanism as test (c)).
- **Stuck Chromium helper** (why the 0.1.1 / 0.1.3 first attempts failed with Error 1306 on `data\webview\chrome_debug.log`): a `jcef_helper.exe` orphan (parent gone; one ran 26 h; the one left from an old dev session is `--type=utility --utility-sub-type=on_device_model.mojom.OnDeviceModelService`, Chromium's on-device AI model service, which does not exit with its parent). The app never disposed CEF (`JcefRuntime.shutdown()` had no caller, the app ends with `exitProcess(0)`). `JcefRuntime` now stops its helpers in a shutdown hook (descendants + parentless ones from its own jcef folder), stops parentless helpers of its folder before CEF starts (tested with a stand-in orphan; other folders untouched), and disables the on-device model features (`--disable-features=...OptimizationGuideModelExecution,OptimizationGuideOnDeviceModel,OnDeviceModelService`). A hard kill of the app leaves no helpers (they notice), only that AI service lingers, hence the start-up cleanup and the installer step.
- Ruled out for "the setup is not opening": file damaged (sha256 identical), SmartScreen/Defender (no events), the MSI itself (opens in about a second, Welcome page up; with several double-clicks several Setup windows open at once and only one install can run).
- **Published**: pre-release `v0.1.5` on `c364db3`, `CloudStream-0.1.5-windows-x64.msi` (377,122,816 bytes, sha256 `1628c6034153f0b14b20d4fcb5b779163266f1404a0b77e8f23444b5fafb9232`, the exact file the sandbox tests ran on), release id 402900259, `make_latest=false`. At the owner's request the old releases `v0.1.0`, `v0.1.1`, `v0.1.3` were deleted (0.1.4 earlier); the git tags `v0.1.0`, `v0.1.1`, `v0.1.3`, `v0.1.4` still exist. Gotcha: the deletion loop first sent malformed ids (grep of a one-line JSON gave many numbers): nothing was deleted, then it was redone with `grep -o '"id": *[0-9]*' | head -1` and a check that the release's `tag_name` equals the tag asked for; GitHub's release list is cached for about a minute after a delete (the tag endpoints answer 404 at once).
- **Not tested**: the in-app updater of an installed 0.1.3 / 0.1.4 downloading and starting this MSI (only `msiexec` runs); a custom install folder chosen in the dialog; a later upgrade from 0.1.5 with a real second release; why two interactive attempts of the owner ended with status 1 at 12:22 (likely cancelled; the same dialogs complete now).
- The installed copy of the owner's PC is now 0.1.5 (left by the tests), its `data` folder next to the exe was deleted by my first test and not recovered; it now falls back to `%APPDATA%\CloudStream` (last written 2 Oct 17:23), untouched.

## 26. Sign-in to OpenSubtitles and SubDL (2026-10-04, after 0.1.5)

- Reported: "logins not working" for both. The servers are fine (curl and the app's own HTTP client, system DNS and DNS setting 2, also the packaged app on an empty data folder: `/netcheck`, new dev endpoint `/httpprobe`), the dialog opens and submits. What was wrong is what the user sees and what the dialog could not do: (1) SubDL answers a wrong login with HTTP 200 `{"status":false,"error":"Email or password is not valid."}`; `SubDlApi.login` parsed that as a login (`OAuthTokenResponse` demands `token`, and `UserData` demanded country, scStepCode, scVerified, scUsername ...) and the dialog showed "Instantiation of ... value failed for JSON property token due to missing (therefore NULL) value ..."; (2) SubDL accounts made with "Sign in with Google" have no password, so e-mail + password can never work for them; (3) OpenSubtitles' blocked login server (Airtel court-order web page) was a raw Jackson error, a wrong login said only "Invalid password or username" (it wants the user NAME, not the e-mail); (4) a token refresh that fails (every search runs `freshAuth()`, tokens last 24 h) threw out of the whole search before the legacy fallback of section 23c could run.
- Now: SubDL `login` reads the answers tolerantly (`readLoginAnswer`) and shows SubDL's own reason; an EMPTY e-mail means "the password field holds the API key" (checked with a search, `loginWithApiKey`; a wrong key is 403 `not_authorized`); OpenSubtitles shows the service's message plus the user-name hint, a clear text for the block page (and marks the new API as blocked), and `refreshToken` returns null instead of throwing (search and the legacy download then go on without an account). The dialog shows a one-line hint per service and the SubDL field reads "Password or API key". Unit tests `SubtitleLoginAnswersTest` (7, shapes captured from the live services with fake details). Verified in the real dialog (source build on an empty data folder): hints, empty-form messages, Ctrl+V paste into a field.
- Not verified: a successful sign-in (no real credentials may be typed by me), the API-key success path (needs a real key), and the packaged installed app has the OLD login code until a new MSI is released. The owner's installed app was running during this work (started 13:26:54): not touched; the portable build was rebuilt (separate folder, its `data` untouched).
- **Release 0.1.6 (2026-10-04)**: the sign-in fixes above as pre-release `v0.1.6` on `178b2fc`, `CloudStream-0.1.6-windows-x64.msi` (377,139,200 bytes, sha256 `cd657806838a660daa7c137d0e7511066e35d80bb95954d3d189b9d63aba9519`), release id 402919856, `make_latest=false`; v0.1.5 stays (the older releases were deleted at the owner's request in section 25, only that one time). Built with `:app:test :app:installerMsi` (11 tests pass), the MSI tables were read back (ProductVersion, Upgrade rule, CsKeepData / CsRestoreData / scoped wipe rows). NOT run: an upgrade 0.1.5 -> 0.1.6 on a real install (the owner's installed copy was open and is the real install: never test installers on it, section 25); it uses the same mechanism as the tested 0.1.3 / 0.1.4 -> 0.1.5 upgrades and the uninstall test of 0.1.5.

Full write-up with measurements and the roadmap: `docs/IMPROVEMENT_PLAN.md`. Short version:

Draw uses a Skia shader (the image overloads with a sampling mode or paint draw nothing in a layer). `FramePacer.hold` is skipped in this mode.
- **Render cap** `-Dcloudstream.rendercap` (default 3.7 Mpix), **monitor-aware `VBlankClock`** (`MonitorFromWindow` -> `CreateDCW` -> `D3DKMTOpenAdapterFromHdc`), **web page as link fails fast** (`MpvPlayer.loadPlayer`).
- **JIO TV (IND)**: the PlayFy worker (`jiotv-mixed.alpha-circuit.workers.dev`) answers this PC with a decoy redirect (curl and the plugin's own OkHttp alike); the owner's phone works, this PC is on Cloudflare WARP; not proven, needs a WARP-off test. LivXow's WILLOW link is 403 at the CDN, PlayFy's WILLOW plays. DRM (ClearKey) itself works.
- **Measurements**: video cadence is ideal (24/25 fps on 60 Hz = 2-3 rhythm, which is the judder); JDK 21 sleeps are precise (0.1 ms); UI steady frames 16.7 ms, first open of a screen 93-348 ms.
- **Tools**: `-Dcloudstream.framestats=1` + `/framestats?s=`, `/look?smooth=true|false`, `/videostats` prints `smooth:`; `EmbedProto` (`./gradlew :app:runEmbedProto`): mpv GPU in an AWT canvas + transparent owned Compose window over it works; `SwingPanel` interop blending does not show a native child window.

- Gotchas: Windows `sed -i` on a CRLF file rewrites it with LF (use a Java splice, `MainActivity.kt` and `SettingsUpdatesScreen.kt` are CRLF); a hand-killed test instance leaves a "Safe mode" dialog (crash flag) in that data folder; dev `/click` takes screenshot pixels.

- **Later the same day**: white flicker = `bgr0` alpha 0 in the shader blend (mpv now outputs `bgra`); a full GC every 30 s from upstream `DataStore.Editor.apply()` -> `System.gc()` removed; `ScreenWarmup` (AppShell) makes first visits of pages smooth; `Diagnostics`, `LogBuffer.startFile` (`logs/app.log`); `SubtitleCat` provider (+ `SubtitleCatTest`); native GPU player (`NativeVideo.kt`, `MpvPlayer.nativeWindow`, `Appearance.nativePlayer`, `Anime4K.kt`, `Shortcuts.kt`); `compose.interop.blending=false` set in `Main`. Test tools: `/look?native=true|false&blend=off|alpha1`, `/framestats`; spike detector approach (capture the test window made topmost with `SWP_NOACTIVATE`, flag one capture far brighter than both neighbours). Never send real keystrokes to the owner's desktop.

## 28. Footprint (2026-10-06): WebView2 instead of bundled Chromium, smaller install, less memory

Users said the app is too heavy and too large. Measured on 0.1.8 with a copy of the owner's data: MSI 377 MB (bundled Chromium 155 MB of it), about 595 MB installed, and after the first Cloudflare check another ~680 MB in the data folder (`data/jcef` 353 MB unpacked Chromium, which took ~2 min the first time; `data/webview` 208 MB profile, 110 MB of it Chrome components; `data/cache/natives` a second 116 MB copy of mpv). Memory: app ~730 MB at rest, and once an extension needed a page (AnimePahe's Cloudflare check on Home) 1.13 GB plus 9 `jcef_helper` processes with 710 MB that never ended (GPU process 200 MB; extension WebViews are never destroyed). The owner chose to move to WebView2.

- **WebView2 backend** (Edge engine that ships with Windows 10/11, JNA COM, no binding library): `runtime/web/wv2/Com.kt` (vtable calls, Java COM handler objects), `WebView2Runtime.kt` (STA thread "webview2" with a Win32 message loop, `WebView2Loader.dll` from `app/native/windows-x64` via `NativeFiles`, environment in `data/webview2`, browser arguments through `WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS`, hidden parking window at -32000, idle shutdown 30 s after the last page), `Wv2Browser.kt` (one page: controller+core in its own child window, created on first use, `withCore` queue), `android/webkit/WebView2Engine.kt` (the WebView contract: clients, popups, JS dialogs, downloads, certificate errors, console via DevTools, JS bridge `JsBridge.kt` shared with JCEF, `shouldInterceptRequest` with deferrals, `loadDataWithBaseURL` served through WebResourceRequested, on-screen display = heavyweight AWT Canvas that becomes the parent of the page window), `HeadlessBrowser.kt` (facade; `Wv2Headless` for WebViewResolver). Vtable indexes come from WebView2.h of Microsoft.Web.WebView2 1.0.4258.31 (`vtbl.txt` generated in the session scratchpad). `WebViewBackend.create` picks WebView2 when `WebView2Runtime.isAvailable`, else `JcefWebViewEngine`; `-Dcloudstream.web=jcef` forces JCEF.
- **Cookies live in the app** (`runtime/web/WebCookies.kt`, `data/web/cookies.bin`, DPAPI-encrypted): `CookieManager.getCookie` never starts a browser any more (it started Chromium before). The engine is a mirror: the first page of an environment gets the whole jar (DeleteAll + add), changes are pushed, the browser's jar is pulled after every navigation (before `onPageFinished`) and every 1.5 s while a page is open. A WebView2 cookie manager belongs to one page and dies with it: it is taken from a live page each time. JCEF fallback mirrors the same way.
- **Forgotten pages are parked**: extensions never call `destroy()` (EnmaDecryptor in StreamPlay keeps hexa.su open for good, AnimePahe's Cloudflare dialogs). A page off screen with no extension call for 2 min (20 s if it was shown in a dialog) is closed and reopens the URL the extension last loaded on its next use (work waits for that load). Page navigations (ads, redirects) do not count as use. `/webstate` lists pages, who made them and why they live.
- **Packaging**: bundled `jcef-natives` removed (jcefmaven downloads Chromium on first use only where WebView2 is missing); `mpv-2.dll` + `WebView2Loader.dll` are plain files (`appResourcesRootDir = app/native`, `app/resources` in the image), loaded from there; the old unpacked copy in `data/cache/natives` is deleted; old `data/jcef` and `data/webview` are deleted 20 s after start when WebView2 is in use; `compressRuntime()` re-links the runtime with `--compress=zip-6` in portableDist/installerMsi (modules 87 -> 35 MB).
- **Memory flags**: `-XX:MaxHeapFreeRatio=30 -XX:MinHeapFreeRatio=10 -XX:G1PeriodicGCInterval=30000` (heap 370 -> 200 MB committed after browsing, ~130 MB live), `skiko.gpu.resourceCacheLimit` 64 MB (Main.kt; D3D12 heaps are system RAM on integrated GPUs). OpenGL renderer measured worse (1097 vs 965 MB). Coil disk cache fixed at 150 MB.
- **Results** (same data, same navigation): MSI 377 -> 213 MB; installed ~595 -> 386 MB; data folder no longer grows by ~680 MB (WebView2 profile ~24 MB); app process 1130 -> ~910 MB after browsing; browser processes 710 MB for good -> ~325-480 MB only while a page is open, 0 after idle. WebView2 starts in 12-50 ms (Chromium: ~2 min first time). AnimePahe's Cloudflare check now passes by itself in ~9 s (failed with JCEF). Harness `phisher,cnc full streamplay cricify sktech livxow`: all home/search/load/links OK.
- **Dev endpoints**: `/webstate`, `/resolvetest?url=&match=` (WebViewResolver), `/cookietest` prints `webview2=`; `-Dcloudstream.webdebug=true` logs navigations and cookie pulls.
- **Not done / next**: libmpv is a full build (Vulkan/shaderc/libplacebo, Blu-ray/DVD, VapourSynth, openmpt, encoders: 115 MB); a playback-only build could be ~35-45 MB but needs an MSYS2/mingw toolchain (owner's decision). Remaining app memory is mostly Direct3D 12 heaps of skiko (64 MB blocks) and the JVM; the CDS archive costs ~85 MB private on Windows but saves about the same in metaspace. The real upgrade of an installed copy to this build was not run (never touch the real install). Uncommitted.

## 29. IStreamFlare seeking and subtitle fonts (2026-10-06, later)

- **IStreamFlare (Phisher) could not seek**: its links are OK.ru DASH manifests of profile isoff-on-demand (`SegmentBase` + `indexRange`, one fragmented MP4 per quality, audio separate). ffmpeg's dashdec takes such a file for one endless segment (plays, no seek); ExoPlayer reads the sidx. `player/OnDemandDash.kt` reads a DASH manifest ahead (thread "dash-probe", `MpvPlayer.loadPlayer` waits for it, `loadGeneration` drops stale results, `onDemand` cache with `NO_FILES`) and, for static manifests with only SegmentBase, plays the best video up to the screen height (at least 1080) with `audio-files-append=%n%<audio url>`; mpv seeks both with ranges. Measured: 1064p + external AAC, `seekable=yes`, seeks to 50:00 and back resume in ~2 s, avsync 0.01 ms. If those files fail, the manifest is played the old way.
- The CDN wants IStreamFlare's exact user agent, which has a Cyrillic "е" in "likе Gecko" (a normal one gets 400). OkHttp refuses non-ASCII values and the proxies dropped the header silently (`runCatching { builder.header }`): `net/RawHeaders.set` sends it as UTF-8 (`addUnsafeNonAscii`), used by DashProxy, HlsProxy, RangeProxy.
- **Subtitle fonts** (`SubtitleStyler`, `SubtitleSettings.fontChoice`): one Font list in the style editor (Settings and the player's Customise dialog): default, packaged fonts, `SubtitleStyler.windowsFonts` (only the ones the PC has; stored as `font=Custom` + the Windows path, libass finds them by name, nothing copied), the user's file, "Font file…" (copied to `files/subtitle-fonts`). Fixed: Google Sans is a font-family XML (members copied, family "Product Sans"); custom files were always copied over `custom-font.ttf`, which libass keeps open, so a second custom font fell back to sans-serif: copies are now named by content hash and older ones removed; family names come from the `name` table (`familyOf`, also CFF OTF and TTC); a font file new to the fonts folder restarts the subtitle track (`sid` off/on) because libass reads that folder only when a track starts. Verified on screen: Product Sans, Georgia, Consolas and an unknown renamed font changed mid-video; the style carries over to the next video and the next run.
- Dev endpoints: `/playsearch?provider=&q=&link=` (search, first movie, first link, play), `/substyle?font=<SubtitleFont>|none` or `file=<path>`.

## 30. Playback-only libmpv (2026-10-07)

`app/native/windows-x64/mpv-2.dll` is now our own build: **39.3 MB instead of 120.8 MB** (portable folder 386 -> 308 MB). Same mpv
(`a1bf4b655`) and FFmpeg (`N-126902-g7e1f63d5d`) as the full build it replaces; the other libraries are taken as of 2026-09-27.
Built with MSYS2 (UCRT64) by `tools/libmpv/build.sh` (stages `fetch deps ffmpeg mpv`, work folder `~/mpvbuild`); everything is
linked statically into the one DLL, which imports only Windows system DLLs (the full build also needed `vulkan-1.dll` and OpenGL).

- **Kept**: libass (DirectWrite fonts, HarfBuzz, FriBidi, FreeType), uchardet + iconv (subtitle charsets), libplacebo, zimg (the
  standard player's frame conversion: without it a 1080p60 frame took 19 ms instead of 2.5 ms and frames dropped), d3d11 vo with
  shaderc + SPIRV-Cross (native GPU player, Anime4K), d3d11va/dxva2 hwaccels, dav1d, libcurl (mpv's HTTP stream; Schannel TLS +
  nghttp2), FFmpeg decoders/demuxers/parsers for the formats extensions serve (H.264/HEVC/VP9/AV1/MPEG-2/VC-1..., AAC/AC-3/E-AC-3/
  TrueHD/DTS/Opus/FLAC..., text and bitmap subtitles, HLS/DASH/MP4/MKV/TS/FLV/AVI/ASF/RTSP/RTMP), the `spdif` muxer (audio decoder HW / HW+).
- **Dropped**: encoders (x264, x265, SVT-AV1, aom, vpx), Vulkan/OpenGL, Blu-ray/DVD/CD, VapourSynth, OpenMPT, Lua/JavaScript,
  libarchive, rubberband, lcms2, SSH/SRT, avdevice, all lavfi filters except a handful.
- **Checked**: same links replayed through both DLLs (link lab `-Dlinklab.replay=<...-links.tsv>`, 40 links of StreamPlay,
  CineStream, AnimePahe, Kisskh, IStreamFlare): same result for 39, the 40th (Pixeldrain) flaky; failures are source-side on both.
  Standard player 1080p60 HLS 0 drops (2.5 ms/frame, as before); native GPU player + Anime4K steady drops equal (first run of a
  new build compiles shaders: a few drops at the start); memory the same (only touched DLL pages count); SRT with Devanagari,
  custom font file not installed in Windows (Gotham), DASH 4K with seeking, packaged exe.
- MSYS2 stays in `C:\msys64` for rebuilds (removable).

## 31. IStreamFlare / CNCVerse playback, seek feedback, source priority (2026-10-07, later)

- **Static DASH with segment templates** (IStreamFlare's OK.ru `type/2` manifests, `SegmentTemplate` + `SegmentTimeline`): ffmpeg's DASH reader opened all qualities (14 s) and stalled after a seek. `OnDemandDash` now also turns such manifests (static, one period, no ContentProtection) into HLS media playlists (init as EXT-X-MAP), served by `HlsProxy.serve` (`/gen`), played as video + `audio-files-append` like the on-demand files. Opens in ~3 s (Akamai test stream 0.7 s), seeks normally.
- **HLS masters** (CNCVerse Netflix / Prime Video / Hotstar): mpv 0.41 maps HLS variants to *editions* (the track list only has the current one's tracks). `readTracks` lists the editions as video tracks (`edition:N`, heights from `HlsProxy.variants`), `setMaxVideoSize` sets `edition` (switches in place, audio and subtitle kept), `chooseBestEdition` picks the best height up to the screen on open. Subtitle renditions that are one whole file (`#EXTINF:99999`, never delivered by ffmpeg's HLS reader) are left out of the master and added with `sub-add` (`HlsProxy.streamSubtitles`, listed with the embedded ones); the language is reduced to its code (Prime: `en-us.[CC]`). First segments of recorded media playlists are fetched in parallel at master time and served from `/seg` (ffmpeg opens 3 variants + 21 audio renditions one after the other): open 9 s -> ~5 s.
- Loading ring: mpv's `seeking` property counts as buffering. Fallback pause: a pause nobody asked for in the 20 s after a load is undone (could not be reproduced with 404s, hangs, cut-off streams or bad playlists; `scratchpad BadServer.java` style tests).
- Source priority: the desktop player never offered it (Android's player menu does) and Settings listed only sources with a saved priority (none). Settings now lists all extractor and provider names; the Sources dialog has a priority button (`PlayerSession.openSourcePriority`). Profile type WiFi is shown as "Stream" (`R.string.profile_stream`, stored name unchanged), Data is hidden.
- Native GPU player and Anime4K checked with the slim libmpv: d3d11va, Anime4K shader loaded without errors, picture drawn under the floating controls; kept.
- Pitfall: `sed -i` in Git Bash rewrote CRLF files to LF (whole-file diffs); inserting a string in the middle of `values/strings.xml` shifted resource ids (the account name showed "null"). New strings go at the end; edit CRLF files with a program that keeps the line ends.

## 33. Subtitle fixes, renderer option, one-click player menus (2026-10-07, night)

- Renderer: owner reversed section 32's forced look -> option `Appearance.subtitleUniversal` (pref `desktop_look_sub_universal`, default false). SubtitleStyler: own = `sub-ass-override=scale` + empty `sub-ass-style-overrides`; universal = `strip` + Bold/Italic/Alignment overrides. UI: card at the top of SubtitleStyleEditor (settings page + player dialog).
- Wrong subtitles: EMBEDDED_IN_VIDEO SubtitleData have url = mpv track id; they stayed in `vm.state.subtitles` across source switches and a kept `selectedSubtitle` selected "track N" of the new file. `loadLink` now drops embedded entries, clears numeric failed ids and replaces an embedded pick by the language auto pick.
- Sync: `autoSubtitle` prefers full (not forced/signs/song) embedded tracks of the language; `autoSelectSubtitles` upgrades an automatic URL pick to the video's own track when it is listed.
- Repeated pill: `applySubtitleSelection` returns when the wanted track is already `sid` (every vm subtitle batch re-applied it); PlayerSession `announcedSubtitle` suppresses Loading/Loaded pills for a subtitle already announced in the episode. Seeks do not reload subtitles (checked with arrow keys on CNCVerse Prime + Kisskh).
- Menus: PlayerDialogs rewritten (check-mark rows, instant apply, lists scrolled to the current item, ✕ + click outside + Esc; no pause). `Overlays.Dialog(dismissOnOutside)`; dialog appear 180 ms. IconButton with a tooltip puts its modifier on an inner node (wrap in a Box to align).
- Video click: own pointer loop (detectTapGestures delayed single taps ~300 ms); double click restores the pre-click play state and toggles fullscreen. Note `ensurePlaying` un-pauses within 2 s of FILE_LOADED (a click then is undone - by design).
- Dev notes: `/click` takes screenshot pixels (scale 1.5); test-videos.co.uk only has 10 s clips (use `speed=0.25`); commondatastorage sample does not load here.

## 34. One player menu, one colour rule, native player timing (2026-10-07, late)

- Owner: "video, audio, subtitle out of sync, stuttering like hanging; some bars white, some blue; menus and sub menus not proper; nothing feels united".
- **Playback measured** (portable exe on a copy of the owner's data, sync neutralised, `-Xlog:gc`): test HLS, Prime Video (Sardar 2, Tamil), Kisskh, seeks and fullscreen: mpv `avsync` 0.00 s, 0 dropped frames. GC pauses 2-12 ms (not a cause). Standard player: late frames cluster in the ~20 s after a load or seek (17 late, max 82 ms, uneven 1/4-refresh cadence), steady and while using the controls 0 late. **Native GPU player was broken by its timing**: `video-sync=display-resample` + interpolation on a child window that sees a jittery refresh (mpv: ~54 Hz, vsync-jitter 0.46 on a 60 Hz screen) -> 66 drops / 155 mistimed frames in 40 s; with mpv's default audio timing 0 drops and 0 late frames -> options removed. The owner's severe desync was not reproduced; `logs/jank.txt` is full since 2026-10-02 (400 KB cap) and `logs/app.log` is not written, so the session left no trace. Ultima's `onActivityResumed` runs on the UI thread on window focus (DesktopLifecycle, every 15 s at most): a possible short freeze of the standard player's picture.
- **Player menu** (`PlayerMenu.kt`): one dark card above the buttons at the bottom right with pages: Source (+ Priority), Quality (named 1080p / 720p ... by the usual tier, exact size as detail), Audio, Subtitles (groups, delay and size page, style, add file, search online, first online result), Playback speed, Picture size, Audio output (SW / HW / HW+), Open in VLC / browser. Buttons: Subtitles, Sources (link icon), Settings (gear) open it at their page; click outside or Esc closes it, nothing pauses. Removed: the top-right Sources / Tracks pills and their big dialogs, the speed / picture size / external flyouts. The subtitle style editor stays a dialog.
- **One colour rule**: accent = position / value (seek bar, volume, every Slider, progress bars, the home hero timer, the volume bubble's level); white = the main button on video and every knob (Slider knob is now a white disc like the seek bar's); one surface for every panel and bubble over the video (`PlayerSurface` 0xF21A1A1C, border 0x24FFFFFF). All bottom-bar buttons 40 dp / 18 dp icons.
- **Stutter found and fixed (standard player)**: with smooth motion on (the default) `MpvPlayer.frameRenderer` skipped `FramePacer.hold` for every video, but blending only runs when the frame rate does not divide the refresh (`blendUseful`, 24/25 fps). 30 and 60 fps videos (Kisskh dramas, much TV) were therefore paced by nobody: mpv hands frames over ~1 frame early without blocking (`BLOCK_FOR_TARGET_TIME=0`), so they showed at whichever refresh came next and up to a frame ahead of the sound. Measured on Kisskh 29.97 fps: 58-90 frames a minute out of rhythm (1/3-refresh pairs in clusters every ~15 s); now the pacer runs unless blending is active: ~10 a minute, mostly single, decided cadence clean. 24 fps unchanged (smooth on and off: steady 2/3). `FramePacer` also smooths mpv's target times (~0.5-1.5 ms jitter: predicted from the average frame length, pulled 1/16 towards mpv, jumps > 8 ms followed at once) and reports decided cadence + jitter in `/videostats` (`pacer:` line).
- `logs/jank.txt` now starts again at 400 KB (old one kept as `jank.txt.1`) with dates; it had stopped on 2026-10-02.
- Not changed: smooth motion (frame blending, on by default) shows double images on pans in screenshots; the native player stays opt-in.

## 35. Subtitles: MovieBox never loaded, StreamPlay picked "random" ones (2026-10-07, night)

- **MovieBox**: its DASH streams become the app's own HLS playlists (`/gen`), opened with `demuxer=lavf,demuxer-lavf-format=hls` since the Mandaadi crash fix; the forced format also applied to every external file added later, so each SRT failed (`avformat_open_input() failed`). Now only `demuxer=lavf`.
- **StreamPlay**: one film = 385 subtitles (~30 English: OpenSubtitles/strem.io, rezesubs, MovieBox's per-dub files, each host's own); the first English by name was picked (usually another release: out of sync). `PlayerSession.bestForSource`: same site as the video link / referer (+2), same "(… Audio)" tag as the source name (+1), then list order; re-checked when the source changes and when subtitles arrive after it started. MovieBox now picks "English (Original Audio)" for the original-audio source, StreamPlay/Movy its own `moon.zenoak.top` VTT.
- A subtitle on the video's own site that is refused (Movy 403) is fetched again with the video's headers / referer (`MpvPlayer.downloadSubtitle`).
- Lists drop duplicates (same link twice); the menu shows the site of each online file ("Online · rezesubs.com"). Dev: `/sublist`.
- **Later**: CNCVerse whole-file subtitles were measured against the speech in the audio (mpv `ao=pcm` to a WAV, `Env.java` loudness envelope): lines showed 0.71-0.93 s after speech onsets (Netflix Squid Game, 5 lines; Prime Sardar 2, 5 lines), identical with the previous full libmpv (not a build problem). `MpvPlayer.shiftedStreamSubtitle` fetches such a VTT (no X-TIMESTAMP-MAP) once and adds it with cues 850 ms earlier. The mouse wheel no longer changes the volume while a player menu, the episode list or a dialog is open.

## 36. Memory while a film plays (2026-10-08)

Owner: "too much RAM usage". Measured with the packaged exe on a scratch copy of the data (`-XX:NativeMemoryTracking`, a VirtualQueryEx walk of the JVM, `jcmd GC.class_histogram -all`), playing a 24 fps HLS test stream muted.

- **Cause: an unclosed Skia `Shader` per refresh** (`PlayerScreen.drawVideoImage`, the smooth-motion mix path, used for 24/25 fps video on a 60 Hz screen): the shader holds the frame (8.3 MB at 1921x1129), so every frame lived until a Java GC ran the cleaner. Private memory climbed ~200 MB/s to ~1.9 GB and fell back at each GC (saw-tooth 1.1-1.9 GB, peak working set 2.0 GB); Java heap, NMT and the Java object counts showed nothing because the memory is native (106 separate 8.3 MB blocks in the walk; `jcmd GC.run` dropped 730 MB at once). Present since 0.1.4. Fix: `makeShader(...).use { ... }`. Playback now holds at ~1.0 GB commit / ~720 MB private working set (peak WS 757 MB), back to ~600 MB on Home.
- Reproduced outside the app (scratchpad `D3dTest.java`: a Direct3D `SkiaLayer`, per-frame `Image.makeFromBitmap` + close is flat at ~493 MB; with an open shader it grew 400 MB/2 s; with the shader closed flat again), so Skia's raster-image path itself is fine.
- **Measured, not changed**: the start-up browser (StreamPlay's EnmaDecryptor opens hexa.su at start, extensions never destroy it) is ~650 MB working set (275-355 MB private) for the first ~2.5 min of every run (park after 2 min + idle shutdown 30 s); G1 flags `-XX:MarkStackSize=512k -XX:ParallelGCThreads=4 -XX:ConcGCThreads=1` take GC bookkeeping from 65 to 17 MB of commit (733 -> 689 MB) but the working set stays the same (577 vs 572 MB), so they were not added. Idle JVM ~500 MB private WS: heap ~150 MB, CDS archive 87 MB, code 50 MB, metaspace 30 MB, the rest native Skia/Direct3D/DLLs.
- The `C:\Program Files\CloudStream` process in Task Manager (CloudStream-Desktop.exe, 0.1.9, publisher "Ayu") is another program, not this app.

Owner list: endless loading after choosing subtitles (4KHDHub, Zinkmovies), anime episode titles cut off (KickAssAnime), no prequels/sequels, buttons only white, season picker not visible, AniList sign-in for other users, spoilers.

Accent `Button`, `PillButton` (Play, Search ...), selected `Chip` / `PillTabs`, active `GlassCircleButton`, the player's play/pause disc, carousel dots and the featured-strip ring all use it; secondary pills are glass tinted with the accent; `ComboBox`/standard buttons get an accent edge on hover. No more white main buttons (slider knobs stay white by the colour rule).
- **Details**: season picker has its own line (chips up to 6 seasons, else a 190 dp `ComboBox`), unselected chips are visible buttons; episode titles use the whole width (3 lines + tooltip), rating/filler under the title; **Related** tab for anime (`AnimeRelations.kt`: AniList GraphQL, id from the page's `anilist`/`mal` sync data or a title search, prequel/sequel/side story/spin-off/alternative..., cards open a search; gated by the TMDB "Title information" switch); **spoilers** (`Appearance.hideSpoilers` default on, `hideSpoilerTitles` off; Settings > Appearance > Spoilers; chip above the list): the still of every episode after the next unwatched one is blurred and its description hidden, the eye pill on the card reveals it.
- **AniList / MAL / Simkl for everyone**: the client IDs are public identifiers (they are in every authorise URL), so they can be built in: `anilist.key`, `mal.key`, `simkl.id` in `local.properties` (or env ANILIST_KEY / MAL_KEY / SIMKL_CLIENT_ID); register `http://localhost:52526/anilistlogin`, `/mallogin`, `/simkl` as redirect URLs. Simkl's PIN sign-in needs no secret. Nothing was changed in the sign-in flow itself.
- **Failing HubCloud "Instant Download" workers** (cdnfileshub.workers.dev, 4KHDHub/Zinkmovies style direct MKV links): they answer HTTP 403 to the 4 MiB range for minutes. Found with new `RangeProxy: upstream ... HTTP n after x ms` log lines. Before: the proxy answered 200 and closed the body early, mpv retried "partial file" for ever or opened a corrupt file at its end (`eof-reached`, black picture, nothing said). Now: `RangeProxy.handle` fetches the first piece before answering and gives the player the real error (and remembers it 20 s), mpv fails, the session goes to the next source (verified: 30 GB source refused, 65 GB source played ~15 s later); an EOF within 20 s of the open at the very end of a long file (not started there) is an `ErrorEvent` (`MpvPlayer` eof-reached rule).
- **Subtitle switch**: `watchTrackSwitch` in `MpvPlayer`: choosing another embedded track mid-playback shows the "Loading subtitles" pill, and if the picture has not moved for 12 s the file is opened again at the position with `sid=` as a loadfile option (`openWithSid`). Switching measured at 0.8-9 s on working sources (mpv re-reads from the playback position). Could not reproduce the owner's exact "endless loading" after choosing a subtitle; the 403 behaviour above is the likeliest cause. Dev endpoint `/subpick?n=` selects the n-th entry of `/sublist`.
- Test copy of the owner's data for runs: copy `shared_prefs`, `files`, `code_cache` only, rewrite the extension paths, set `loggedIn` false and the Firebase URL to a dead address (Ultima sync), run `runDev -PdataDir` with `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS=-Dcloudstream.ipcport=52999`.

**Round 2 (same night)**
- Default theme is Classic again (`Themes.DEFAULT_ID`): first start and "Reset to default" give the plain Windows colours; Midnight etc. are a choice. Saved choices are kept.
- AniList / MAL / Simkl client IDs are in `local.properties` (git-ignored) and baked in (`BuildConfig`); Simkl has no secret (PIN sign-in). Redirect URLs to register: `http://localhost:52526/anilistlogin`, `/mallogin`, `/simkl`.
- **Clone site never worked on desktop**: upstream loads the clones in `MainActivity.onCreate` (`onAllPluginsLoaded`), which the native UI never runs. `DesktopBootstrap.loadClonedSites` now runs after every plugin load and when a clone is added. Engine dialogs: `WhiteButton` style (`android-res/values/styles.xml`) is filled with `colorPrimary`/`colorOnPrimary` (the accent) and `NativeApp` re-populates the context themes whenever the colours change.
- **Zinkmovies is broken at the source, not in the app**: its domain list (`phisher98/TVVVV/domains.json`) gives `new4.zinkmovies.foo`, which now hops to `new4.zinkmovies.cc` and then to a landing page (`redirect.zinkmovies.org`, `?re=zink` -> `new5.zinkmovies.monster`); the saved cf_clearance is for the dead domain, so the plugin asks for Cloudflare again for ever. The live site `new5.zinkmovies.monster` answers HTTP 200 with the saved cookie but has a new page layout (`movies-grid` links, no `<article>`): the plugin finds 0 results there too (verified through a clone). Needs a plugin update from its author.
- **4KHDHub**: all 16 sources of Inception and the first 7 of an episode were started one by one: 14 / 16 play in 4-24 s, the "HubCloud [Instant Download]" workers (one worker host per link) refuse or start slowly (10-25 s), "10Gbps [Download]" and "[Pixeldrain]" start in 4-6 s. New `net/HostHealth.kt`: among sources of the same quality the kinds that failed (extractor + bracket label, kept 6 h, also across runs, `desktop_source_health_v1`) go last, "instant download" starts with a prior of 1; a failed link keeps its place (`nextLink` wraps to untried ones). Subtitles of the sources are the video's own tracks and rendered fine (checked `sub-text`). Pixeldrain answers 403 for a while after many requests from one IP (my probing), not a bug.
- Dev: `/apisearch?name=&q=` (one provider's search with the error), `/httpprobe` `&find=<regex>` / `&max=`; `local.properties` and the scratch data copy are never committed.

## 42. Stremio add-ons and torrents (2026-10-08, night 3)

Owner: "add stremio and torrent support like ayu's app, super proper". `reference/ayu-client` was read for the design only (it is GPL-3; this is our own code). Packages `desktop/stremio` and `desktop/torrent`; settings page `StremioSettings.kt` (Settings > Stremio & torrents).

- **Protocol** (`StremioModels.kt`, `StremioClient.kt`): manifest (resources as strings or objects with types/idPrefixes, catalogs with `extra` or old `extraSupported/extraRequired`, `behaviorHints` configurable/p2p), resource URLs `/{resource}/{type}/{id}[/{extra}].json` (ids URL-encoded, extras `k=v&k2=v2`, the manifest's query string kept), meta with `videos`, streams (`url`, `ytId`, `infoHash`+`fileIdx`+`sources`, `externalUrl`, `proxyHeaders`), subtitles. TTL cache for catalogs/meta.
- **Add-ons as providers** (`StremioAddons.kt`, `StremioApi.kt`): every add-on with catalogs/meta becomes a `MainAPI` in `APIHolder.allProviders` (same trick as clone sites, re-synced after every plugin load through `afterPluginsLoadedEvent`): Home rows from catalogs without a required extra, paging with `skip`, search, `load` (movie/series/anime/live by type and id prefix `kitsu:`/`mal:`/`anilist:`...), episodes carry `Target(type,id)` JSON, `loadLinks` asks all stream/subtitle add-ons. Prefs key `desktop_stremio_addons_v1`; Cinemeta is the default, Torrentio / OpenSubtitles v3 / Anime Kitsu are one-click suggestions.
- **No enrichment (changed 2026-10-09):** the first version added Stremio streams and subtitles to titles of any extension (`StremioStreams.collectForOther` in `RepoLinkGenerator`, IMDb id lookup, a Settings switch). The owner wanted the two worlds apart, so it was removed: an extension title gets only its own extension's sources, a Stremio title only the add-ons' (all stream add-ons that offer the id). Related / "More like this" cards search the extension of the page (`Navigator.search(name, only = apiName)`), right-click offers all extensions. MAL: the token request carries an optional client secret (App Type web) and a refused sign-in shows MAL's own message.
- **Torrent engine** (`TorrentEngine.kt`, `TorrentConsent.kt`): TorrServer (YouROK, GPL-3, MatriX.145.2, `TorrServer-windows-amd64.exe` 63.6 MB) downloaded from GitHub only after the owner agrees, run as a child process on `127.0.0.1` (`-p port -i 127.0.0.1 -d data -l log --dontkill`, no console window), API: `POST /torrents {add|get|drop|list}`, `POST /settings`, `GET /stream/<name>?link=<hash>&index=<1-based>&play|&preload`, `/echo`, `/shutdown`. File choice (`pickFile`): the add-on's `fileIdx` (+1), else the episode in a pack (E1 does not match E10), else the file name, else the biggest video. Settings applied: disk cache on, `RemoveCacheOnDrop`, cache size, no Bonjour/DLNA, 80 connections, optional forced encryption. Stops 5 min after the last torrent, in a shutdown hook, an orphan from a crash is shut down at the next start.
- **Player**: `PlayerSession.loadLink` resolves a magnet / .torrent link (consent first, `torrentBusy` stops the stall watchdog while a question is open) to the engine's HTTP stream, which mpv plays; loading overlay shows the phase, peers, speed and buffer %; `TorrentPill` while playing; leaving the player drops the torrent and clears its cache; VLC / browser hand-off gets the engine's URL (`resolvedTorrent`). Source sort (`PlayerGeneratorViewModel`): direct sources first; torrents with fewer than 3 reported seeders last; then quality; then the most seeders (`seeders` is read from the add-on's title, kept in `extractorData`). Source list shows a "Torrent" tag. `LOADTYPE_INAPP` includes TORRENT/MAGNET.
- **Tests**: `StremioProtocolTest` (7 tests: addresses, manifests, streams, magnets, meta, file choice) pass. Live: Cinemeta Home/search/details, Anime Kitsu episodes, Torrentio 75 sources for Inception, OpenSubtitles 90 subtitles, 68 Torrentio sources added to a StreamPlay title, consent -> download (63.6 MB, under 10 s) -> start -> BBB web-seeded torrent plays at 7 MB/s with seeking, drop + cache clear on leaving, engine gone after quit.
- **Limits to tell the owner**: a swarm needs peers: on this PC / ISP a torrent that the add-on lists with 28 seeders had 4 peers after 60 s and was skipped (next source tried, as designed); the first start of the engine needs time for DHT. Windows may show a firewall prompt for TorrServer. `externalUrl` streams are only logged. Not affiliated with Stremio; Torrentio / Kitsu are third-party services that can be down.
- Dev: `/stremio?add=|remove=|enable=&on=|streams=<type>|<id>|list=1`, `/open?api=&url=&name=` (Details by address), `/click?x=&y=` takes screenshot pixels.

## 43. IntroDB for Anime, MAL OAuth fix, Anime4K, Anti-Spoiler view, Skip Placement & Auto-skip, Stremio Badges, Seekbar Time Toggle (2026-10-09)

Eight specific features and fixes implemented across player, extensions, integrations, and UI:

1. **IntroDB for Anime & Movie skips** (`IntroDbSkip.kt`, `TheIntroDBSkip.kt`):
   - Added support for `TvType.Anime`, `TvType.OVA`, `TvType.AnimeMovie`, `TvType.Cartoon`, and `TvType.Movie` in `supportedTypes` across both `IntroDbSkip` and `TheIntroDBSkip`.
   - Added season fallback `season = episode.season ?: 1` (resolves anime episodes having null seasons).
   - Movie handling: passes `is_movie=true` without season/episode parameters when title type is Movie or AnimeMovie.
   - Fallback IMDb/TMDb resolution: when IDs are missing, resolves via TMDB metadata lookup (`Tmdb.info(title, year, isMovie, hints)`).
   - Added fallback from millisecond values (`start_ms`, `end_ms`) to second values (`start_sec`, `end_sec`) in `IntroDbSkip`, and guarded `TheIntroDBSkip` network parsing with `runCatching`.

2. **MyAnimeList (MAL) Authentication fix** (`MALApi.kt`):
   - Fixed OAuth PKCE authorization: added missing `code_challenge_method=plain` query parameter to authorization URL.
   - Enforced code verifier length restriction to 128 characters maximum (`take(128)`) to satisfy MAL API requirements.
   - Added matching `redirect_uri` in both the authorization request URL (`$encodedRedirectUri`) and the token exchange POST request body, avoiding code invalidation/burnout.

3. **Anime4K Real-time Shader filter** (`PlayerMenu.kt`, `MpvPlayer.kt`, `Anime4K.kt`):

## 44. MAL OAuth Fixes, Extensions Stremio Logo, Anime Source Separation, and Accurate AniSkip Timestamps (2026-10-09)

Four major stability and integration issues addressed:

1. **MyAnimeList (MAL) OAuth Login Flow Fixes** (`OAuthCallback.kt`, `DesktopBootstrap.kt`, `DesktopUiHost.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `AuthAPI.kt`, `AuthRepo.kt`, `MALApi.kt`):
   - **Persistent OAuth Payload & Multi-Process Survival**: In `AuthRepo.kt`, persisted `oauthPayload` to `DataStore` (using `CloudStreamApp.setKey` / `CloudStreamApp.getKey`) so that OAuth authorization codes returning via a fresh app instance or external protocol dispatch retain their `codeVerifier` and `requestId`.
   - **Re-login Grace Handling**: In `AuthRepo.setupLogin`, when an account with the same user ID already exists, updated the existing account's token and user object instead of throwing `ErrorLoadingException("Already logged into this account")`.
   - **MalUser Deserialization Resilience**: In `MALApi.kt`, marked `location`, `joinedAt`, and `picture` as nullable (`String? = null`) with explicit defaults in `MalUser`, plus JSONObject fallback parsing, preventing JSON deserialization failures when MAL's `/v2/users/@me` endpoint omits these fields.
   - **OAuth Request ID, Code Verifier & Redirect URI Recovery**: Saved `requestId`, `codeVerifier`, and `redirectUri` to persistent settings during `loginRequest()` and automatically recovered them if `payload` is null in `login()`. Matched token exchange `redirect_uri` to the incoming URL scheme (`http://localhost:$PORT/mallogin` vs `cloudstreamapp://mallogin`).
   - **Loopback Server & Intent Handling**:
     - `OAuthCallback.kt`: Added eager `start()` call in `DesktopBootstrap.kt` during engine initialization.
     - Relaxed host/origin validation to accept `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`, and `[::1]:52526` (fixed IPv6 host parsing where `substringBefore(':')` returned `"["`). Allowed `origin == "null"`.
     - In HTML callback page, fixed loopback callback bug: removed erroneous `window.location.href = appLink` from `.then(r.ok)` where `history.replaceState` stripped parameters and sent an empty code to the app triggering "Failed to authenticate MAL". Preserved intact query parameters in fallback `.catch()`.
     - Dispatched loopback link format `http://localhost:$PORT/$id$cleanSearch` to preserve redirect URI scheme for token exchange.
     - `DesktopUiHost.kt`: Intercepted loopback HTTP URLs (`localhost:52526`, `127.0.0.1:52526`, `[::1]:52526`) and forwarded them directly to `MainActivity.handleAppIntentUrl` instead of looping back to the external browser.
     - `MainActivity.handleAppIntentUrl`: Expanded URL condition to accept loopback addresses and URLs matching any API redirect pattern in `AccountManager.allApis`.
     - `AuthAPI.isValidRedirectUrl`: Fixed matching logic to handle both `/$id` and `://$id`, and required `code=`, `token=`, or `error=` parameter presence so bare redirect URLs do not trigger failed login attempts.

2. **Stremio Logo in Extensions Sidebar & Repository Lists** (`ExtensionsScreen.kt`):
   - Added `customIcon: (@Composable () -> Unit)? = null` parameter to `RepoRow`.
   - In `ExtensionsScreen.kt`, passed `StremioLogo(size = 24.dp)` to the `STREMIO` repository sidebar item, displaying the branded purple Stremio logo alongside the add-on count badge.
   - In repository item rows (`repoList`), rendered `StremioLogo(size = 24.dp)` for any Stremio repository row where `repo.name` or `repo.url` contains "stremio" and `iconUrl` is null.

3. **Exclusion of Stremio Add-on Sources for Anime Extension Titles** (`RepoLinkGenerator.kt`, `StremioStreams.kt`):
   - In `RepoLinkGenerator.kt`, introduced comprehensive anime detection: checking `page.type` against `TvType.Anime`, `TvType.OVA`, and `TvType.AnimeMovie`, checking if `page is AnimeLoadResponse`, checking `page.syncData` keys (`mal`, `anilist`, `kitsu`), checking anime tags, and inspecting provider name and supportedTypes.
   - Passed `page = currentResponse` to `RepoLinkGenerator` in `ResultViewModel2.kt` (`loadLinks`).
   - If the media is anime, completely bypassed `StremioStreams.collectForOther` parallel link fetching, preventing unrelated torrent/add-on streams from cluttering the anime sources list.
   - In `StremioStreams.collectForOther`, added comprehensive early return guard checking `AnimeLoadResponse`, anime types, syncData, tags, and anime provider names.

4. **Accurate Anime Intro Skip Timestamps & Live-action Disambiguation** (`AniSkip.kt`, `IntroDbSkip.kt`, `TheIntroDBSkip.kt`, `VideoSkipIntegrationTest.kt`):
   - **AniSkip AniList GraphQL Multi-Season & Offset Resolution**:
     - Expanded `AniSkip.supportedTypes` to include `TvType.TvSeries` and `TvType.Movie` so anime titles loaded under TV series types are not skipped by `SkipAPI.videoStamps`.
     - In `AniSkip.stamps`, added comprehensive anime check (`AnimeLoadResponse`, anime types, `syncData` keys `mal`/`anilist`/`kitsu`, anime tags, and anime provider names). Non-anime titles return null without overhead.
     - Rewrote `resolveFromAniList`: when `season > 1`, performs targeted AniList query with search `"$cleanTitle Season $season"` and recursively traverses multi-level nested `SEQUEL` relations to resolve exact MAL IDs and episode counts for Season 2, Season 3, Season 4, etc.
     - Added robust absolute and relative episode offset resolution:
       - Maps absolute episode numbers (e.g. Ep 38 in Season 3) to season-relative numbers against target season MAL ID.
       - Maps show-wide absolute episode numbers (when season is not provided) to the correct season's MAL ID and relative episode number.
       - Handles single-entry continuous MAL shows (e.g. Naruto Shippuden / Black Clover) when season is split in extensions.
     - Falls back to query AniSkip without episode duration constraint when duration-constrained query returns no stamps.
     - Stopped mutating `data.syncData["mal"]` to prevent corrupting subsequent queries across seasons.
     - Added `cleanTitle` helper to strip dub/sub labels and bracketed release tags.
   - **TheIntroDB & IntroDB Anime Guard**:
     - In `IntroDbSkip.kt` and `TheIntroDBSkip.kt`, expanded `isAnime` check to include `AnimeLoadResponse`, `syncData` (`mal`, `anilist`, `kitsu`), tags, and anime provider names.
     - For anime titles, TMDB lookup results that do not belong to the "Animation" genre (e.g. live-action One Piece or live-action Cowboy Bebop) are rejected, preventing incorrect live-action TV show timestamps from being applied to anime episodes.
   - **Integration Tests**:
     - Verified supported types in `VideoSkipIntegrationTest.kt` (including `TvSeries` and `Movie` for AniSkip), tested `cleanTitle` normalization, tested `extractSeasonNumber`, and confirmed MAL OAuth payload serialization and recovery.



## 45. Live Stream Teardown Deadlock Fix & Player Teardown Hardening (2026-10-09)

Resolved a compound triple-deadlock and freeze occurring when navigating back from live stream playback (e.g. JIO TV live channels such as "Sun Gemini HD" with `Appearance.nativePlayer = true`):

1. **AWT Toolkit & Win32 Custom Chrome Deadlock Elimination** (`WinChrome.kt`):
   - **Root Cause**: When navigating back from `PlayerScreen`, Compose disposes the secondary transparent `DialogWindow` (`NativeOverlayWindow`), invoking `Window.dispose()` -> `doDispose()` -> `hide()` -> `WComponentPeer.hide()`. In OpenJDK Windows AWT (`awt_Component.cpp`), `WComponentPeer.hide()` enters `AwtToolkit::SyncCall` holding `Component.getTreeLock()` on the EDT. Hiding the owned dialog causes Windows Win32 to send `WM_NCACTIVATE` / `WM_ACTIVATE` to the main application window (`DesktopUiHost.window`). Because `WinChrome` subclassed the main window procedure and did not handle `WM_NCACTIVATE`, the message was forwarded to AWT's native `AwtWindow::WndProc`. In `awt_Window.cpp`, `AwtWindow::WmNcActivate` attempts to acquire `AwtToolkit::GetTreeLock()`, which deadlocked `AWT-Windows` against the EDT.
   - **Fix**: Added `WM_NCACTIVATE (0x0086)` handling in `WinChrome.handle` returning `api.DefWindowProcW(hwnd, msg, wParam, -1L)`. Per Microsoft custom frame specs (DWM / `WM_NCCALCSIZE`), passing `lParam = -1` updates the window activation state without repainting the non-client title bar, completely bypassing `AwtWindow::WmNcActivate` and eliminating the TreeLock contention on the toolkit thread.
   - Added `DefWindowProcW` binding to `User32Ex`.

2. **Native Video Canvas Isolation from WinChrome Subclassing & VO Detach** (`WinChrome.kt`, `NativeVideo.kt`):
   - **Root Cause**: `WinChrome.refreshChildren` periodically enumerated child windows with class name `"SunAwtCanvas"` and subclassed them with JNA (`handleChild`). When `NativeVideoHost` mounted an AWT `Canvas` for mpv Direct3D 11 rendering (`wid`), `WinChrome` subclassed it as well. mpv's D3D11 swapchain hooked the HWND, causing DXGI and JNA hook collisions and routing teardown messages through JNA callbacks on `AWT-Windows`. Furthermore, clearing `canvasHwnd = 0L` on disposal exposed the HWND to re-subclassing during teardown.
   - **Fix**:
     - Added `NativeVideo.isNativeCanvas(hwnd)` with persistent `nativeCanvasHwnds` concurrent set, retaining all HWNDs ever associated with `NativeVideo` so `WinChrome.refreshChildren` permanently ignores them even during and after disposal.
     - In `NativeVideoHost.onDispose`, proactively invoked `MpvPlayer.active?.detachNativeWindow()` to reset `wid = "0"` before AWT Canvas peer destruction, preventing mpv's VO thread from rendering into an invalid HWND.

3. **MpvPlayer Teardown Pipeline Hardening** (`MpvPlayer.kt`, `Mpv.kt`):
   - **Root Cause**: Previously, `MpvPlayer.release()` detached references and spawned `MpvPlayer-Destroy` to execute `mpv_terminate_destroy(ctx)` without stopping active playback or clearing `wid`. For live DASH streams, mpv's demuxer thread was actively blocked on synchronous network socket reads, and the VO thread was attached to the canvas HWND. Calling `mpv_terminate_destroy` caused mpv to synchronously join its internal threads, hanging the destroy thread for 15+ seconds.
   - **Fix**:
     - When `release()` runs: immediately resets `wid` to `"0"` (`mpv_set_property_string(ctx, "wid", "0")`) to unbind the D3D11 swapchain from the canvas HWND.
     - Sends non-blocking async stop command (`mpv_command_async(ctx, 0L, arrayOf("stop", null))`) to halt demuxer and decoder pipelines.
     - Added `mpv_wakeup(ctx)` to `Mpv.kt` JNA interface and invoked it during `release()` to wake `eventThread` instantly without waiting on `mpv_wait_event` timeouts.
     - Exposed `detachNativeWindow()` on `MpvPlayer` for proactive detachment on canvas teardown.

4. **Multi-Proxy In-Flight Request Cancellation & Active Call Lifecycle** (`DashProxy.kt`, `HlsProxy.kt`, `RangeProxy.kt`):
   - **Root Cause**:
     - In `DashProxy.kt`, `upstream()` was previously removing `Call` from `entry.activeCalls` in its `finally` block before response body streaming began. In-flight body streaming was thus uncancelled when `DashProxy.cancel()` was called.
     - In `RangeProxy.kt` and `HlsProxy.kt`, cancellation mechanisms did not exist. Navigating away from HLS or MP4 streams left active background thread pools, futures, and socket reads running.
     - In `RangeProxy.kt`, `activeCalls` tracking had race conditions and call leaks across probe and chunk downloads.
   - **Fix**:
     - Refactored `DashProxy.upstream` to an inline closure `block(r)` that holds `Call` in `activeCalls` during the entire response body streaming process. Added checks for `entry.cancelled` inside the byte copy loop to abort immediately on cancel.
     - Implemented `RangeProxy.cancel(address)` and `RangeProxy.cancelAll()`. Designed `UpstreamResult` (implementing `Closeable`) to tie `Call` lifecycle in `activeCalls` to stream consumption, closing the response and removing the call safely on finish.
     - Implemented `HlsProxy.cancel(address)` and `HlsProxy.cancelAll()`. Added in-flight OkHttp call tracking (`activeCalls`), cancelled prefetch futures in `firstSegments`, cancelled pending playlist futures in `cache`, and cleared header/variant tables.
     - Updated `MpvPlayer.release()` to invoke `cancel()` and `cancelAll()` across all three proxies (`DashProxy`, `HlsProxy`, `RangeProxy`).

5. **Player Focus Guarding** (`PlayerScreen.kt`, `PlayerSession.kt`):
   - Exposed `val isReleased: Boolean get() = released` on `PlayerSession`.
   - In `PlayerScreen.kt`, guarded `LaunchedEffect(dialogsOpen)` so focus requests (`DesktopUiHost.window?.requestFocus()`, `focus.requestFocus()`) do not trigger after the player session has been released or during exit transitions.

6. **Automated Verification**:
   - Added `DashProxyTest.kt` unit tests:
     - `testDashProxyWrapAndCancel`, `testDashProxyCancelAll`: verified URL wrapping and safe cancellation.
     - `testDashProxyInFlightCancellation`: verified in-flight socket read termination within 2 seconds using a slow mock chunk stream.
     - `testRangeProxyAndHlsProxyCancel`: verified multi-proxy cancellation handling.
     - `testRangeProxyInFlightCancellation`: verified in-flight RangeProxy streaming cancellation within 2 seconds.
   - Added `testMpvWakeupAndStop` to `MpvTest.kt` verifying `mpv_wakeup`, `mpv_command_async("stop")`, and `wid` unbinding.
   - Full test suite passed across all projects (`:app:test`, `:android:test`) with 100% pass rate.

## 43. Player redesign (2026-10-09)

Owner: "redesign the player super cool, get inspired from Ayu's app (smoothness and UI), no need to build exactly". Ayu's player is HTML/CSS in WebView2 over libmpv (`reference/ayu-client/.../player-ui`, seen by assembling player.html + player.css with a stub); only the look and motion were taken, all code is ours.

- `ui/screens/player/PlayerChromeModern.kt` (new): `ModernChrome` (top: glass back button, title, `TopPills` source+quality / subtitles / audio; bottom: `ModernSeekBar`, two `Capsule`s, `TimeText` with end time when >= 1100 dp wide, compact below 760 dp), `GlassButton`, `SeekGlyph` (canvas, no font), `PlayButton` (white), `SkipPill`, `PauseInfo`, `ResumePulse`, `glide()` (cubic-bezier 0.16,1,0.3,1; snap when Appearance.motion is Off). The Classic player style keeps the old `PlayerChrome`.
- Rules kept: controls hide 2 s after the last move, accent = bars, white = knobs and the main button, one paged menu (`PlayerMenu`, now opens from the pill (top) or the gear (bottom) with a grow-in). Animations read their state inside layer / draw blocks (`graphicsLayer`, `drawBehind`) so the position tick never recomposes anything; measured 59.9 fps, 0 late frames, 0 drops with the new controls up.
- `PlayerScreen`: `SkipLayer` (skip + next-episode pills, countdown), episodes panel is a floating opaque card that slides in, loading overlay with the episode picture. `PlayerSession.episodeDescription / episodePoster / debugStamp`.
- Dev: `/player?stamp=Recap&from=0&to=60000` puts a segment on the video; `/skipcheck?title=..&mal=..&ep=..&dur=..` asks every skip provider (AniSkip, TheIntroDB, IntroDb, AnimeSkip) what it has. Skip recap verified: JJK S1E3 gives Recap 0.5-41.6 s from all three.
- Known: `SkipAPI.videoStamps` returns the FIRST provider that has anything (order AniSkip, TheIntroDB, IntroDb, AnimeSkip), it does not merge them; so a recap that only TheIntroDB knows is missed when AniSkip has an opening for the episode.

### 43b. Same day, after the owner saw it ("controls scrambled, not in order", "app hung after leaving a video", "Chinese in the tray menu")

- Layout rebuilt in a fixed order (see CHANGELOG): the top pills are gone; Sources / Subtitles / Audio & video are named buttons in the right capsule (labels from 1040 dp, a mark on Subtitles when one is on); `MenuPage.Tracks` = audio + video qualities; Keyboard shortcuts row in the settings root; the bottom controls are not drawn while `loadingText` / `failure` is up; `SourcesPanel` (Show sources) on the loading and failure screens.
- **Hang after leaving a video (native player on, e.g. Re:ANIME / JIO TV):** hang report 07:41 showed the UI thread in `Window.dispose -> WComponentPeer.hide` while the AWT-Windows thread sat in WinChrome's `CallWindowProcW` and `MpvPlayer-Destroy` in `mpv_terminate_destroy`: the overlay / canvas windows were destroyed while mpv's own child window (another thread) still existed, and `release()` and `detachNativeWindow()` called `mpv_set_property_string(wid)` synchronously on the UI thread. Now: `Navigator.back()` asks `PlayerExit.hook` -> `PlayerSession.closeThen` first (native pages only): the core is released, `wid=0` and `mpv_terminate_destroy` run on the destroy thread, the page shows a dark "closing" screen, and the route pops when `MpvPlayer.destroyed` completes (20 s cap). Measured: core gone in ~120 ms, page gone ~0.5 s later, UI answers throughout. NOT reproduced with a stalling server (my stall test still exited in 0.2 s), so the real Re:ANIME case is unverified.
- **Tray menu in Chinese:** `AppendMenuW` / `RegisterWindowMessageW` were declared with `String` and loaded without `W32APIOptions.UNICODE_OPTIONS`, so JNA sent ANSI bytes to a wide API ("Open" -> U+704F U+6E65); now `WString` (checked by reading the menu text back with GetMenuStringW). TaskbarCreated had the same bug (the icon was not re-added after an Explorer restart).
- Dev: `/overlayshot` (the native controls window as Skia drew it), `/player?hold=1` keeps the controls up.

### 43c. Third round (taskbar, full screen exit, menus)

- **Root cause of "app does not come to front" and the full screen exit freeze:** `NativeOverlayWindow` is a Compose `DialogWindow`, which is DOCUMENT_MODAL; AWT disables the owner (`mainEnabled=false`, `WS_DISABLED` in the style) while it is up. A disabled window ignores `SC_RESTORE` (taskbar click on a minimised app; measured: stays iconic in native mode, restores in standard mode; `OpenIcon` / `ShowWindow(SW_RESTORE)` did restore), does not take keys, and disposing a modal dialog is the `WWindowPeer.hide` / `modalEnable` step of the 07:41 hang dump. Fix: the dialog is set MODELESS when it is created (hide, `modalityType = MODELESS`, show). Verified in dev: `mainEnabled=true`, `overlayModality=MODELESS`, SC_RESTORE restores, full screen -> Back leaves in ~0.7 s. Dev: `/winstate?act=minimize|restore|front|back|open|showna`.
- Menus (`PlayerMenu`) open above the button that was clicked: the buttons report `boundsInRoot()`, `PlayerContent.menuAnchor` places the card (clamped to the window), `originX` sets the grow-in origin. Root order: Video & Audio, Subtitles, Sources, then speed, picture size, audio output, Anime4K, skip, then shortcuts / VLC / browser.
- Episode list: `PlayerSession.episodes()` ran on the UI thread (a `getViewPos` DataStore read per episode); now `produceState` on IO, and the panel is a `graphicsLayer` slide (no per-frame re-layout of the list).
- Spoilers: `EpisodeCard` menu entry Hide / Show spoiler (state `uncovered`), player episode list hide button.
- Play button stays between the two 10 s jumps (the owner asked for left, then for the middle again).

### 43d. Fourth round

- Video & Audio moved to a one-button capsule at the top right (`Capsule(44.dp)`); the menu hangs below a button in the upper half of the window (`fromTop`, `belowTop`).
- **Opening glitch ("small screen at the top left, then it grows"):** measured with dev `/winrects` every 50 ms after Play: the native controls window first existed at `0,0 600x450` (the dialog's default place) and only then jumped to the canvas rect. `NativeOverlayWindow` now starts from the canvas bounds when known, is `visible = placed`, and shows 80 ms after it has its bounds. After: first sample already `154,139 1611x894`.
- Test hygiene: the owner's portable app was running while I tested. A second instance forwards its arguments to a running one (single-instance IPC), so test copies must use `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS=-Dcloudstream.ipcport=52999`; `portableDist` must not run while `dist/CloudStream-Portable/CloudStream.exe` runs (use `createDistributable`).

### 43e. Fifth round

- **Title bar on the video page:** `WinChrome.autoHides` (maximised window: the bar slides over the app when the pointer touches the top edge) can not work over the native player: the mpv child window and the controls window are above the app's layer. `WinChrome.keepBar` (set by `PlayerContent`) makes `windowedBar` true and `autoHides` false, so the bar is a row of its own above the video also when maximised; full screen and picture in picture are unchanged. Verified in dev: maximised window, native player, canvas starts below the bar, `/hit` reports CLOSE / MINBUTTON at its right end.
- The accent dot on the Subtitles button (`ToolButton(on = ...)`) is removed.

## 46. Updates, synced library, rank rows, PiP, controls (2026-10-09, evening)

Owner's list: repositories / extensions lost after an update, AniList and other synced libraries only open when StreamPlay is installed, every StreamPlay / CineStream row has rank numbers, separate anime section in the library, StreamPlay / CineStream video stutters ("hangy"), controls that do not hide and come back again and again, "pip is shit and not movable". All done; CHANGELOG "Unreleased" has the user-facing text.

- **Data kept across updates.** No code path drops repositories or extensions on an update (the owner's installed and portable data were intact). What was found: `PluginData.filePath` is absolute, so a copied / moved data folder (the portable zip unpacked into a new folder, `data` copied over) listed its extensions and never loaded them. `PluginManager.getPluginsOnline()` now rebases `.../files/Extensions/<repo>/<file>` onto the current data folder (`PluginData.rebased`), and `downloadMissingPluginFiles` (called first in `loadAllOnlinePlugins`) downloads files that are missing from the address saved with them (4 at a time, 2 tries). `platform/DataKeep.kt` mirrors `shared_prefs/*.xml` to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\CloudStream-keep` (after every PrefsBackup snapshot and at exit, only when the settings hold repositories / extensions) and `restoreIfFresh` puts it back in `DesktopBootstrap.initApplication` when the data folder has no settings and `version.txt` of the copy differs from `AppInfo.version` (same version = a deliberate reset stays). Runs with `-Dcloudstream.data` never read or write it; `-Dcloudstream.keep=<dir>` moves it (tests). Verified in dev on a copy: data folder moved (all 22 entries rewritten to the new folder), a deleted extension file downloaded again. Tests: `DataKeepTest`.
- **Synced library.** Cause shown in the running app: an AniList item opened "StreamPlay-Anime" because that provider's `mainUrl` makes `getApiFromUrlNull("https://anilist.co/...")` match; without it `ResultViewModel2.load` fails with "This provider does not exist". `LibraryScreen.openLibraryItem`: a provider that claims the name or the address opens it, otherwise `Navigator.search(title)` (what Android's `loadLibraryItem` does). `PosterGrid(onOpen = ...)` is new. Verified with StreamPlay removed from the test copy.
- **Library filter** All / Anime / Movies & TV (`LibraryScreen`, pref `desktop_library_kind`), shown when a list holds both kinds. Dev: `/bookmark?name=Heat&type=Movie` (`&delete=1`) adds / removes a Local library entry.
- **Rank rows.** `isRankedRow` matched "top", "popular", "trending", "hot" anywhere in a row name; now only `Top 5 / Top 10 / ...` (`chartSize`, 3 to 20) and the poster count follows the number.
- **Controls.** (1) `pointerWatch(ownsHover)`: a Move at the position of the last one that showed the controls is ignored (Compose repeats the last position as a Move when something under a resting pointer changes); with the native player only the controls window answers "is the pointer on the controls" (the app's window under the video has other coordinates, the answer flipped); (2) the standard player's root had `.let { if (hidden) pointerHoverIcon(blank) }`: adding / removing a pointer modifier at the moment of a press cancels the press (trace: Press+, then a synthetic Release, then 20 moves nobody saw), so the first click on a video with hidden controls only showed them and no drag could start; now the modifier is always there and only its icon changes. Measured: a resting real cursor shows the controls, they hide after 2 s and stay hidden (pokes constant over 10 s). Dev `/player` prints `controls= pokes= overControls= pip=`.
- **PiP** (`WinChrome`, `PipControls` in PlayerScreen.kt). The old strip was dragged by the frame window (HTCAPTION), which never sees the pointer over the native video's windows. Now Compose moves it: `pipDragBegin` at the press (cursor and window rect from Windows), `pipDragMove` from `detectDragGestures` (window = start + cursor delta, snapped to the work area's edges within 16 dp and kept on the screen the pointer is on), `pipMoved` stops the release from counting as a click; eight grips (`PipGrip`, edges 6 dp, corners 16 dp) call `pipResizeBegin/Move/End` (aspect-locked by `fitPip`, minimum 240 dp, at most 90 % of the work area); the system's invisible resize border is turned into HTCLIENT in PiP (a drag on it resized the window but the video canvas never followed). The picture fills the window (the 28 dp strip is gone); size and place are remembered (`pipClientWidth`, `pipRect`). Tested with a REAL mouse (`SetCursorPos` + `mouse_event`; the PowerShell process is DPI-unaware: pass physical / 1.5), native and standard player: drag, edge and corner resize, click (pause), double click (back to the app). Dev: `/pip?on=1`, `/pipdrag?x=&y=&dx=&dy=` (debug cursor), `/screenshot?sk=1` (Skia's own picture when PrintWindow returns grey), `/details?url=&api=&name=`. `tapGestures` read the `pip` of the first composition (stale); it now reads `WinChrome.pip`.
- **Stutter (not reproduced).** A 4K HEVC HLS source from StreamPlay played with 0 dropped frames and 19 % of one core (d3d11va) on this PC, so a decoding fault is not the story. What is: StreamPlay returns 200 sources and CineStream 100+, first by quality: 2160p REMUX, 30 to 70 GB files on throttled hosts (HubCloud, Pixeldrain). `net/SourceWeight.heavy` (quality > 1200, "REMUX", >= 25 GB in the name) sorts those after the rest in `PlayerGeneratorViewModel.sortLinks` (setting `Appearance.smoothSources`, default on). Also the UI-freeze log of the owner's install shows `getLocationOnScreen` / `findHeavyweightUnderCursor` stuck 1.5 to 2.5 s in native video sessions: `NativeOverlayWindow` polled `canvas.locationOnScreen` every 16 ms (a blocking call into the AWT toolkit thread, also the thread of WinChrome's window procedure and mpv's child window); it now follows ComponentListener events of the canvas and its ancestors (16 ms only until the window is placed, then a 250 ms safety net). Ask the owner which source stutters before going further (`/player` shows `source=`; HostHealth / RangeProxy log lines show a slow host).
- Test data: `scratchpad/data` copy of the installed data (paths rewritten with `Rewrite.java`, Firebase URL `http://127.0.0.1:1/`), `data2` = moved copy. Both hold the owner's tokens: delete them at the end (done).
