### 47c. Third round (2026-10-09, night): "dont copy exactly his, inspire from him, more smooth and fluent, much better ui"

- Own identity: the default `NavPosition.Floating` is now a capsule at the top (`ui/shell/FloatingBar.kt`: `FloatingTopBar`, `NavCapsule`, `CapsuleItem`); the Ayu-like vertical pill is `NavPosition.Dock` ("Floating dock"). Capsule: dark glass `0xD90B0B10`, 26 dp corners, a light pill (`0x2EFFFFFF`) that glides with a spring (damping 0.78, stiffness 420) between the measured x/width of the items (`onGloballyPositioned` + `positionInParent` into a state map); names are shown while `maxWidth / 2 >= rightNeed + 250 dp` (`rightNeed` = 360 dp on Home, 80 dp elsewhere), under `+150 dp` the capsule moves to the left (after the mark) so it never runs under the right-hand buttons. No bar background: `ShellState.topBarSolid` fades the page colour in under it.
- `ui/fluent/SmoothScroll.kt`: `Modifier.smoothWheel(state, step = 72.dp)` takes the wheel in the Initial pass (consumes vertical, non-Shift/Ctrl scroll events) and feeds `WheelGlide` (remaining px, per frame `remaining * (1 - exp(-dt / 0.09))` through `ScrollableState.scrollBy`, stops at the end of the list). Measured: one synthetic notch = about 100 dp. Applied to Home, Details, Search, Person, Extensions, Settings and `PosterGrid`.
- `UnderlineTabs`: one indicator (spring 0.8 / 460) over the measured tab positions instead of a line per tab; the tab row now takes the whole width (a `weight(1f, fill = false)` row beside a `weight(1f)` spacer clipped the last tabs).
- `FeaturedStrip` rewritten: open card 168x64 dp with the name and a progress line, closed slices 54 dp (66 on hover), spring widths; used by `HomeHero` on windows >= 900 dp, the segments stay for narrower ones. Parallax: `HomeHero` / `HeroBackdrop` / `Header` take a `scroll: () -> Float` (the first item's scroll offset, `Float.MAX_VALUE` once it has left) and read it only inside `graphicsLayer` blocks (no recomposition): the picture is translated by 0.3 x scroll under the bottom fade, the text by 0.18 x scroll with alpha going to 0 over half the height.
- `ui/fluent/Reveal.kt`: `Modifier.reveal(key, delayMs)` = rise 26 dp + fade over 560 ms (ease-out `0.16, 1, 0.3, 1`), once per key per run (a `HashSet`, decided with `remember(key)` so scrolling a row out and back, or returning to Home, shows it at once). Home's Continue watching, bookmarks and every row (delay 60 + 70 ms x row index up to 4).
- `PosterCard`: scale on a spring (damping 0.62, stiffness 520) 1.035 on hover, 0.975 pressed. Details: `UpNextCard` (330 dp glass card, the still of `playEpisode.poster` or the backdrop, CONTINUE when `getRealPosition() > 0` else UP NEXT, S/E and name, a progress line) at the header's bottom right on wide windows, series only.
- Verified on a scratch copy of the data (Firebase neutralised): capsule at 1280 and 820 px wide, Home banner + strip, scrolled Home and title page (parallax, fade), Details tab line. Not done: Search Explore block, continue-watching stills, player menus, Extensions/Settings cards.

### 47d. Fourth round (2026-10-09, late): "no button is clickable, not smooth, not optimized", continue watching (VegaMovies), extension search

- Clicks: `WinChrome.classify` answers HTCAPTION for every pixel of the top `DRAG_HEIGHT_DP` (36 dp) that is not inside a `noWindowDrag` rectangle. A maximized window used to hide its title bar (`autoHides = maximized || fullscreen`), so the app started at y = 0 and the new `NavCapsule` (no `noWindowDrag`) sat in the band: presses started a window drag. Now `autoHides = fullscreen && !pip && !keepBar` and `windowedBar = enabled && !fullscreen && !pip` (a maximized window has the title bar row too), and the capsule has `noWindowDrag("capsule")`. Verified with real mouse clicks (`realclick.ps1`: SetCursorPos + mouse_event, refuses unless WindowFromPoint is the app, uses ClientToScreen for the origin) on a copy of the portable build, normal and maximized (`/hit` shows CAPTION in the bar, CLIENT in the capsule).
- Idle cost: `HomeHero` ran `Animatable.animateTo(1f, tween(9000))` for the progress line, i.e. a frame request on every refresh for as long as Home was shown (Compose Desktop redraws the whole window per frame). Measured with `scratchpad/cpu.ps1`: 55 % of a core on Home idle, 16 % with the pointer on the banner (paused), 14 % on Library. Now a `mutableFloatStateOf` stepped every `HERO_TICK_MS` = 120 ms: about 6 %. `-Dcloudstream.framestats=1` + `/framestats` forces a frame per refresh, so it hides this kind of cost: measure CPU without it.
- `ui/fluent/PageFade.kt` `Modifier.fadeIntoPage()`: over a plain page (backdrop not Ambient, no wallpaper, Floating or Dock look = `c.bg`) the fade is a `drawRect` gradient of `c.bg` over the header; otherwise the old Offscreen layer + `DstIn` mask. Used by `HeroBackdrop` and the title page header. Spoiler covers: `SoftImage` (40x60 decode, stretched) instead of `.blur(26.dp)` per card.
- Continue watching: `Shelf` (LazyRow) keeps the item it shows when items are inserted before it, so a card put at index 0 stayed off screen to the left of a scrolled row (reproduced: played a VegaMovies film, the card was first in the list and the left chevron was lit). `Shelf` remembers the key of its first item and `animateScrollToItem(0)` when it changes. The click path itself (`openCard` -> `START_ACTION_LOAD_EP`) was checked with a Prime Video entry and resumed.
- Search: `ShellState.homeSearchOpen`, `ExtensionSearch` in `FloatingBar.kt` (button -> `GlobalSearchBox(only = provider, onBlur = close)`, `hadFocus` guards the first not-focused report), Ctrl+K opens it on Home in the Floating look (`NativeApp`), `SearchScreen` orders `results` with `selectedHomeProvider()` first (stable sort, only when `route.only == null`). The capsule's `rightNeed` no longer depends on the page (it moved between Home and the other pages).

### 47e. Fifth round (2026-10-10 early): duplicates, pill, Extensions in Settings, playback watchdogs, memory

- The pill of `NavCapsule` for Settings: `CapsuleItem` reported `positionInParent().x` from inside `Tooltip { body() }`, i.e. relative to the tooltip wrapper (0), so every item without a visible name put the pill at x = 0 (Home) - Settings always, the others only until they were selected (they then show their name and report right). Now an outer `Box` that is a direct child of the row reports the place.
- `footerItems` = Settings only; `Route.Extensions.tab()` = `Tab.Settings`; `SettingsScreen` inserts the Extensions `CategoryRow` before `Page.About`. `HomeActions.customise` is shown as a `Button` in the `item(key = "end")` of Home for the Floating and Dock looks (the other looks keep it in the Browse header).
- Duplicate extensions: `PluginManager.loadedMeta` (file path -> (lower case internal name, version)); `loadPluginNow` checks and registers under one lock after the manifest is read: a twin (same internal name, other file) with a version >= this one means this one is not loaded (still listed and saved), a lower one is unloaded first. `ProviderSelector` also `distinct()`s the names. The dev log of the user's data shows Desi's v38 loading first and CSX's v82 replacing it.
- `PlayerSession`: `markSeek` / `kickStuckSeek` (checked by the 2 s loop of `watchStalls`: `status == IsBuffering` 6 s and 12 s after a jump, `player.seekTo(target)` again, at most twice); first-source start limit 14 s when `nextLink() != null`, mid-play stall 20 s. `LoadingOverlay` / `FailureOverlay` use `Fluent.colors.bg` (the radial gradient `0xFF191D26 -> 0xFF08090B` was off the theme).
- Memory: while testing, the Kotlin daemon (2 GB), two Gradle daemons (0.6 + 0.5 GB) and the dev app left 0.7 GB of 14 GB free and the UI thread froze for up to 150 s in the jank log; stopping them gave 4.8 GB back. Build with `-Pkotlin.compiler.execution.strategy=in-process` (one JVM) and `./gradlew --stop` (+ kill the Kotlin daemon) when not building. In the app: Coil `SingletonImageLoader.setSafe` with a 160 MB `MemoryCache` in `Main.kt`, `lowOnMemory()` gate on `ScreenWarmup`.
- Title bar again: `Appearance.hideTitleBar` (default true, Settings > Appearance); `WinChrome.autoHides = enabled && (fullscreen || (maximized && hideTitleBar)) && !pip && !keepBar`, `windowedBar = enabled && !fullscreen && !pip && (!maximized || keepBar || !hideTitleBar)`. The reveal over the native video is drawn by the player's own overlay window (`RevealedTitleBar()` in `PlayerScreen`), so `keepBar` (never set) is not needed. Checked on a copy of the packaged app: maximized -> no bar, `/pointer?x=900&y=2` (dev pointer override; a real `SetCursorPos` is put back before a capture) -> bar over the app, `/hit` on the close button = CLOSE, away = CLIENT; real clicks on the capsule items in the top 36 dp navigate (they have `noWindowDrag`).
- Library: `opensDirectly` = `APIHolder.getApiFromNameNull(card.apiName) != null` only (the URL-claim fallback opened AniList items in StreamPlay-Anime); anything else `Navigator.search(card.name)`, which orders the Home extension first. Checked: an AniList item goes to `Search(query=...)`.

### 47f. Sixth round (2026-10-10): library filter, subtitle resume, white areas, motion

- `LibraryScreen`: `Kind` = All / Movies (`type == TvType.Movie` and not anime) / Shows (not anime, not Movie) / Anime; the `PillTabs` row is gone, a funnel `IconButton` + `MenuFlyout` (counts of the whole list, `saveKind`) sits beside Refresh.
- `MpvPlayer.watchTrackSwitch`: the pause-and-play nudge (did nothing) became `seek +0.4 s` at `NUDGE_MS` = 2 s and `+1.5 s` at 2.4 x that, `SWITCH_PATIENCE_MS` 9 s (re-open with `sid` at the open). The cause is the stream's refresh seek waiting for a range request; a new seek sends another one.
- White areas: from a user screenshot - the page drawn at the old size in the top left of a larger window, the rest white (a Compose scene that did not follow the window; the caption buttons were at the old right edge). Not reproduced (the user said it is too fast to find). `NativeApp.watchSceneSize` compares `LocalWindowInfo.containerSize` with `WinChrome.clientSize()` (GetClientRect) every 500 ms, after 2 polls apart by more than 3 px: `WinChrome.resendFrame()` (SetWindowPos FRAMECHANGED, no size) + `window.invalidate/validate/repaint`, the second time also a +1 px / back `setSize` (not when maximized, full screen or pip); logged as `NativeApp: the picture is WxH but the window is WxH`. `paintBackground` sets the AWT window and its children (not the video's `Canvas`) to the page colour. `PageHost.transitionSpec`: no transition into or out of `Route.Player` (SwingPanel / video window ignore alpha and slide).
- Motion: `FluentMotion.tweenInAfter(base, delay)`; page enter = fade 240 + rise 24 px after a 30 ms delay (no scale), back = fade 220, exit fade 90; `FlyoutSurface` fades in and grows 0.97 -> 1 from its top edge in 150 ms.
- Dev note: a dev window that is covered by another window (Claude, the browser) is not drawn: `/screenshot?sk=1` returns an 8 KB grey picture and `FrameStats` stays at "no frames". `scratchpad/top.ps1` puts it on top without activating for N seconds; `/placement?v=floating` undoes a saved maximized state.
- Regression and fix (same night): the first `watchSceneSize` used a 3 px tolerance. In the packaged app (WinChrome enabled, caption removed by `WM_NCCALCSIZE`) the Compose scene is always about 21 x 10 px smaller than `GetClientRect` (measured: client 1632x958 vs scene 1611x948 windowed, 1942x1150 vs 1923x1130 maximized), so it "healed" every second: `SWP_FRAMECHANGED`, `validate`, and a +1 px / back `setSize` from the second heal on. Dev (no WinChrome, native frame) did not show it, which is why it was missed; the owner reported subtitles not loading and endless loading after a seek. Now the tolerance is an eighth of the window (at least 160 x 120 px), it must last 4 polls (2 s), the actions escalate (layout, frame resend, nudge), full screen and pip are skipped, and `paintBackground` only sets the window and its content pane. Checked on a copy of the packaged app: window rect constant over 10 s, no heal logged, StreamPlay played (loading took ~55 s, one source answered 403 to range requests), an external English subtitle track was selected, seeking worked. Rule: anything that touches window chrome, size or painting is tested on the packaged copy, not in dev.
- Reload sources: `PlayerMenu` Sources page header action "Reload" -> `PlayerSession.reloadSources()` (`forceClearCache`, `releaseForReload`, `vm.loadLinks()`); `SourcesPanel` (loading / failure overlays) refresh `IconButton`; `DetailsScreen` `ActionRow` More menu gets `ACTION_RELOAD_EPISODE` for `playEpisode` (`ResultViewModel2` clears the cache and fetches the links again, with a toast). Compiled and unit tests run; the dev window could not be captured for a visual check (it stays undrawn when covered).

### 47g. Seventh round (2026-10-10): "borders are not smooth, breaks in borders" = the picture was stretched

- **Cause (measured on a copy of the packaged app at 150 %, javap of skiko-awt 0.150.1):** `LayerDrawScope` makes the Direct3D swap chain `(int) (dp * scale)` px; `SkiaLayer.doLayout` sizes the native canvas with `adjustSizeToContentScale` (one dp more when `dp * scale` has a fraction of about a half). The content pane was 1281 x 753 dp (AWT frame 1295 dp minus 7 dp insets): buffer 1921 x 1129, canvas window 1923 x 1131, so DXGI stretched the buffer by x1.001. Every 1 px line (all `Dp.Hairline` borders) landed on two pixels with a phase that changes across the window: `0E 14` / `0F 13` split pairs at the left and top of a card, a single crisp `1D` at its right edge. `/screenshot?sk=1` (`SkiaLayer.screenshot`) re-renders at 1923 and looks crisp, which hid it for rounds.
- **Fix:** `platform/EvenPixels.install(window)` (main window in `NativeWindow`, the player's controls `DialogWindow` in `NativeOverlayWindow`): an empty `TrimBorder` on the content pane takes the dp that do not make a whole number of px off the right and bottom (step = smallest k <= 8 with `k * scale` whole: 2 at 150 %, 4 at 125 / 175 %, 1 = nothing at 100 / 200 %). Listens to resize / move / show of the window and the pane, so a drag to another display or any window size is handled. Result: ComposeWindowPanel 1280 x 752 = SkiaLayer canvas 1280 x 752 = 1920 x 1128 px = client area; floating sizes 1001x701, 1100x640, 999x777, 1003x703 all even (986x694 ...). A 1 px strip of the page colour remains at the right and bottom (the canvas starts 1 px left and above the client area: AWT rounds the 7 dp inset 10.5 -> 10 px); it was hidden before by the canvas being 3 px too big.
- **Verified** on the real screen (`CopyFromScreen`/`BitBlt` with CAPTUREBLT of the client rectangle while the window was topmost for 0.7 s): card border pixels `1D1D1D` single on left, top, right and bottom; the player's error panel buttons (separate overlay window) `222222` single on all four sides. OpenGL (`-Dskiko.renderApi=OPENGL`) was crisp too but costs memory (see 920) so D3D stays.
- **Not possible:** `SetWindowPos` on the SunAwtCanvas from outside (AWT takes the px as dp and multiplies by 1.5: 2885 x 1697); changing Skiko's `+1 dp` rule (inside the library).
- Polish in the same round: the Home featured strip's progress line floats 8 dp above the bottom (it sat on the 1 px edge and ran into the rounded corners) and its title moved up; `Modifier.scrollEdgeFade` (PageFade.kt) fades the cut end of the Explore chip rows and of a gutterless `Shelf` (recent searches) into the page colour, only over a plain page; Extensions cards 196 -> 168 dp; the Downloads storage bar is empty at 0 bytes (it showed a dot). Dev endpoint `/skiainfo` (Java bounds tree + Compose scene px).
- Test recipe: scratchpad `screencap.ps1` (topmost no-activate, frontmost check by process id, BitBlt SRCCOPY|CAPTUREBLT), `px.ps1` (pixel rows), `wins.ps1` (window tree with sizes), `crop.ps1` (nearest-neighbour zoom). A test copy of `app/build/compose/binaries/main/app/CloudStream` with scratch data and the sync URL pointed at `127.0.0.1:9`; `./gradlew --stop` afterwards.

## 48. The updater again, banner of 8, local Explore, pause screen, title bar drag (2026-10-11)

Owner asked: "in app updater proper one; more posters than 3 in hero; video player pause logo and description and proper pausing effect; local / global content in explore; unable to drag from title bar when in player". All on `dev`, uncommitted.

### 48a. The updater (`desktop/update/UpdateCheck.kt`, cards in `ui/screens/settings/UpdateCards.kt`)

- **Why the old one was removed** (IMPROVEMENT_PLAN item 3): msiexec ran in a visible console, a failed upgrade wiped data (0.1.4), nothing was checked. The new one keeps the data out of reach (settings live in `%APPDATA%\CloudStream`, DataKeep has a second copy), verifies the file and never closes the app without a click.
- **Install kinds** (`UpdateCheck.installKind`): `Setup` (an `unins000.exe` next to `CloudStream.exe`: the Inno Setup program of `tools/installer/cloudstream.iss`), `Portable` (`portable.txt` or a `data` folder next to the exe), `Msi` (the default `%LOCALAPPDATA%\CloudStream`, or a folder a registered "CloudStream" Windows Installer product points into), `Other` (a development run, a copy of the program folder somewhere else: release page only, so a test copy can never start an MSI that upgrades the owner's real install).
- **Assets**: `*-setup.exe` for Setup, `*.msi` for Msi, `*portable*.zip` for Portable. Size and GitHub's `digest` (`sha256:`) are checked; only `https://github.com/<REPO>/releases/download/...` is downloaded (unless `-Dcloudstream.updateApi=` is set, the test hook). A finished, verified file in `%TEMP%\cloudstream-update` is reused.
- **Flow**: check (start + every 30 min tick, 6 h limit, never in the Player or the first-run setup) -> dialog (notes, Update now / Release notes / Later / Skip this version) -> progress dialog (Hide / Cancel; Settings > About shows progress too) -> `Ready` -> "Update ready" dialog (Restart and update / Later; during a video only a toast, the button is in Settings > About) -> `install()`.
- **install()** writes `cloudstream-update.cmd` into the temp folder and starts `cmd /d /c` with `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` (JNA `Kernel32.CreateProcess`, breakaway from a job first; a plain ProcessBuilder when that fails): it waits for this PID (`tasklist /FO CSV | find`), runs the installer (`start /wait`; Inno `/SILENT /SUPPRESSMSGBOXES /NORESTART /CLOSEAPPLICATIONS /NOCANCEL`, MSI `/passive /norestart /l*v`, portable: `tar -xf` into `extract`, remove `app` and `runtime`, `robocopy /E /XD data`), then starts the app again whatever happened. The app closes through WINDOW_CLOSING (settings flushed, DataKeep mirror) with a `halt` after 10 s. Log: `%TEMP%\cloudstream-update\update.log` (+ `setup.log`, `msi.log`).
- **Tested** on a copy of the packaged app (portable kind, own `data`): mock release feed (`jwebserver` + `releases` JSON), dialog, progress, SHA-256, restart: 14 s from click to the relaunched app; `app/UPDATED-MARKER.txt` appeared, `data/KEEP-ME.txt` and `portable.txt` were still there. **Not tested**: a real Inno setup or MSI run (the MSI would upgrade the owner's installed product: never run it here); only the script branches differ.
- **Releases must now carry** `CloudStream-<v>-windows-x64.msi`, `...-setup.exe` and `...-portable.zip`. Installs made before this version (1.0.2 and older) have the "go to GitHub" prompt: they must install this version once by hand.
- Dev hooks: `/update` (state; `?do=now`, `?do=restart`), `/updatecheck`.

### 48b. Home banner of 8

`HomeViewModel.load` loaded three preview titles (the Android app's number). It still posts those three at once, then `loadMoreBannerTitles()` loads the rest (`BANNER_TITLES = 8`, after 1.2 s, in parallel) and posts a longer list. `HomeHero` shows `min(8, as many slices as fit)`: `((width - 2 gutters - 300 dp - 168 dp) / 62 dp) + 1`, so the strip never runs under the Watch now button; narrow windows keep the slim segments (8).

### 48c. Explore: Global / Local

`ExploreScope` (`ExploreClient.kt`), `ExploreFilters.scope` / `language` (remembered in the default preferences `desktop_explore_scope`, `desktop_explore_language`). Local rows are TMDB discover calls with `with_origin_country=<Tmdb.region>`; a language (Hindi ... for IN, one per other country, `ExploreClient.languages`) replaces the country with `with_original_language` (English keeps the country). Rows: Trending (last 6 months of releases / 12 of first air dates, ranked when no genre or service is chosen), In cinemas (`/movie/now_playing?region=`, films only, hidden when a language is chosen), Most watched (`vote_count.desc`), Top rated, New. Anime has no scope (AniList is not national). Verified against the live TMDB with region IN: Malayalam / Hindi titles in the trending row.

### 48d. Pause screen (`PlayerChromeModern.kt: PauseInfo`)

After 1.1 s of a pause with the controls away: dim (0x52 black) + radial vignette + left shade; PAUSED with the pause mark; the logo (`LoadResponse.logoUrl`, else `Tmdb.info(...).logo`, looked up while the video plays and preloaded by a 1 dp invisible image), else the name; episode label; one line (year, age rating, up to 3 tags); the story (episode description, else the title's plot); "00:11 of 10:34 · 10:23 left"; a 3 dp progress line on the bottom edge. One 1.1 s progress value, each part starts later (0, .08, .2, .34, .5); leaving takes 0.22 s. Compose gotcha found: `produceState` makes its state once (`remember { mutableStateOf(initial) }`), only the producer restarts when keys change, so the producer must assign `value` itself. Dev hooks: `/player?logo=<url>&plot=<text>&pausenow=1` (artwork for a plain link).

### 48e. Title bar in the player

The revealed title bar of a maximized / full-screen video is composed in the controls window (`RevealedTitleBar()` in `NativeOverlayWindow`); that window is not the frame, so `WM_NCHITTEST` never made it a caption. `TitleBarRow` now has `moveWindowByPress()`: a press on the empty part calls `WinChrome.windowMoveBegin()`, which starts a 8 ms poll of `GetAsyncKeyState(VK_LBUTTON)` + `GetCursorPos` and moves the frame with `SetWindowPos` (a maximized window is restored under the pointer at the same fraction of its width first; the button let go at the top edge of the screen maximizes it again); two presses within 400 ms maximize / restore. `pollReveal` keeps the bar while a move runs.

### 48f. Blank window at the start (found while testing)

- A copy of the packaged app started while only 1.1 GB of memory was free showed a grey window for ever (`/framestats`: no frames, `/log`: `Uncaught exception in the window: IllegalStateException: Reading a state that was created after the snapshot was taken ...` at `NativeVideo.getOverlayActive` from `NativeApp.kt:179`). `NativeVideo` (an `object` with `mutableStateOf` fields) was first initialised by the `video-window-warm-up` thread (`Warmups.kt`, 2.5 s after the start) while the first composition, whose snapshot was older, was still running; its read of the new state failed. `Main.kt` already pre-touched `Startup.stage` and `Appearance.navPosition` on the EDT for the same reason: `NativeVideo.overlayActive` and `UpdateCheck.status` are touched there now. Rule: an `object` that holds Compose state and can be touched by a background thread must be touched in that `invokeAndWait` block.
- A dev window that is covered or created maximized behind other windows shows the same grey picture without any error (DWM does not present it): check `/log` for `Uncaught exception in the window` before blaming the window state.

## 49. Seek counter, antivirus report, shady installer, white area, weak PCs (2026-10-11, later)

Reports: "keyboard seeking 10 s instead of 5 and show increments"; a Windows Security screenshot (`Trojan:HTML/Redirector.DD!AMTB` in `C:\Users\4B\AppData\Roaming\CloudStream\cache\http_cache\80000cf8....1.tmp`); "terminal use when installing the app, people find it shady"; "sometimes at the launch, in a small window width, the rest of the screen turns white" (screenshot: the app drawn in the top left 1583 x 868 of a 1920 x 1080 window, the old top-tab look = an older build); "video is not even 15 fps, hangy".

### 49a. Seek
`PlayerSession.seekBy` keeps `seekBubbleMs` / `seekBubbleAt` (`SEEK_BUBBLE_MS` 1 s = how long the bubble stays up, so a run is what the viewer sees): same direction within 1 s adds, else starts again; text `+N s` / `+M min S s`. Keys: `PlayerContent.onKey` arrows 10 s (Shift 30 s), J / L 10 s, all through `seekKeyReady()` (140 ms between jumps for a held key). `Shortcuts.player` text changed.

### 49b. The Defender finding = a cached web page
OkHttp's `Cache` (`RequestsHelper.buildDefaultClient`, `%APPDATA%\CloudStream\cache\http_cache`, 50 MB) stores whatever the response headers allow; `<key>.1.tmp` is the body of an entry still being written (or abandoned: the owner's own folder had one holding a Firebase event stream). `net/NoPageCache` is a network interceptor (it runs before the cache stores the response) that adds `Cache-Control: no-store` for HTML, XHTML, JavaScript, SVG, Flash, VBScript and `text/event-stream`; the folder is emptied once (`desktop_http_cache_pages_purged`). `NoPageCacheTest`: local `HttpServer` + `Cache`, page 2 requests / data 1 request, nothing of the page in the folder, and the control run without the interceptor keeps the page. Not covered: Chromium's own cache in `data/webview2` (the Cloudflare challenge window) can hold pages too.

### 49c. The installer and PowerShell
The shipped 1.0.2 MSI had `CsSetPowerShell` / `CsKeepData` / `CsRestoreData` (type 114 custom actions: `powershell.exe -NoProfile -NonInteractive -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -WindowStyle Hidden -Command ...`, sequence 796 / 797 / 6650, condition `NOT Installed`): a console flashes and the flags are those of droppers. Removed from `tools/patch-msi.ps1`, which now also deletes them from an MSI that has them and fails the build when any custom action mentions powershell, cmd, wscript or cscript. Checked on a copy of the 1.0.2 MSI (tables read back through `WindowsInstaller.Installer`, not installed). Accepted loss: an upgrade from 0.1.3 / 0.1.4 with a `data` folder inside the install folder no longer has the data moved aside (the installers since 0.1.5 keep data in `%APPDATA%\CloudStream`; DataKeep has a second copy of the settings).

### 49d. White area at the start
Cause not reproduced (5 launches, saved maximized with a small normal size, default size and a small floating window, one set with 16 busy threads; scratchpad `launchcheck.ps1` = start, topmost no-activate, `CopyFromScreen`, share of white pixels). `NativeApp.watchSceneSize` was made quicker and stricter (150 ms polls for 20 s, tolerance `max(48 px, 4 %)`, 4 polls) and its third step toggles `WindowState.placement` Floating -> Maximized for a maximized window (the nudge of 1 px did nothing for it). Log line: `the picture is WxH but the window is WxH: laying it out again (#n, maximized=...)`. Related find of the same day: a grey window with `Uncaught exception in the window` = section 48f.

### 49e. Weak PCs: `player/PlaybackGovernor.kt`
Standard player = libmpv software renderer (`vo=libmpv`, `hwdec=auto-copy-safe`, frames rendered at the view's pixel size, `MpvSurfaceView`), about 2 ns per pixel + two copies; a 1080p window is 4 ms on the Ryzen here. Reports of < 15 fps are PCs where that or the decoding (software HEVC 10 bit) is the limit. Governor: every 5 s (`PlayerSession` loop, 20 ticks) reads `surface.stats.windowSerial / windowCpuMs / windowSeconds` (new numbers of `VideoFrameStats`) and mpv's `frame-drop-count`, `decoder-frame-drop-count`, `container-fps`, `paused-for-cache`; two windows with drops > 10 % of the film's frames or render time > 70 % of the frame interval raise the step: 1 `smoothAllowed=false` + `framedrop=decoder+vo`; 2 `qualityCapPixels=1.1 M` + `vd-lavc-fast=yes`; 3 `0.56 M` + `vd-lavc-skiploopfilter=nonref`. `level` is static (kept for the run). After step 3 and two more slow windows: one dialog offering the GPU player (`Appearance.nativePlayer`, applies from the next video). Native player: nothing to do (mpv's GPU renderer).
- **Not verified on weak hardware** (none here); verified that the steps apply (`/player?perf=N`, `/videostats`).

### 49f. The full libmpv is back (owner: "the hangy issue is due to the reduced libmpv and its GPU dependencies, restore the full libmpv")
- `app/native/windows-x64/mpv-2.dll` = the 120,812,544-byte build of commit f997257 (LFS oid `0a81c004...6f6a`, still in `.git/lfs/objects`, sha256 verified after the copy); the slim one (oid `05ee9b83...`) stays in the history of 5736c21, `tools/libmpv/build.sh` can still make it. Its load-time imports include `vulkan-1.dll` (checked with `objdump -p`; the slim one imported system DLLs only), so `vulkan-1.dll` (Vulkan Runtime 1.4.309.0 copied from this PC's System32, Khronos loader, Apache-2.0) and `vulkan-1.NOTICE.txt` are shipped beside it: JNA loads with the DLL's own folder first, so the app-local loader is used.
- Compared on this PC, same HLS 1080p60 stream, standard player at 1611x894 and the GPU player: slim: render cpu 3.9 ms, `d3d11va-copy` / `d3d11va`, 0 drops; full: cpu 4.3 ms, `d3d11va-copy` / `d3d11va`, 0 drops, `vo=gpu` `gpu-api=d3d11` in both. mpv with the full build also probes nvdec / vaapi / vulkan / d3d12 before settling on d3d11va-copy, and logs `auto_profiles` hook warnings (mpv's built-in Lua script, harmless). No difference is visible here: the weak-PC effect is not measured.
- **Gotcha**: `:app:createDistributable` reported UP-TO-DATE after `app/native` changed (the jpackage task does not list `prepareAppResources` as an input) and the distributable kept the old DLL; delete `app/build/compose/binaries/main/app` first. `installerMsi` and `portableDist` copy from that folder.

## 50. Owner list: volume wheel, upscaling modes, banner, themes, episode data (2026-10-11, evening)

Report (pasted): *scrolling the server list in the link loading screen changes the volume; more upscaling modes; the home hero loads 3 at once and the rest later, make it load at once; more themes, the current themes do not work; episodes: descriptions and spoiler posters do not change per episode, the last 5-7 episodes show the default series poster (checked in 4khdhub, screenshot)*.

### 50a. Volume wheel
`PlayerScreen.pointerWatch` (Initial pass, so it sees the wheel before any list consumes it) called `stepVolume` for every wheel event unless an episode panel, menu or dialog was open. The loading and failure screens (`LoadingOverlay` / `FailureOverlay`) have their own `SourcesPanel` list and were not excluded. Now `s.loadingText != null || s.failure != null || s.closing` blocks it.

### 50b. Upscaling: `Upscaler` (Appearance.kt) + `player/Upscaling.kt`
- `Appearance.upscaler` replaces the `anime4k` boolean (pref `desktop_look_upscaler`; an old `anime4k=true` loads as Anime4K A). `Upscaling.settings(mode)` = `scale`, `cscale`, `dscale`, `correct-downscaling`, `sigmoid-upscaling`, `glsl-shaders`; **Off writes mpv's own defaults** (bilinear, no sigmoid) so switching back at runtime restores them; at player start Off writes nothing.
- Chains (Anime4K v4.0.1 guide, "fast"): A = Clamp_Highlights, Restore_CNN_M, Upscale_CNN_x2_M, AutoDownscalePre_x2, _x4, Upscale_CNN_x2_S; B = Restore_CNN_Soft_M instead of Restore_CNN_M; C = Upscale_Denoise_CNN_x2_M; A HQ = Restore_CNN_VL, Upscale_CNN_x2_VL, ..., Upscale_CNN_x2_M. FSR = `FSR.glsl` + `CAS.glsl` (agyild's mpv ports: FSR runs when the output is larger than the video, CAS only at 1:1). Files in `app/src/main/resources/shaders` (+410 KB), unpacked once into `<data>/shaders`.
- Menu: `MenuPage.Upscaling` (root row "Upscaling"), `MpvPlayer.applyUpscaler()` sets the properties on the playing video. Dev: `/upscaler?mode=Fsr` (also prints the properties), `/look?upscaler=...`, then `/mpv?name=vo-passes` lists the passes (the list only refreshes when a frame is drawn: seek `time-pos`/`pause=no` after switching a paused or ended video). Checked on a 640x360 sample in the GPU player: every mode lists its user-shader passes (FSR: EASU + RCAS luma), the log has no shader error; `Quality` shows `scale=ewa_lanczossharp`.

### 50c. Home banner
`HomeViewModel.loadBanner`: `BANNER_TITLES` (8) loads started in parallel, posted once; the rows (`_page`) are posted first so they do not wait; after `BANNER_SOFT_MS` 5 s the banner goes with what is there if 3 titles are (hard stop 15 s); the first two slides' TMDB artwork is fetched with them (<= 1.5 s, cached 6 h, the banner asks again from the cache). The previous two-step version (3, then 5 more 1.2 s later) made the strip of slices jump. Log: `HomeVM: banner: 8 of 8 titles, posted once after 4529 ms` (4K HDHUB, cold).

### 50d. Themes
Cause: `FluentTheme` applied `oled()` whenever the backdrop was *Pure black*, and that is the default (cinema look), which replaces the theme's bg / layer / card / flyout; `AppShell.ContentArea` drew the glows only for non-edge looks and non-Black backdrops, the floating bar and dock being `edge`. So a theme was an accent colour. Fix: `oled()` only for Classic (`black = backdrop == Black && preset.id == "classic"`), glows always, retuned palettes (bg a visible hue, not 0A0A18), 14 presets, a light-mode wash. Dev: `/look?theme=<id>&glow=true&mode=Light`. Screenshots of Midnight, Matcha, Synthwave checked.

### 50e. Episode lists (4KHDHub = `FourKHDHub`, Phisher repo)
- Harness mode: `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS=-Dharness.q=Title+words ./gradlew :app:runHarness -Pargs="<dir> phisher episodes fourkhdhub"` prints every episode's season/number/name/poster/description/data. For *When Life Gives You Tangerines* E1-E16 have TMDB names, stills and synopses; **E17-E20 are the season packs** (`name` = file name + `[1080p, 48.46 GB]`, `poster`/`description` null). That is what the owner saw as "the last 5-7 episodes show the series poster"; the app's fallback (series art + number, covered by the spoiler pill) is for episodes without a still.
- `DetailsScreen`: `isSeasonPack` (name has `.zip/.rar/.7z` or "season/complete pack") splits them off into a "Season packs" group (`EpisodeCard(pack = true)`: folder, quality / size, `packParts` cleaned name, click = `ACTION_SHOW_OPTIONS`, no spoiler cover, count text "16 episodes · 4 season packs"). `withTmdbEpisodes` fills a missing still / synopsis / generic name / rating / length from `Tmdb.season(tvId, n)` (stills at `original`), only for a season that matches (names agree for >= 50 %, else numbers fit 80 % and not an anime). Unit test: `EpisodeDataTest` (5 tests).
- Seen on the way, **not changed**: `Appearance.hideSpoilerTitles` is saved and has a Settings switch but `EpisodeCard` never reads it (a covered episode is always "Episode N"); wiring it would show real titles on covered cards by default.

## 51. Setup program replaces an MSI copy (2026-10-11, night)

Owner: "many users are on the old MSI; build both MSI and exe, do not publish; what happens when the exe is installed over an MSI; make it best for update".

- **Before**: `tools/installer/cloudstream.iss` had its own AppId and `{autopf}\CloudStream` (= `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\CloudStream`), the MSI is in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\CloudStream`: two installs, two Apps entries, both on `%APPDATA%\CloudStream`; the desktop `CloudStream.lnk` is the same path (the Inno copy overwrote it and the MSI's removal later deleted it).
- **Now** (`[Code]` of the .iss): `FindMsiCopies` = `WindowsInstaller.Installer.RelatedProducts(upgrade code 8F7A1C2E-...)` (COM; per-user context 2 on this PC, `ProductInfo(code, 'InstallLocation')`); `DefaultDirName={code:DefaultAppDir}` = the MSI folder; `PrepareToInstall`: version guard (an MSI newer than the setup stops the setup, exit code 7), `CloseApp` (WMI `Win32_Process.ExecutablePath = <that folder>\CloudStream.exe`, so a portable copy or dev run of the same exe name is never touched; `taskkill /PID` repeated every 4 s for 20 s, then `/F /T`; interactive runs ask first), `KeepLegacyData` (`<dir>\data` -> `%APPDATA%\CloudStream`, or put aside beside it, never deleted), `msiexec /x {code} /qn /norestart` (1925 / 1730 -> again with `runas`), then a last Windows Installer query. A registration whose folder is gone does not block. `UpdateReadyMemo` tells the person on the last page. Test knobs: `/DAppIdValue /DAppNameValue /DMsiUpgradeCode /DAppExe /DShortcutName /DLegacyDataTarget`.
- **The old MSIs' removal is safe**: the 1.0.2 MSI's PowerShell actions are conditioned `NOT Installed` (they run when the NEW product installs, not at removal) and its folder wipe rows are the app and runtime folders only. Older MSIs (<= 0.1.4) wipe the whole install folder: that is what `KeepLegacyData` is for.
- **Gotchas**: a line of Pascal that starts with `#13#10` is read as a preprocessor directive (the compile fails with "Unknown preprocessor directive"); `GetTickCount` does not exist in Inno's Pascal Script (the log lines carry time stamps); a first `taskkill /PID` without `/F` reached no window of a still-starting Java app (hence the repeats); `Remove-Item` with a variable path containing `Program Files` trips the shell tool's path guard, use `[IO.Directory]::Delete`.
- **Test recipe** (`scratchpad/migtest`, not kept): a tiny Swing app made into an app image (jpackage) twice (old / new), the old one into an MSI with its own `--win-upgrade-uuid` and name, `msiexec /i ... INSTALLDIR=<scratch>` (per-user, no prompt), the setup compiled with the test knobs, run with `/VERYSILENT /TASKS="" /LOG=`. Never run the real setup or the real MSI against this PC while the owner's MSI 1.0.2 is registered: it IS the product the setup replaces.
- **Tools**: ISCC from Inno Setup 6.7.3 (official GitHub release asset of jrsoftware/issrc, SHA-256 checked) was installed per-user into a scratchpad folder and removed afterwards; WiX 3.x for the MSI was an older session's scratch copy on PATH for the build only.
- **Not done on purpose**: the in-app updater still picks the `.msi` for an MSI copy. Switching it to prefer the `-setup.exe` would move every MSI user to the Setup kind at their next update; do that once this migration has run on real PCs.

## 52. Local lists showed the United States (2026-10-11, night)

Report: "local content shows United States in build version". `Tmdb.region` was `Locale.getDefault().country` with a "US" fallback; `CommonActivity.setLocale` (App language, pref `app_locale`, set by the first-run setup and Settings > General) does `Locale.setDefault(Locale.forLanguageTag("en"))`, a locale with no country. The installed copy's prefs had `app_locale=en`, the portable data none (so a development run and the portable folder looked right). Now `Tmdb.systemRegion` (lazy): `HKCU\Control Panel\International\Geo\Name` (ISO2 of "Country or region"), else `user.country.format` / `user.country`, else the default locale's country, else US; `Appearance.tmdbRegion` still wins. `TmdbRegionTest` (3 tests). Repro: a scratch data dir whose `shared_prefs/com.lagradost.cloudstream3_preferences.xml` holds `<string name="app_locale">en</string>`, `runDev`, `/nav?route=explore` (shows "Local · India" now). The MSI and -setup.exe in dist/release (built earlier that night) predate this fix; the portable folder was rebuilt with it.

## 53. Owner list from the Discord #feature-request channel (2026-10-14)

Source: `docs/feature-requests/README.md` (11 posts + their images). Not done on purpose (owner): Nuvio home catalog, Nuvio plugin, mini player.

- **Notifications bug found**: "Get notified about new episodes" stored the subscription but nothing on the desktop ever checked it (the upstream `SubscriptionWorkManager` has no runner here). `notify/UpdateNotices.kt` now checks every subscribed title 90 s after the start and every 3 h (4 at a time, `api.load` + `getLatestEpisodes`, same rule as the worker), keeps the result in `data/notices.json`, raises a silent tray notification when the window is not in front (`Notifications.post`). The **Schedule** page (new nav item, `ui/screens/schedule`) has two tabs: Notifications (poster cards, unread dot, hover lift, mark all read, check now) and This week (AniList airing schedule for 7 days, `notify/AiringSchedule.kt`, "My list" / "Everything airing", titles of library, subscriptions, favourites marked). The nav icon carries a dot while there is something unread (`NavItem.badge`, `NavIcon`).
- **Bell button** next to the library button on the title page (same action as the menu entry).
- **Auto status**: `SyncViewModel.autoStarted()` (Watching from None / Planning / On hold / Dropped after 20 s of playback) and `autoFinished(ep)` (Completed when the last episode passes the sync percentage). Setting Settings > Player > "Update list status automatically" (`auto_status_sync_key`, default on). The episode count update was there already (`episode_sync_enabled_key`).
- **Continue watching**: 16:9 cards (`HomeScreen.ResumeCard`): the still of the episode (`stats/ResumeExtras.kt`, `data/resume_extras.json`, written by the player for the episode and the next one), title, "S1 · E3 · 40 min left", source name top right, bar. Old entries have no still: the poster is cropped from the top.
- **AniList recommendations**: `AnimeRelations.recommendations` (GraphQL `recommendations(sort: RATING_DESC)`), tab "Recommended" on anime title pages, one-click **Add to Planning** (`AnimeRelations.addToPlanning`, needs the AniList account).
- **Stats page** (`Route.Stats`, button on History): `stats/WatchLog.kt` is a diary in `data/watchlog.json` (ms per title per day, plus type, genres, cast, studios from TMDB, languages, rewatch counts). Tiles (all time / 7 d / 30 d / streak), heat map, by type, genres, extensions, most watched, studios, cast, languages, watched again. "All time" also counts the saved positions of before the diary (`DataStoreHelper.getAllViewPositions`). NSFW and live are not logged. Reset button.
- **Sources list**: sort chips Best / Quality / Size / Seeders (only the ones that mean something for the sources found), size and seeders in the detail line, "Last used first" (`net/LastSource.kt`: remembered per title by provider + name without sizes / episode tokens, also the first choice of the automatic pick via `sortLinks`).
- **Extension icons** in the Home extension list (`net/ExtensionIcons.kt`: api -> plugin file -> repo plugin url -> iconUrl, cached in `data/extension_icons.json`); `ComboBox(leadingItem = ...)`.
- **Subtitles**: Kitsunekko provider (`syncproviders/providers/Kitsunekko.kt`, Japanese, folder match + episode number); the search dialog has Full dialogue / Signs & songs / forced filter and tick boxes: "Add N" shows the first and puts the second one beside it as a **second subtitle** (mpv `secondary-sid`, `MpvPlayer.setSecondarySubtitle`, Subtitles > Second subtitle page). OCR translation of on-screen text is NOT done.
- **Seek preview**: `player/SeekThumbs.kt` second headless libmpv (`vo=image`, scale 320, keyframe seeks, coarse to fine, ~1 frame / 0.3 s, 20-72 frames, in memory only) started 8 s into a video of 90 s or more; shown above the time bubble of the modern seek bar. Setting `Appearance.seekPreviews` (Settings > Appearance > Player). Measured with a 10 min mp4: first frame after 4 s, 72 frames in ~70 s. Dev: `/seekthumb?f=0.5` returns the frame as PNG; the overlay window of the native player cannot be hovered by `/move`.
- **Animation**: play/pause morph (`fluent/Morph.kt`), glyph pop on the round art buttons, flyouts grow from the top edge (scaleY 0.78 -> 1, 200 ms), the played part of the seek bar glides between position reports.
- **"Pixelated borders"** (Pushpa's `add better icons`): could not be reproduced here at 100 % (the 4x crops of the pill and the ring are smooth); his crop shows non-anti-aliased edges. Defensive change: the redundant `.clip(shape)` in front of `.background(shape)` + `.border(shape)` was removed from buttons, pills, chips, glass circle buttons, text boxes (one anti-aliased path instead of a clip on top of it). `Dp.Hairline` already is a 1 px stroke in Compose's border (not a zero-width one). Not fixed: scales whose first whole-pixel step is above 8 dp (110 %, 115 %, 130 %, ...) keep the D3D stretch of section 24 (EvenPixels.step); worth a look if the report comes back.
- Test copy recipe used: scratch copy of `shared_prefs`, `files`, `code_cache` of the portable data with both Firebase URLs set to `http://127.0.0.1:9/` and `loggedIn` false, `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="-Dcloudstream.ipcport=52999 -Dsun.java2d.uiScale=1.0"` (100 % scale). A leftover `files/last_error` / `crash.log` after a killed run shows "Safe mode on" in the overlay of the player: delete them.

## 54. Owner round after the first build of section 53 (2026-10-14, evening)

- **Extension list on Home** rewritten (`ui/shell/ProviderSelector.kt`): a button with the extension's icon and name opens a panel with a search box (Up / Down / Enter), rows with icon, language and offered types, "Extensions" and "Stremio add-ons" groups, Random on top. It replaced the plain ComboBox (which cut the name to "Cin…").
- **Library**: the failure page had no way out (the list switcher lived only inside the grid header), so one failing service (AniList / MAL signed out, offline) locked the whole library. It now shows the service name with the error, *Try again* and an *Open another list* menu. `LocalList.library()` no longer fails as a whole on one bad entry (`runCatching` per item, no `?: return null` on an empty watch state list). The exact error the owner saw is unknown (not reproducible: Local list loads on a copy of the portable data).
- **Several windows** (`platform/MultiInstance.kt`): a plain start while the app runs opens a second window on its own data folder `<data>/instances/<n>` (copy of `shared_prefs` + `files`, `code_cache` as a folder junction, own IPC port 52525 + n - 1). Deep links (`://`, `cloudstream...`) still go to the first window, which keeps the tray icon and the new-episode checks. Switch: Settings > Appearance "Several windows" (file `single-instance.txt`). What is watched in a second window is not merged back (two processes must not share one data folder: each writes the whole prefs file). Tested with two `runDev` on one data dir (ports 8765 / 8766).
- **Player picture too low / controls too narrow** (owner's screenshot: window 1246 x 764, video window 1246 x 828 so the picture started 32 px too low and was cut, controls window 872 wide): `NativeVideo.fitChildren()` puts mpv's child window back to the canvas client size whenever it differs, and the loop of `NativeOverlayWindow` now also sets the real bounds of the controls window when they differ from the video's (not only the dialog state). Not reproduced (owner asked not to).
- **Borders**: every 1 px border (`.border(Dp.Hairline, ...)`, about 90 places) is now `hairBorder` (`ui/fluent/Edges.kt`): a filled ring (outline minus the outline one pixel in, even-odd) instead of a stroke, so thin curves no longer depend on the GPU's stroke renderer. The conditional ones (`if (selected) 1.5.dp else Dp.Hairline`) are still strokes. The `.clip(shape)` in front of `.background(shape)` was also removed from flyout rows and the combo box.
- Test-run gotcha: a td copy of the portable data may lack `HAS_DONE_SETUP` (the wizard opens); click "Skip setup".
- **2026-10-14 night: "all extensions got deleted" in the portable build.** Not the app: `data/shared_prefs/rebuild_preference.xml` went from 92 KB (23 extensions, repositories, bookmarks, accounts) to 9 KB during the owner's first run of the day (16:43); the extension files themselves (`files/Extensions`, `code_cache/dex-jvm`) were untouched. What survived is only the resume-watching category, and `ULTIMA_PROFILE_TOMBSTONES` holds `{"rebuild_preference": <Oct 12 22:00>}`: another device (the phone) deleted its whole profile on Oct 12 and the Ultima sync plugin applied that tombstone here, deleting the bookmark / extension / search-history / settings keys. Repaired by copying `backups/prefs-20261014-164335/rebuild_preference.xml` back (the wiped file is kept as `backups/prefs-wiped-by-sync-20261014/`) and setting `loggedIn` to false in the Ultima sync config so the tombstone is not applied again at the next start (the owner turns sync back on in the plugin when the other device is in order). `PrefsBackup` now never deletes the newest copy that still has the extension list. Ideas not done: a dialog "your list was emptied during this run, restore the copy of the start?".

## 55. Owner round of 2026-10-15 (eight reports)

- **Keys went to the browser's video** (two videos side by side, CloudStream in front): the controls window over a native video is a no-activate window, so a click on the picture left the keyboard with the program that had it before (the browser), and Space / arrows paused ITS video. `WinChrome.activateMainWindow()` (SetForegroundWindow, allowed because this process just got the click) is called on every press in the controls window. Media keys (`WinMedia`): a low-level keyboard hook serves them to the player whenever a window of this process is in front (nobody else sees them), the global hot keys remain for the case that another program is in front, and **both are only held while a player page is open** (before, the hot keys were taken at the first video and never given back: the media keys of a music player did nothing for the rest of the run; probed from another process: 1409 while a player is open, free after it is left). mpv gets `input-media-keys=no` / `media-controls=no`. Real keys were not injected (test hygiene): the activation path is by code reading plus the hot key probe.
- **Several windows share the data** (`MultiInstance`, `SharedPreferencesImpl`): the second window used to work on a copy. Now all windows use the same folder. A write re-reads the file under a lock on `<data>/.prefs.lock` and puts only the keys this process changed on top (so nobody's changes are wiped), a timer looks at the file once a second and takes over another window's changes (listeners get the keys). `AccountManager` watches the account keys, so a sign-in or log out in one window shows in the others (tested with two runDev on one data dir: log out of AniList in window 1, window 2 says "Not signed in"). IPC ports: first window 52525, the others 52527 and up (52526 is `OAuthCallback.PORT`: with the old numbering window 2 listened on the sign-in port and the sign-in could not come back; a likely cause of "MAL not working" while two windows were open). Own log file `logs/app-<n>.log`; the first window keeps tray icon, update checks, window.properties. Not merged (last writer wins): `watchlog.json`, `resume_extras.json`, `notices.json`.
- **Seek preview**: the bubble was placed with a guessed constant and measured inside the 28 dp bar (the picture was cut to the bar's height and centred in what was left, half a height too high). `SeekBubble`: the picture with the time in a pill over its lower edge, bottom 12 dp above the bar, centred on the pointer, no frame; black bars of a letterboxed film are cut off (`SeekThumbs.content()`: union of the non-black areas of all frames). Dev: `/player?hold=1&seekhover=0.42` shows the bubble without a pointer, `/overlayshot` looks at the controls window.
- **Extension icons** (Home picker): Google's `s2/favicons?sz=` stretches the site's 16 px icon; `ExtensionIcons.sharper` asks `t3.gstatic.com/faviconV2 ... size=256` (the site's real icon, 192 px measured for nimegami.id).
- **Dotty borders**: a 1 device pixel line on a curve is a string of dots at 150 %. `hairBorder` is now a stroke (the GPU's analytic circle / rounded-rectangle shaders, uniform on curves; the filled ring was uneven in the corners) one density-independent pixel wide: 1 px at 100 %, 1.5 px at 150 %. A/B on the real window at 150 % (`/border?mode=stroke|ring&width=<multiplier of 1 dp>`, `cap.ps1`-style real-screen capture at 8x). The conditional `if (selected) 1.5.dp else Dp.Hairline` strokes of the settings pages are unchanged.
- **Zinkmovies / Desi popup**: the "Desi Repo community" dialog of the Desi extensions closed itself after a while. `WebView.loadDataWithBaseURL` with base `https://desi-extension.local/` and an `AndroidPopup` bridge now calls the extension's own `AndroidPopup.close()` at once (the dialog is dismissed before it is drawn; the extension still counts it as shown, 24 h).
- **Log out / MAL**: logging out worked (tested with fake accounts on a scratch copy: `auth_tokens/<service>/0 = []`, `auth_ids = 0`) but nothing said so. Now a toast "Signed out of <service>", the library refreshes, and the rows in Settings > Accounts & security say "Signed in as <name>" / "Not signed in" (`AccountManager.changes`). MAL: the authorize and token endpoints were probed with the built-in client id (redirect `http://localhost:52526/mallogin` accepted, token endpoint passes client authentication without a secret, API calls with `X-MAL-CLIENT-ID` work, both field lists accepted); the sign-in code is fine up to a real approval (not done: needs the owner's login). Error text for a used-up code no longer blames a "web" client. **The portable data's account keys were wiped again at 18:36 on Oct 14** (`rebuild_preference.xml` 92 KB -> 37 KB, `ULTIMA_PROFILE_TOMBSTONES` still holds the Oct 12 stamp; `auth_ids/mal/0` left, every `auth_tokens/*` gone): the Ultima sync plugin applies a phone's profile deletion. Accounts have to be signed in again there; turning sync off in the plugin (or deleting the tombstone key) stops it.

## 56. Owner round of 2026-10-16 (six reports)

- **Extensions and accounts wiped, here and on the phone (probable cause found, not seen happening)**: `SharedPreferencesImpl` replaced the file by renaming the old one to `.bak` and the new one in, so for a moment `shared_prefs/rebuild_preference.xml` did not exist. The Ultima sync extension (`UltimaBackupUtils.detectAndProcessProfileDeletions`, `getAllAccounts` lists `shared_prefs`) takes a profile whose file is missing for deleted: records a tombstone (`ULTIMA_PROFILE_TOMBSTONES`, stamp of 12 Oct 22:00 is in the data), removes the profile's keys here and pushes the deletion to the phone. The file is now replaced in one step (`Files.move` ATOMIC_MOVE + REPLACE_EXISTING, else an overwrite in place), never renamed away; test `SharedPreferencesNeverMissingTest` polls `exists()` during 400 commits. A stale `.bak` is only used when the file itself is gone. Not proven (no log of the moment): `logs/app.log` was never written (see below).
- **"Installed" on the repository page but not in Installed extensions**: that page decides by the file, the list and the loading at start go by `PLUGINS_KEY`. `PluginManager.adoptDownloadedPlugins` puts files that are on the disk but not in the list back into it (and loads them): when a repository's page is opened (`PluginsViewModel`) and 25 s after the start for every repository (`adoptUnlistedPlugins`, only when `hasUnlistedFiles`). Tested on a scratch copy with `PLUGINS_KEY` removed: 0 extensions at the start, 23 after the pass, "25 installed" on the Extensions page.
- **Zinkmovies "Cloudflare Bypass" sheet** (it is the plugin's `CloudflareWebViewDialog`, a bottom sheet, not the Desi pop-up of section 55): it waits for `cf_clearance` of the host it was given (`new4.zinkmovies.foo`) and for a title that is no challenge; when the site sits on another host (the hub `redirect.zinkmovies.org` -> `new5...`) the cookie never comes, the sheet stays two minutes and every request that fails asks for it again (seven times in two seconds measured). `DialogFragment` (shim): a challenge sheet (class name has `Cloudflare` / `CFBypass`) is not shown again within two minutes of the end of the last one (its `onDismiss` runs at once, the extension hears "no cookies"), and a sheet whose page is no challenge any more (title) for 6 s, at least 10 s after it opened, is closed. Measured: sheet shown at +2 s, closed at +15 s, the repeated requests refused. The plugin stays broken until its author updates the domains. Section 55's Desi pop-up closing is kept.
- **StreamPlay subtitles**: the dozens of online files per language are looked at in the background as soon as they arrive (`MpvPlayer.checkSubtitle`: 4 at a time, the language wanted first, fetched through the same path as a choice, cleaned file kept so choosing it does not download again). Dead links, block pages, empty files and files without cues leave the list; within one name the files that work come first, files that end far from the film's end (< 50 % or > 120 % of the duration: another cut) go last; the automatic pick prefers a checked file (`sourceAffinity`) and replaces a pick that turns out dead. Test with local files: missing / HTML / empty / short / good delivered in that order: only the short and the good one stay, the good one is chosen.
- **Play / pause**: `togglePause()` decides by what the viewer last asked (`pausedByUser`) and not by the property mpv reported a moment ago; Space shows the HUD of what the press did (it said "Playing" for a press that paused a buffering video); the main button shows pause while the video plays or waits for data (`PlayerSession.showsPause`), play when paused. Quick presses checked through `/key`: three Space presses end paused.
- **"Cancel in settings"**: not reproduced (Extension languages, Add repository, the folder chooser's Cancel all close and change nothing). Hardened: the dialog buttons close the dialog even when the handler of the button throws (`try/finally`). Waiting for which dialog it is.
- Found on the way: `logs/app.log` was never written (`AndroidRuntime.dataDir` is not initialised when `main()` starts the file log, the exception was swallowed by `runCatching`); now it resolves the folder like the start-up does, so a report like the ones above can be read.

## 57. Owner round of 2026-10-16, evening (search in sources, subtitles, priority, encoding, on-screen text)

- **Search in the source list** (`PlayerMenu.SourcesPage`): a pill search box above the sort chips (shown from 5 sources), every word has to be in the name, quality, site name or address. The controls window is a no-activate window, so while the box has the focus `PlayerSession.typing` makes it focusable (`dialogsOpen`); the player's key handler and the keyboard refocus loop leave the keys alone meanwhile (Space typed into the box does not pause). Tested with the new dev endpoints `/oclick`, `/otype` (they dispatch to the controls window's Skia layer) and `/player?menu=<MenuPage>`.
- **"Random" subtitles (Dilwale Dulhania Le Jayenge)**: the automatic choice took the first file that arrived (StreamPlay delivers dozens named "English n"), before the look-ahead of section 56 had said which ones are dead or made for another cut, and then switched when the checks finished. Now `PlayerSession.autoSubtitle` waits up to 15 s for the checks of the files that arrived, ranks them (`checkRank`: ends within the video's length = 1, a little off = 2, far off = 3, dead = out), prefers rank 1, adds a bonus by the source's own affinity (`sourceAffinity`), and a later arrival only replaces the choice when it is a clear improvement (no flip-flop). Tested with `/subdeliver` and local files (`jwebserver`): 404, empty, 9 s, 90 min and the right 38.5 s file for a 40 s video: the first two leave the list, the right one is chosen and stays.
- **Extension priority** (`net/ExtensionPriority.kt`, `ui/ExtensionPriorityDialog.kt`): the extensions to put first, in the order they were put. Settings > General > Extension priority (rows with up / down / take off, search + add); a star on every row of the search's extension list; search results, the search's extension list and the Home picker (`ProviderSelector`: "Pinned" group) follow it. Saved in the preferences of this PC (`extension_priority`, JSON list), not synced. `ExtensionPriorityTest`.
- **Subtitle encoding** (the phone's dialog: list with a check mark, Apply / Cancel): `player/SubtitleEncoding.kt` (list from the phone's `subtitles_encoding_list/values` arrays, filtered by what this runtime can read; key `subtitles_encoding_key`), `ui/SubtitleEncodingDialog.kt`, Settings > Player > Subtitle encoding and Subtitles > Encoding in the player menu. A chosen set is used for every subtitle file (`SubtitleFiles.decode`) and for embedded tracks (mpv `sub-codepage`, set before `mpv_initialize` and re-applied by `MpvPlayer.subtitleEncodingChanged()`, which reloads the subtitle that is on). The jlinked runtime needs `jdk.charsets` (Shift_JIS, GBK, Windows-125x ...): added to the module list in `app/build.gradle.kts`. `SubtitleFilesTest` has a Shift_JIS file.
- **Translate on-screen text** (community request: "signs and game windows are not in the subtitles; OCR + translation over the picture"): T (or Settings gear > Translate on-screen text) pauses, takes the frame from mpv (`screenshot-to-file ... video`: the picture without subtitles and controls, at the video's own size), lets Windows' own OCR read it (`resources/ocr.ps1`, WinRT `Windows.Media.Ocr` through PowerShell 5.1, about 0.7 s for 1280x720, nothing leaves the PC for this), joins stacked lines into sentences, translates them (public Google Translate endpoint, the text only) and draws each piece in a dark box where the original stands (`ScreenTextLayer`; the picture's place in the window comes from mpv's `osd-dimensions`, so letterboxing and Picture size are right). Playing, seeking or leaving puts it away. Languages: the writing is in Japanese / Korean / Chinese / English / "whatever Windows reads" (default Japanese), translated into 18 languages (default English), both in the menu page. **Windows needs the OCR language pack of the language** (Settings > Time & language > Language & region > Add a language > Japanese > Optical character recognition); on this PC only en-GB and en-US are installed, so Japanese shows the instruction card (with a button to the settings page). Tested with English text in a video (translated to Spanish, boxes within a pixel of the original places) and the missing-pack card. The Japanese path itself was NOT run (no pack here). `ScreenTextTest`.
- **Already there (the screenshot of the community message is from an older build)**: Kitsunekko, SubDL and Subtitle Cat are subtitle providers (Settings > Accounts & security, the subtitle search dialog), and the dialog has the "Signs & songs / forced" chip.
- Test-copy gotcha: `PluginManager.adoptUnlistedPlugins` (section 56) puts a plugin whose file is in `files/Extensions` back into the list, so removing Ultima (sync) from `PLUGINS_KEY` of a copy of the data is not enough: delete its `.cs3` too, and leave out the Ultima Firebase URL. The run on 2026-10-16 loaded Ultima once from the copy ("checking for cloud updates", no database configured in the copy, nothing was sent).

**Same evening, second pass (the owner: "what is this" about the star dialog, "still random subtitles")**
- *Search extensions* dialog: the stars sat right behind each name at a different x; the name now takes the room and the Stremio badge and the star stay in one column at the right edge; the list is 300 dp tall so that Apply / Cancel fit a short window; a line says what the star does.
- *Random subtitles, real cause* (found in `data/logs/app.log` of the owner's run, then reproduced with `/playsearch` and with the title page's Start over on a test copy): the subtitles of an extension arrive while the sources are still being collected, so the film's length is **unknown** (`durationMs` 0) and `checkRank` called every readable file "fits" (rank 1); the automatic choice took one of them at once ("English 1", then "English 9" for the source), and the owner then cycled through the list by hand (the repeated "subtitle chosen" lines are `selectSubtitle` = a person pressing S). What the data looks like for Dilwale Dulhania Le Jayenge (StreamPlay, 190 min): 335 files in all languages (193 dead from rezesubs.com), of the ten English files from subs5.strem.io two are the first CD only (700 cues, end at 6000 s), the others end at 11242-11343 s but start at 6 s, 15 s, 16 s, 29 s, 30 s, 38 s, 52 s ... so an offset, not a wrong film, is what "random" looks like.
- New automatic choice (`PlayerSession`): (1) no online subtitle before the film's length is known (`durationMs`; 30 s after the video started at the latest); (2) then the files of the wanted language are looked at (all of them, or 15 s, or 8 s when three fit); (3) of the files that fit, the winner is the one with the **timing most files share**: `SubtitleFiles.timingProfile` (cue starts in 2-minute bins) and `firstCueStart` / last cue of every file, the middle of the files that fit is the "typical" timing (`typicalTiming`, needs three), `timingPenalty` = shape distance x 100 + first-cue difference (1 point per 2 s) + last-cue difference (1 point per 10 s); `autoScore` = 10 x the source affinity - penalty; (4) later arrivals and the end of the checks change the choice only for the dead one or for 10 points more (no wandering); (5) a note "Choosing the best subtitles…" shows while it waits; (6) files of other languages are looked at only when the wanted language is done (hundreds of downloads no longer queue in front of the files that matter); (7) the menu's list puts, inside a name, the files with the typical timing first. On the film above the choice is stable (English 8: starts at 30 s, ends at 11342 s, penalty 1). Dev: `/subtable` lists every online subtitle with rank, source affinity, cues, first / last cue, penalty and score.
- `logs/app.log` tells now: "subtitle automatic: <name> [rank, source]" for every automatic choice ("subtitle chosen" is only a person's choice).

## 58. Owner round of 2026-10-16, night (controls, subtitle pill, Stremio, translate, PiP, borders, schedule, upstream)

- **Episode list** (`PlayerScreen` overlay): a scrim below the controls closes it on a click on the picture; the click does not reach the tap layer (no pause). Tested with `/key?name=E`, `/oclick`.
- **PiP**: `PipSeek` buttons (10 s) around play (`SeekGlyph` is internal now). `WinChrome.place` puts the small window AND the controls window of the native player (`NativeVideo.overlayWindow`, offset measured at the start of a drag / resize) in one `DeferWindowPos` batch; the work area, frame insets and dpi are measured once per drag (`Work`) instead of at every pointer report, and a report that gives the same place is dropped. Log: "with the controls window in one step: true". The feel on a real mouse was not measured (no real input, test hygiene).
- **Controls hiding** (`PlayerContent`): `pointerDown` (a held button) keeps them; `Appearance.controlsHideSeconds` (Settings > Appearance > Hide the player controls: 2, 4, 8, 15 s, Never; default 2 = the owner's rule). "Some people do not see the controls" is not reproduced: possible causes are a pointer that never reaches the transparent controls window (touch, remote desktop), a window that stays unplaced, or white controls over a bright picture; the new setting is the mitigation. Ask for the player mode (native or standard), the display scale and a screenshot.
- **Subtitle pill** (`PlayerSession.lookingGaveUp`, `SubtitlePill`): the "Looking for subtitles" pill is tied to `loadingMore` (any extension still collecting sources), which can last for ever for a title without subtitles; it goes after 8 s, other Loading pills after 20 s, and is not offered again for that video.
- **Stremio** (`StremioAddons.load`): the first start lists Cinemeta with `enabled=false`. Saved lists are untouched.
- **Translate on-screen text, checked on the real native player**: a lavfi source with Arial text (`av://lavfi:color=...,drawtext=...fontfile=/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf`; no drive letter, a backslash or space in the URL makes `/playurl` fail) with `screen_text_from=en-US`, `screen_text_to=es`: "Welcome" -> "Bienvenido", "Weapon" -> "Arma", boxes where the words are. Japanese shows the missing-pack card on this PC (en-GB, en-US only); the Japanese path itself is still not run. Installing the pack is `Add-WindowsCapability -Online -Name "Language.OCR~~~ja-JP~0.0.1.0"` (administrator, a Windows download); not done without the owner's say.
- **Signs in their places**: mpv draws a SECOND subtitle with `secondary-sub-ass-override=strip`: an ASS track becomes plain text stacked at the top (measured with `/mpvshot` on a track with `\pos` signs: all three lines at the top). `=no` keeps positions and styles (signs at their places, dialogue at the bottom). `MpvPlayer.secondaryKeepsPlaces()` (track name has sign / song / typeset / karaoke, or the viewer's choice `secondaryInPlace`) sets it; Subtitles > Second subtitle > Where it is shown. As the main subtitle positions always worked (Universal renderer excepted: it strips).
- **Border in the video player (measured with the real-screen capture, `screencap.ps1` / `edgepx.ps1` in the session scratchpad: make the window topmost for 0.3 s without activating, refuse unless `WindowFromPoint` says it is the window)**: at 150 % a maximised native video had a black column at the right edge (x = 1919 black, 1918 white). AWT puts the content one pixel up and left of the real client area (7 dp inset = 10.5 px, rounded down) and EvenPixels trims the right / bottom, so no child window covered the last column (`WindowFromPoint` gave the frame). `EvenPixels.hold(frame, true)` while a native video fills the window (maximised or full screen): no trim then; now the canvas owns column 1919 and it is white. A "bleed" (a video window 1 dp larger) was tried first and does not work: AWT clips a heavyweight child to its lightweight parent. The bottom edge was not measured (the test picture has black bars there). Other borders (hairlines of the controls) not reproduced here; the owner's report has no detail yet.
- **Schedule** (`ui/screens/schedule/`): `ScheduleScreen` (tabs: Anime timetable, Movies & series, Notifications), `TimetableTab` (`AiringSchedule.timetable(first, 7)`: the viewer's local days midnight to midnight, up to 14 pages of 50 from AniList; sticky day strip - a lazy list sticks a header behind the floating bar, so `StickyDays` adds `TopBarHeight` of room once `firstVisibleItemIndex` is past it; timeline rows = one `item` per minute, now line, aired-today fold; filters My list / Popular (library or popularity >= 4000 and not a short) / Everything; `TimetableState` is hoisted into `ScheduleScreen`), `ComingSoonTab` (slider + shelves), `NoticesTab`; data `notify/ComingSoon.kt`: TMDB `/discover/movie` with `region`, `with_release_type` 2|3 (cinemas) and 4 (digital), the date of the viewer's country from `/movie/{id}/release_dates` (the list shows the primary date), `/discover/tv` first air dates (anime left out, network from `/tv/{id}`), AniList `NOT_YET_RELEASED` anime with `startDate` (a film = cinemas). 30 min cache. Default tab: Notifications while something is unread, else Coming soon.
- **Upstream** (reference/cloudstream now d9c55e2, was d77c856): ported the Dynamic Range Compressor as `Appearance.dynamicRange` (`DynamicRange`: Light -18 dB / +4, Dialogue boost -24 / +12, Heavy -30 / +16; ratio 8, attack 5 ms, release 400 ms) with mpv `af=lavfi=[acompressor=...]` (the shipped full libmpv has the filter; checked with a sine source: audio keeps playing with it), `DevicePreferenceStore.compressorEnabled`, strings and `ic_compressor_24`, the DNS fix (`settings.general.dns.set(newValue)` before `initClient`), `DataStoreHelper.setAccount(showToast)`, 19 updated translations. Not ported: the Compose account screen, test screen, logcat dialog, updater (Android UI / its own updater here).
- Dev rig added: `/mpvshot?file=&mode=subtitles|video|window` (what mpv draws), `/mpvcmd?args=a|b|c`, `/player?second=<name>&inplace=1|0`, `/addsub?...&second=1`. Test sources: `av://lavfi:testsrc2=size=1280x720:rate=30:duration=3600` (a muted test pattern, no real file needed).

- **Settings reset to General** (`SettingsScreen`): measured with a `DisposableEffect` log: only a change of `Appearance.navPosition` rebuilt the page host (`AppShell`'s `when (position)` composes `ContentArea` in another branch, so `PageHost` and every `remember` in it are new); theme, radius, scale, density, spacing, mode do not. Fix: `SettingsNav.views[entry.id]` keeps page, search text and scroll outside the composition (dropped when the entry leaves the stack in `PageHost`). Other pages still lose their scroll on a navigation-position change (not touched; `movableContentOf(PageHost)` would fix it).
- **Settings model** (`SettingsModel.kt`, `SettingsPages.kt`): a page is `List<SettingGroup>`, a group `List<SettingItem>` (id, title, about, keywords, content). Upstream rows are picked by what they keep (`Upstream.key("dns_key")`) or by title for button rows, the app's own cards are `GroupBuilder.card/link/block` (`LookSettings.kt` is now `GroupBuilder.themeItems/layoutItems/backgroundItems/homeItems/windowItems/videoItems/controlItems/...`). What is not picked is not shown (this replaced the deny lists `androidOnly`): phone layout, gestures, poster toggles, search/cast switches of the phone UI, Kitsu posters, Links, benene. Search (`SearchResults`) filters the same items by all words in title + about + keywords + group + page, and shows them working.
- **List status** (`SyncViewModel.autoStarted/autoFinished(includeUnlisted)`, `PlayerSession.positionChanged`): NONE (on no list) is only touched when `auto_status_sync_all_key` is on (default off); Planning / On hold / Dropped still go to Watching, `auto_status_sync_key` (default on) turns the whole thing off. Both are in Settings > Accounts & sync > Lists (they were not in the Compose player screen before, only in the XML).
- **Cloudflare** (`android/.../web/CloudflareGate.kt`, key `cloudflare_solvers_key`, default on): off: `CloudflareKiller` returns a 403/503 Cloudflare answer as it is (0.8 s instead of 60 s on animepahe, measured with `/cfkiller`), `DialogFragment` challenge sheets are not shown, and a plain `Dialog` that holds a `WebView` is refused and its Cancel button pressed (Nepu's `NepuSolverDialog` is an `AlertDialog` with a WebView and a Cancel button: after a blocked request it opens the dialog, polls the cookie jar for `cf_clearance` once a second, retries, and clears and asks again). On: a 3rd such dialog within 120 s pauses them for 20 minutes with a toast (`/cfdialog?hold=ms` is the dev check). **Nepu's flashing, reproduced with the real extension** (installed in a scratch data dir with the new dev `/installplugin?url=&name=&repo=`, which uses the app's own `PluginManager.downloadPlugin`; `/nepusolve?wait=ms` opens its real `NepuSolverDialog`): `wipeWebViewCookies` does `CookieManager.setCookie("https://nepu.io", "cf_clearance=; Max-Age=0; expires=1970")`, `WebCookies.set` keyed that as host-only `nepu.io` while the stored clearance is the domain cookie `.nepu.io`, so nothing was deleted and the dialog's 1 s poll found `cf_clearance=` at once (`result=true after 1131 ms`), the retry was blocked again, the session was cleared and the dialog came back. Fixed in `WebCookies.set` (an expired cookie without Domain deletes every cookie of that name the address would be sent, host-only or domain, and tells the engine's jar): the same call now stays open until a real clearance arrives (`result=null` after the 14 s wait). With the switch off the same call answers false in 2 ms (its Cancel button is pressed).
- **Cancel for solvers** (owner, 2026-10-17 later): `CloudflareGate.solves` (Compose state list of `Solve(host, cancel)`) is filled by `CloudflareKiller.bypassCloudflare` around its `WebViewResolver` (new desktop member `WebViewResolver.cancel()` destroys the pages that instance has open; the wait loop then returns without a request). `ui/CloudflareBar.kt` (`CloudflareSolveBar`, placed after `ToastLayer` in `AppShell`) shows a pill with a spinner and Cancel once a solve is older than 1.5 s; `cancelSolves()` also remembers the host for 2 minutes (`allowsSolving(host)` is false, the next request is not held up). Page dialogs (a `WebView` in a `Dialog`) without a Cancel of their own get a row with one under the page in `AndroidDialogHost` (a button on top of the page is invisible: the web view is a layer Compose cannot draw over; found by screenshot). Dev: `/cfkiller?bg=1&url=` (this server answers one call at a time: a one-minute check held every other call, including screenshots and clicks), `/cfdialog?nocancel=1&keep=1`.

## 61. Owner round of 2026-10-17 (external mpv, "proper, smooth UI")

- **External mpv** (`ExternalPlayers.kt`): `mpvPath()` = the chosen `external_mpv_path_key` (Settings > Player > External mpv, `ExternalPlayerHints.chooseMpv`) else `findMpv()` (registry App Paths, PATH, WinGet Links, scoop, Chocolatey, Program Files, C:/mpv; mpvnet.exe too). `mpvCommand` puts the link first, then `--no-terminal --ytdl=no --user-agent --referrer`, every other header as `--http-header-fields-append=Name: value` (mpv sends them with each request, HLS segments included: no proxy as for VLC), `--force-media-title`, one `--sub-file=` per downloaded subtitle (up to 4, English first), `--start=` after 5 s. Checked with a real mpv.exe (the one in Documents/cs3/cs3_windows/media-runtime; mpv is not installed on this PC otherwise): `/mpvtest?url=&exe=&ref=&at=&x=--vo=null|--log-file=...` and its log shows every option parsed. `ExternalMpvTest` has 5 unit tests. `DesktopMpvAction` is in `VideoClickActionHolder.allVideoClickActions`; `PlayerSession.openExternal(Target)` replaced the `vlc: Boolean`.
- **What was measured for "smooth"** (scratch copy of the owner's data without accounts or sync, `-Dcloudstream.framestats=1`, the packaged app and dev; new dev tool `/edtprofile?start=1&ms=2` ... `/edtprofile?stop=1&top=40` samples the UI thread and lists the busy code): steady state is fine (idle 16.7 ms frames, p99 under 25 ms; a second scroll, menus and title pages stay under 40 ms). The stalls are first visits only: the first scroll down Home (9 to 13 frames over 50 ms, max 150 to 200 ms: composing new rows of poster cards, text layout, first draws), the first open of Schedule (270 to 310 ms), Library (60 to 300), Settings (120 to 480) and the player (300 to 380). Tried and dropped because they did not move the numbers: composing 140 cards off screen to warm the JIT before the first scroll, `LazyLayoutCacheWindow` and a nested prefetch of 10 cards on Home, and a longer class-data-sharing training run (explore, schedule, history, stats, the player with a lavfi test picture and its 15 menu pages: archive 85 to 95 MB, same stalls). So these stalls are composition work and code running for the first time, not class loading.
- **What was fixed**: Settings search first key (586 ms): `AndroidResourceImages.preloadVectors` (709 drawables in about a second on a thread of its own, started from `ScreenWarmup` also when memory is short) takes the icon parsing out of the frame (368 ms left, the code running cold); `SettingsGeneralScreen` sorted every extension language by its localized name on each redraw (60 ms for every switch or slider on General) and now does it once per set of extensions; Settings pages fade in (`AnimatedContent` with no exit, no size animation, one "search" target while typing); `PageHeader` subtitles may take two lines.
- **Not done / ideas**: the first-visit stalls need less composition per card (`PosterCard` has three `animateFloatAsState`, a hover source, a focus ring and a hairBorder each) or composing rows over several frames; the Skia `PictureRecorder` and text layout are the other big items.
