A native file dialog (Settings > General > Download path) freezes a dev instance for as long as it is open: never click those rows in a test run. This PC often has under 1 GB free of 14 GB: `ScreenWarmup` needs 1.5 GB free, so here the page warm-up mostly does not run.

## 62. Smooth UI, second part: what the first-visit stalls really were (2026-10-17, measured with the packaged app on a scratch copy of the data, interleaved before/after runs)

- **Method** (new dev tools, all on the dev server; the app needs `-Dcloudstream.framestats=1` for the frame numbers): `/edtprofile?start=1&ms=1` ... `/edtprofile?jank=40&s=6&top=14` lists, for every frame over 40 ms, how many samples the UI thread was busy or idle in and the code it was in (a park or wait inside an event shows as "HELD UP"); `?threads=1` the CPU time of each thread; `&thread=NAME` samples another thread (the render thread is `skiko-dispatcher-to-block-on`); `/composetrace?start=1 ... stop=1&top=40` times every composable (the compiler's trace markers, `CompositionTracer`); `/framestats` now also prints `stall` (sum of the time over 17 ms); `/gpubench?mode=row|bare&n=12&reps=3` puts n fresh posters in a real Shelf and reports the frame intervals. A long frame with a busy UI thread is composition/layout, a long frame with an idle UI thread is the render thread.
- **Found and fixed: network reads on the UI thread.** `app.get/post(...).text` called from a `LaunchedEffect` (Main dispatcher) reads the HTTP body on the UI thread (NiceHttp's `.text` is lazy and blocking; the profile showed `Http2Stream.waitForIo` under `FlushCoroutineDispatcher`). `AiringSchedule.fetch` (the whole week, up to 14 pages) was the 270 to 310 ms of the first open of Schedule; `Tmdb.json` (artwork and logos of the banner and of every title page) was 40 to 100 ms per answer. Now in `withContext(Dispatchers.IO)`: AiringSchedule, Tmdb.json, ComingSoon, AnimeRelations (relations, recommendations), ExploreClient (Cinemeta, AniList) and StremioClient (`getJson`, `manifest`). New desktop code that fetches from UI code needs the same.
- **Also**: libmpv (`Mpv.INSTANCE`, the 39 MB DLL and its JNA bindings) is loaded on IO from `ScreenWarmup` (the first video loaded it on the UI thread: `MpvPlayer.initMpvIfNeeded`, about 100 ms of the first player frame; the other half of that frame is a window reshape with first-time GPU work); `PosterCard` no longer adds `hoverable` and `focusRing` a second time (`fluentClickable` has both).
- **Packaged, first visit, same machine, max frame / frames over 50 ms (before: 4 runs, after: 3 runs, interleaved; the machine's refresh rate drifts between 60 and 40 Hz, so counts are compared, not sums):**

| first visit | before | after |
|---|---|---|
| Schedule | 278 / 274 / 312 / 289 ms (2 to 3 frames over 50) | 39 / 38 / 34 ms (0) |
| Title page | 97 / 84 / 93 / 134 ms (1 to 3) | 43 / 19 / 40 ms (0) |
| Home, first scroll down | 176 / 154 / 173 / 147 ms (12 / 11 / 7 / 12) | 192 / 151 / 162 ms (13 / 7 / 9): not changed |
| Library, Settings, Search, Stats, Downloads, Extensions | 18 to 35 ms | the same |
| Explore, History | 33 to 70 ms | the same (47 to 75) |
| Settings > Appearance page | 77 / 136 / 99 / 116 ms (1) | 90 / 90 / 84 ms (1) |

  Library and Settings were smooth in these runs because the machine had memory for `ScreenWarmup`; the 300 to 480 ms of earlier notes were memory pressure (paging), not code.
- **Home's first scroll is not fixed.** The UI thread is idle in the long frames; the render thread is inside `Direct3DContextHandler.flush` for 50 to 70 ms per hitch: Skia's Direct3D backend makes the GPU programs and resources the first time a kind of picture is drawn. It is once per process (the second scroll and the rows 4 to 9 of the first are smooth; in the `/gpubench` lab only the first burst of fresh posters costs, 50 to 140 ms, later bursts of 12 to 24 images cost 0 to 25 ms). Composition is about 41 ms of the whole first scroll (PosterCard about 0.7 ms cold), so a cheaper card does not help. Scrolling with the pointer parked outside the cards halves the stall (about 330 vs 590 ms): the hover effects of cards passing under the pointer are the other half. Tried and dropped (no measurable gain): `CompositingStrategy.ModulateAlpha` for the reveal layers, a 128/256 MB Skia cache (default of the app 64 MB), OpenGL and ANGLE instead of Direct3D (same or worse), drawing a pile of real cards once at start (a strip 4 to 150 dp wide at alpha 0.004: first version drew nothing because `requiredSize` centres oversized content on the strip; the fixed one took about 15 % off; drawn in full behind the loader it added 0.7 s to the start and gained nothing). Not tried: reducing the number of different draws of a card (shadows, gradients, layers), or a pre-scroll of the real Home behind the loader (Home's data arrives after the loader lifts).
- **Hover pieces ruled out** (dev build, two interleaved runs each, first scroll with the pointer in the cards' path, frames over 50 ms): no change 9 / 9, without the play overlay of the hovered card 0 / 10, without the lift 5 / 8, without the row's chevron buttons 10 / 10. The one 0 was a fluke: single runs mislead here (the same build varies 7 to 14 frames), so the stall is the sum of many first-time draws, not one removable effect.

## 63. "Audio and video out of sync": what was measured, what was found (2026-10-17, evening)

> **Removed on 2026-10-18 at the owner's request** ("remove the bluetooth latency completely"): `AudioSync.kt`, `AudioSyncTest`, the "Audio sync" menu page and row, the `]` / `[` keys, the Bluetooth bubble, the `audio-delay` option and the device watcher in `MpvPlayer` are gone. What is below is the history of why it was added; nothing of it is in the app any more.

- **Report**: "i feel like audio video out of sync" (no title, source or moment). Started from facts: a scratch copy of the packaged app (the owner's own `desktop_look_*` settings, nothing else: no extensions, accounts or sync) and the new dev build, both muted / volume 0, the owner's native player (`desktop_look_native_player=true`: vo=gpu, d3d11, flip model, `video-sync=audio`).
- **mpv's own sync is fine**: `av://lavfi:testsrc2=size=1280x720:rate=30` + `audio-add av://lavfi:sine=...`, native player: `avsync` 0.000005 to 0.000009 s for two minutes, `total-avsync-change` 0, 0 dropped / delayed frames, `/mpvsample?s=20`: 0 frame gaps over 70 ms, 0 audio clock jumps. Speed 1.5 / 0.75 / 1.0: `avsync` stays 0.00001 s (8 dropped frames at 0.75, none at 1.5). Standard player (software render): `avsync` 0.00001, 0 drops, publish -> draw 14.8 ms avg. The libmpv preload of section 62 only loads the DLL early (`Mpv.INSTANCE`, no option changes): not a cause. No difference in the owner's audio settings either (audio decoder Software, no compressor, no upscaler, no `audio-delay` anywhere in the app before this round).
- **The finding is the output device**. The default Windows output of this PC is `Headphones (Noise Airwave Max 4)`, a Bluetooth headset (two more Bluetooth audio devices are paired: pTron TWS and others). mpv log (`-Dcloudstream.mpvlog=v`): `[ao/wasapi] Selecting device ... (Headphones (Noise Airwave Max 4))`, shared mode, device buffer 1056 samples = 22 ms. mpv delays the picture by the sound still queued *in front of Windows' endpoint* (its buffer plus those 22 ms). A2DP adds codec, radio and the headset's own buffer behind that point (typically 100 to 300 ms) and Windows reports none of it, so mpv's `avsync` reads 0.000 while the voices are heard late. This is the same for every Windows player; others hide it behind a manual offset. **Not measured here**: the real latency of this headset (it would need a microphone at the headphones; the headset is the owner's and may be on their head) - the number to use is the owner's to find with the new control. Also not measured: the native player's picture latency (flip model, `swapchain-depth` 2, `d3d11-sync-interval` 1 = about 1 to 2 refresh intervals, 17-33 ms at 60 Hz, up to 50 at 40 Hz; GDI `GetPixel` of the desktop did not see mpv's swapchain and Desktop Duplication was not tried) and the standard player's (publish -> draw 15 ms plus one refresh).
- **Added: Audio sync** (`player/AudioSync.kt`, page `MenuPage.AudioSync` in `PlayerMenu.kt`, root row "Audio sync" with the value). `earlierMs` (positive = sound earlier) -> mpv `audio-delay` = `-earlierMs/1000` (mpv manual: positive delays the audio, negative delays the video; checked by measurement below). Steps of 50 ms, limit +-1.5 s, Reset always shown (dimmed at 0: a button that appears under the stepper moved it and the next click missed). **Kept per output device**: the default render endpoint's id and name are read through Core Audio with JNA vtable calls (`IMMDeviceEnumerator::GetDefaultAudioEndpoint`, `IMMDevice::GetId / OpenPropertyStore`, `PKEY_Device_FriendlyName`, on a thread of its own in the MTA, 1.5 s limit, `null` when it fails -> one shared "default" value); pref key `desktop_audio_sync_ms:<endpoint id>` in the main preferences (0 removes it). Applied as the `audio-delay` option when a core starts (`MpvPlayer.initMpvIfNeeded`, not headless) and live with `applyAudioSync()`; the page re-reads the default device when it opens (headphones switched on after the video started). `ScreenWarmup` reads the device once after start. mpv's WASAPI output only follows a changed default device when a new core starts; nothing was changed there.
- **Before / after on the same clip** (`/playurl av://lavfi:testsrc2...` + the sine, `time-pos` (picture) minus `audio-pts` (sound), 25 samples each, sequential HTTP reads so about +-30 ms of noise): delay 0 -> +21 ms (min -1 / max +57); `audio-delay=-0.2` set directly -> -178 ms; 200 ms earlier clicked in the menu (4 x Earlier) -> -172 ms (min -192 / max -155); Reset -> +21 ms (min -7 / max +43). The saved value was applied by a new core at start (`audio-delay=-0.050` read back right after a restart). `avsync` does not move with `audio-delay` (it is the error against the wanted offset), so it can not be used to see this control work.
- **Dev/test notes**: `:app:test` green (new `AudioSyncTest`: sign, limit, device lookup never throws). Dev `/mpv?name=audio-delay&set=-0.2`, `/player?menu=AudioSync`, `/look?native=false` (toggle, then wait 3 s before `/playurl`: switching during a load left the native window gone: "Failed initializing any suitable GPU context"). `/playurl` URLs need `%7C` for `|` in `/mpvcmd` args and `%2B` for `+`. **The packaged scratch exe registers `cloudstreamapp:` / `cloudstreamrepo:` / `cloudstreamsearch:` in HKCU on every start (`WindowsProtocols`)**: a test run from a scratch copy leaves them pointing at the scratch exe; they were put back to `dist/CloudStream-Portable/CloudStream.exe` (the app registers its own path again at its next start). Mute BEFORE playing a source with sound: a new core resets `mute`/`volume` (use a lavfi source without sound, or a Windows session mixer), a 12 s wait before muting a real HLS stream could have played it on the owner's headphones.
- **What the owner should do / tell us**: play something, open the gear menu > Audio sync > Earlier until the voices match; the number (and the headset name) is what to report. If the sound is wrong with the *speakers* too, or drifts over minutes, or only for one source, tell us which title / source / when - none of that could be seen in the cases tested here.
- **Follow-up, same evening**: the owner: "still audio out of sync when using headphones, rest all apps is fine" (not said whether Audio sync was tried). Measured more, all muted / volume 0, scratch data without accounts:
  - **Windows reports no latency for the headset to ANY app**: `IAudioClient::GetStreamLatency` = 0.0 ms on `Headphones (Noise Airwave Max 4)` before and while a silent shared-mode stream runs 6 s (mix 48 kHz stereo float, device period 10 ms, buffer 1056 frames = 22 ms). mpv's `ao_wasapi.c` (`get_device_delay`) takes `IAudioClock::GetPosition` + QPC correction + the buffer, the same information any other WASAPI player has; it never calls `GetStreamLatency`. So a plain WASAPI app cannot see the A2DP delay either; why the owner's other apps look right is not explained by anything found here (candidates: they are equally late but less noticeable, or a Windows BT path that only they use).
  - **The native player's picture is on time**: a 1 Hz flash (`av://lavfi:color=c=black:s=640x360:r=30,drawbox=...:color=0x909090:t=fill:enable='lt(mod(t,1),0.2)'`) timed with DXGI Desktop Duplication (QPC time of each composed desktop frame, one pixel of the video read back on the GPU) against mpv's `audio-pts` (clock fit residual 0.3 ms): the native player's picture reaches the composed desktop **12.6 ms (median 11.8, sd 3.7, n=19) after its place on the audio clock**; the same clip in standalone mpv (`media-runtime/mpv.exe` of the owner's other project, `--ao=null`, own window) **6.5 ms** (6, 6, 7, 7, 7, 7). The 5 ms difference is the embedded window; neither is near what a viewer can notice, and a real panel adds at most one refresh (17 ms at 60 Hz) after the composed frame. This replaces the "not measured" for the native picture above; the standard (software) player was not timed this way (publish -> draw 15 ms + Compose + DWM).
  - Audio sync page checked in the **native player's controls window** too (`/player?menu=AudioSync`, `/overlayshot`, `/oclick`): 3 x Earlier -> `audio-delay=-0.150`, Reset -> 0.
  - Harness (scratchpad, not in the repo): `dup.ps1` (Add-Type C# with raw vtable calls: D3D11CreateDevice -> IDXGIOutput1::DuplicateOutput -> AcquireNextFrame, `LastPresentTime` is QPC; the frame's dirty rects can NOT be used, mpv presents every frame so the whole video area is dirty every time; read one pixel through a 1x1 staging texture instead; `SetProcessDPIAware` first or the window rect is in scaled coordinates). `-Pipe` reads `audio-pts` from standalone mpv's JSON IPC (`--input-ipc-server=<name>`), otherwise from the dev server `/mpv?name=audio-pts`. The test window must be visible and not minimised (a minimised window reads at -32000,-32000: restore it with `ShowWindow(root, SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE)`), the harness makes it topmost without activating it and puts it back.
  - **Still open / what is needed from the owner**: the number that fixes it on the headset (Audio sync > Earlier, in the player menu); whether the speakers are fine; which other apps and which sources/titles. If Audio sync fixes it, the cause is the unreported Bluetooth delay (the app now has the control Windows does not give). If it does not, something specific to one source (e.g. a DASH/HLS source with a separate audio track offset) is the next suspect: needs the title and source name.
  - **Owner, after the follow-up**: "still video coming first then audio a little later" = the sound is late, the case Audio sync > **Earlier** is for (a few presses of 50 ms; the number was not asked for yet). Added quick keys in the player: **]** sound 50 ms earlier, **[** later (`PlayerScreen.onKey`, `AudioSync.label()`, a clock HUD bubble "Sound 200 ms earlier", entry in `Shortcuts.player`): checked in the native player with the dev `/key?name=close_bracket|open_bracket` (4 x ] -> `audio-delay=-0.200`, 1 x [ -> -0.150; `/overlayshot` shows the bubble). Bluetooth could not be detected reliably from the endpoint's properties (dumped them: nothing names the bus), so there is no automatic default and no "this is Bluetooth" hint.
  - **Confirmed by the owner**: with the headset (default output `Headphones (Noise Airwave Max 4)`), 6 presses of ] = **300 ms earlier** made picture and sound match. That is the unreported Bluetooth delay; the value is kept for that device (`desktop_audio_sync_ms:<endpoint id>` in the owner's own data), so it applies to every video from then on, and the speakers keep 0.
  - **Automatic for Bluetooth** (owner: "can't it match automatically, I can't give the users this one"): Windows gives no Bluetooth latency to any app (stream latency 0 ms in every stream category Other / Movie / Media / Communications, `IsOffloadCapable` 0, the `IAudioClock2::GetDevicePosition` counter only a free-running device clock), so the value can not be read, only guessed and then learned. `AudioSync` now detects a Bluetooth **music (A2DP)** output from the default render endpoint's property store: `PKEY_Device_EnumeratorName` (`{a45c254e-...},24`) = `BTHENUM` and the endpoint's interface string (`{b3f8fa53-0004-438e-9003-51a46e139bfc},2`, `BTHENUM\{0000110B-...}`; `0000111E` is the hands-free phone link, left alone; the registry copy under `MMDevices\Audio\Render\<id>\Properties` shows the same). Value of the day: what was chosen for this endpoint (also 0 = reset), else what was chosen last for any Bluetooth device (`desktop_audio_sync_bluetooth_ms`), else **250 ms** (`BLUETOOTH_DEFAULT_MS`, a typical SBC / AAC delay on Windows; the owner's headset needs 300, a low-latency aptX one 40-120: those press `[`), else 0. The automatic value is never written; the first `]` / `[` / Reset writes it for the device. `AudioSync.announceOnce()` + a HUD bubble 3 s into the first video of each device: "Bluetooth: sound 250 ms earlier  ·  ] and [ adjust"; the menu page says "Set automatically for <device>" and explains in one sentence. Tested on a fresh profile (no saved value): `audio-delay=-0.250` at start, bubble seen in `/overlayshot`, one `]` -> -0.300 and both prefs written (device 300, learned 300); `AudioSyncTest` covers the value rules. Not tested: a Bluetooth device that is not the one on this PC, LE Audio, a USB Bluetooth dongle (same BTHENUM), AirPods.
  - **Why 250 and not a measurement**: the only way to measure the real delay is a microphone at the earcup or a tap test (user taps when they see a flash and when they hear a click; the difference of the two reaction times is the delay, about +-50 ms). That is the next step if 250 + learning is not enough: a 10 s "match the sound" test on first Bluetooth use. Not built.
  - **Headphones switched on or off in the middle of a video** (owner's question): mpv's WASAPI output already follows the default device by itself (`ao_wasapi_changenotify.c`: `OnDefaultDeviceChanged` for the multimedia role while no device was named, and `OnDeviceStateChanged` / `OnDeviceRemoved` -> `ao_request_reload`: the sound reopens on the new default with a short gap, the video goes on), so only the offset had to follow. `AudioSync.startWatching()` (from `MpvPlayer.initMpvIfNeeded`, `stopWatching()` in `release()`, counted) looks at the default render endpoint every 500 ms on a daemon thread ("AudioSyncWatch", 15 ms of CPU in 24 s; gone after the video is left, checked with `jcmd Thread.print`); another endpoint id -> `applyDevice` (saved value of that device / the Bluetooth guess / 0) and `MpvPlayer.applyAudioSync()`; the bubble ("Bluetooth: sound 250 ms earlier ...") is keyed on `AudioSync.deviceKey` so a Bluetooth device switched on mid-video says it once, 3 s later. The lookup runs outside the lock. Worst case the new device has the old device's value for up to half a second (the video holds back or runs ahead for that moment). **Not tested with a real switch** (it would cut the owner's headphones): `AudioSyncTest.theOffsetFollowsADeviceSwitchedInTheMiddleOfAVideo` drives `pollOnce()` with a fake device source (speakers -> headset -> speakers -> none), and the log of a 12 s run shows no false change from the polling.


## 64. "All my data was deleted after the in-app update" (2026-10-18)

- **Report** (a few minutes after the update from 1.0.3 to 1.0.4 on an installed copy, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\CloudStream`, data in `%APPDATA%\CloudStream`): extensions, repositories, search history and settings gone.
- **What the files say.** `backups/prefs-20261018-093126` (27 files, taken by the old version) against `shared_prefs` after the update (9 files): the 18 `profile_*.xml` files of the Ultima sync extension are gone and `rebuild_preference.xml` went from 270 KB to 17 KB (`PLUGINS_KEY`, `REPOSITORIES_KEY`, 93 search-history keys, the video profile keys lost; resume watching and bookmarks stayed), and `ULTIMA_PROFILE_TOMBSTONES` now holds `{"rebuild_preference": 12 Oct 21:59}`, the same tombstone that wiped the portable copy on 14 Oct (section 56). The sync times in the data show that this copy had not synced since 12 Oct: its first sync after the restart applied the tombstone that sits in the owner's cloud database.
- **Not the updater.** Read: `UpdateCheck.install` (only the program's own folders are replaced), `cloudstream.iss` (the `data` folder of an MSI folder is moved, `%APPDATA%\CloudStream` is never touched), `patch-msi.ps1` (removes `app` and `runtime` only). The wipe came from the sync extension, and the update was only the moment of the first sync.
- **Repair of the owner's copy.** With the app closed: copies of everything first (`dist/rescue-data-20261018/`: the 1.0.3 copy outside the data folder `CloudStream-keep`, the 09:31 backup, the wiped state), then `rebuild_preference.xml` and the 18 `profile_*.xml` of the 09:31 backup put back, and Ultima's `loggedIn` set to false in it so that 1.0.4 (without the guard) does not apply the tombstone again. The tombstone is still in the cloud: the sync stays off until a version with the guard is installed; the guard keeps the data whatever the cloud says.
- **Fix in the code.** `android/.../runtime/WipeGuard.kt`, used by `SharedPreferencesImpl.commitToMemory`: removal of `PLUGINS_KEY` / `REPOSITORIES_KEY` while they hold something is refused; removals that add up within 2 minutes to >= 25 keys and >= 40 % of the file (one `clear()` or a run of single `remove`s) are refused and the keys removed earlier in the window are put back; puts always go through. `WipeGuard.allow {}` lets the user's own bulk changes through (clear history, delete a profile, clear search history, restore a backup). The log names the caller (`WipeGuard: kept N of the M keys ...`) and a toast says what happened. `UpdateCheck.install` takes one more `PrefsBackup.snapshotNow` before it installs. Tests: `WipeGuardTest` (7). Checked in the real app on a throwaway copy of the data with sync off: dev `/wipe?mode=clear|singles|plugins` left 291 of 291 keys each time.
- **Not covered.** Code that writes the XML file directly (not through the preference calls) or deletes `profile_*.xml` files is not stopped; it was not seen doing so. The decompiled plugin was not read (no decompiler on this PC); the guard is built on what the data shows (keys removed in bulk, the extension keys).

## 65. "Pixelated everywhere", See all stops at 20, the mpv source list (2026-10-20)

Owner: "pixelating issue everywhere in the app", "play in mpv, sources list too small, unable to see what it is exactly", "catalogs, when clicked show all, show only 20", "need proper smooth uniform ui". Earlier reports of pixelated icons / borders (feature request 10, sections 53 and 54) were never reproduced; this one was.

- **Cause of the pixelation (read from the bytecode of coil-core-jvm 3.3.0, then seen on the real screen):** `coil3.util.makeFromImage` (the Skia decoder's shrinking and enlarging) ends in `Canvas.drawImageRect(image, src, dst)`, the overload without a sampling mode = **nearest neighbour**. Every picture whose decoded size differs from its place was resampled like that: a 2000 x 3000 poster in a 340 x 510 card keeps one pixel in six (stairs, white speckles around thin black lines: seen on the One Piece card of the Popular page), a 185 px poster is a mosaic. The cache of the owner's portable build held mostly 2000 x 3000 and 3840 x 2160 originals plus ~60 of 185 x 278 and 40 of 780 x 439 (TMDB w185 / w780 sent by extensions). `FilterQuality.High` in `RemoteImage` could not help: the bitmap is already wrong when it reaches Compose. The window itself was fine (scene 1920 x 1128 = canvas = client at 150 %, at six window sizes; EvenPixels holds).
- **Fix 1, `ui/components/ImageQuality.kt` `CrispDecoder`** (registered in `ImageModuleCoil.buildImageLoader` and the early loader in `Main.kt`, in front of Coil's own): same target size as Coil computes (`DecodeUtils.computeDstSize` / `computeSizeMultiplier`, INEXACT capped at 1, so the memory cache behaves as before), made by `ImageQuality.scaled`: halving with a bilinear 2:1 draw (= box average) while the picture is more than twice too large, then Catmull-Rom for the last step and for enlarging. Real-screen capture (`CopyFromScreen`, 2x crop) of the same card before / after: speckles and stairs gone.
- **Fix 2, `ImageUpgrade` interceptor:** before a request, `betterUrl(url, need)` where `need = max(width, 2/3 height)` of the resolved size: TMDB `/t/p/wNNN/` goes to the smallest of 185 / 342 / 500 / 780 / 1280 / original that is >= 95 % of `need` (nothing when the current one is within 10 %), metahub poster / background / logo small -> medium -> large. When the larger address answers an error the original is requested (and the address is remembered as missing for the run). AniList (`large/bx21-...` exists, `extraLarge/` with the same name does not) and Kitsu were probed with curl and left out; unverified rules (IMDb `_V1_`, WordPress `-WxH`) were not added.
- **App logo** (192 px, drawn at 16 to 64 dp through `BitmapPainter`'s default Low filter) now `FilterQuality.Medium` (mip maps) in `Images.kt`, `AppShell.kt`, `Startup.kt`.
- **See all** (`Route.Section(title, items, more)`): `more` answers the whole list so far and whether another page can follow; `SectionPage` calls it when the last 6 cards come into view (`PosterGrid(onEnd = ...)`), footer progress ring, "210+ titles". Home rows use `HomeViewModel.expandAndReturn(name)`, Search results `SearchViewModel.expandAndReturn(name)`; both return null while the shelf behind the page is extending the same row (the page keeps the list it has and asks again after 600 ms; three answers without a new title end it). Measured on AniPM Popular: 30 -> 210 titles after six scrolls to the end.
- **Source list of "Play in mpv / VLC / Copy link ..."**: `SelectPopup.SelectLinks` hands the `ExtractorLink`s to `DetailsScreen.showSelectPopup` -> `ui/screens/details/SourcePicker.kt`: 780 dp wide, name over two lines, detail line (site when the name does not say it, HLS / DASH / direct file / torrent, size, seeders, host), quality pill, search box above 5 sources, Best / Quality / Size / Seeders chips (`LastSource.sort`, shared with the player's list). `Overlays.Dialog` got `bodyMax` and clamps its width and body height to the window (`BoxWithConstraints` in `DialogLayer`); the plain choice lists are 520 dp wide with two-line rows.
- **Tests:** `ImageQualityTest` (4: TMDB / metahub rules, left-alone cases, a 1 px checkerboard shrunk 4x comes out grey, not black or white); `:app:test` 94 green.
- **Not done / open:** "smooth uniform UI" was not specified further; the pixelation was the visible roughness found (everything else on Home, Explore, Extensions, Details and the dialogs looked consistent in screenshots). The player's own overlay (separate window) was not looked at. Explore shelves have no "View all".
- Test recipe used: scratch copy of the portable build + `shared_prefs`, `files`, `code_cache`, `external_files`, `storage` (no sign-ins, sync keys not present), `runDev -PdataDir=<copy> -PdevPort=8767` with `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS=-Dcloudstream.ipcport=52998`; `scratchpad/screencap.ps1` (SetProcessDPIAware, topmost no-activate 0.7 s, five `WindowFromPoint` checks, `CopyFromScreen` of the client rectangle, undo topmost) and `crop.ps1` (nearest-neighbour zoom).

## 66. "More proper translations needed, everything half done, mainly Arabic and Turkish" (2026-10-20)

- **What "half done" was (measured in a Turkish run):** the Android-style screens (Settings rows, Library status tabs, sort lists) were Turkish because they read `values-b+tr/strings.xml` (738 of 761 texts there; the 23 missing were all added by desktop work: compressor, profile picture, list status, Cloudflare), but every Fluent screen is written with English literals (about 1,100 static and 200 interpolated texts in `ui/**`, plus notify, torrent, update, stremio) and nothing translated them.
- **`i18n/Tr.kt`:** `FText` (the one sink of all Fluent text, `Controls.kt`) calls `Tr.t(text)`. The catalog is `app/src/main/resources/i18n/strings.tsv` (one row per English text, a column per language: `en ar tr`). Lookup order: exact, trimmed, `...` = `…`, templates with `{0}` pieces (anchored regex, longest literal first, pieces looked up on their own up to depth 2; a key with fewer than 4 letters and no word before its first piece only takes numbers: `E{0}`, `{0} ep`, but `on {0}` takes words), then the line cut at `\n`, ` · `, ` • `, ` - `, `: ` and each part looked up. Values may hold `{0,plural,one{#..}other{#..}}` (CLDR categories, Arabic has six). A cache per catalog; `Tr.misses` lists the strings shown untranslated (dev `/i18n`, `?lang=ar|tr|en` switches live, `?res=<android string>` reads an Android string). `CommonActivity.setLocale` calls `Tr.localeChanged()` (a Compose state: every FText is redrawn, no restart).
- **Where texts are built from pieces** the table has the whole sentences ("{0} episodes  ·  {1}", "Nothing from your list airs {0}" with "today", "tomorrow", "on {0}" as pieces) instead of changing the code. Not changed: "Trending {0}in {1}" style Explore titles (the genre / service words inside stay English), TMDB-based labels keep English words inside.
- **Arabic (RTL):** `AppShell` provides `LocalLayoutDirection` Rtl when `Tr.rtl` (ar, arz, apc, ars, fa, he, ur, ckb ...) around everything but the title bar; the player page (`Route.Player`) is forced LTR and the controls window is its own composition (LTR, its menu text is Arabic and flush right). `Icon` mirrors `Icons.pointing` (back, forward, chevrons). Bars whose pill or line glides to a measured x (`NavCapsule`, `PillTabs`, `UnderlineTabs`) are laid out LTR (`fluent/Rtl.kt` `LeftToRight` + `visual(count, rtl)` reverses their items); the first attempt (a `layout` modifier that took the parent's whole width) made the capsule as wide as the window: do not do that. `FluentSlider` flips the pointer's x. `FText` sets `textDirection = Content` and, in an RTL page, `TextAlign.Right` for lines without an alignment. `setLocale` builds the locale with `-u-nu-latn` (digits 0-9).
- **Smaller:** Turkish capitals (`Tr.upper` in the player menu section labels and weekday names), the settings search also matches `Tr.t(title/about/group/page)`, tray menu (`Open CloudStream`, `Exit`) and the new-episode tray text through `Tr.t`, the shortcuts window's Home key is `"Home​"` (otherwise it would be translated as the navigation page), `values-b+ar` and `values-b+tr` got the 23 missing strings.
- **How the table was made:** `tools/i18n/Extract.java` lists every string literal of the desktop code (interpolations as `{0}`), 2,087 candidates; the UI ones were translated by number and joined back to the exact English keys with a merge script (not kept: the table is the source of truth); a walk through every page in each language with `/i18n` then listed what was still English: content (titles, extension names) and Android texts, nothing else.
- **Tests:** `TrTest` (8: exact, pieces, Arabic's six plurals, number-only keys, part-by-part lines, the real table: same pieces in every language, no empty cell, no duplicate English text). `:app:test` 102 green.
- **Not done / open:** other languages (the table has two columns; the Android strings of the others are as before); the Windows OCR language names in the player ("Japanese ...") and TMDB's own texts (plots, titles) stay as they come; the controls window of the player is not mirrored in Arabic; Explore's "Trending <genre> in <country>" rows. A `null` profile name appeared in Settings once after switching languages several times in a second with the dev endpoint (the data store gives the right name in all three languages): not reproduced in a normal start.

## 67. "The whole app feels like a lava pool: not smooth, not consistent, Windows here, Mac there, Material there" (2026-10-21)

- **Measured first (scratch copy of the packaged app with the owner's data, accounts removed):** page switches were already smooth (0 to 4 slow frames each, `-Dcloudstream.framestats=1`), the first visit of Home and Search costs one or two stalls; a streaming search had 1.3 s of stalls in 12 s (composition was 80 ms of it: `/composetrace`; JFR showed two GC pauses of 48 and 25 ms, the Java threads were almost idle), so the stalls are the render thread / first draws, as in section 62. What was found instead is how long things take to settle: a page faded and slid for 440 ms, then every row and card revealed again (cascade of 45 ms per card, 12 cards, 560 ms each: the twelfth card appeared after 1.1 s), the wheel glide had a 90 ms time constant, and `reveal` ignored "Reduced" motion.
- **Why it looked inconsistent (screenshots of every page):** two control languages side by side. Buttons, drop-downs, text boxes and tags were WinUI controls (4 dp corners, a 2 dp accent underline on focus, blue chevrons and icons, a blue "Save"), while the top bar, tabs, chips, the Play button and the search box were pills; the primary was white in one place (`primaryFill`) and blue in another (`ButtonKind.Accent` drew `accentBrush`); selection was a blue-tinted card with a blue border on Extensions and a white lit pill in the top bar; the carousel dots were white on Home and blue on Explore; the Settings left list had a WinUI blue edge mark.
- **One shape language (`FluentShapes`):** everything you press or type into is `FluentShapes.pill` (Button, IconButton = circle, ComboBox, TextBox, Chip, Badge, StremioBadge, `ArtChip`, the Settings left list, the top-bar extension selector, the card-menu chips); everything that holds content is a rounded rectangle (`card`, `overlay`), and rows inside menus, lists and dialogs use `FluentShapes.row` (the overlay radius less its 4 dp inset, so curves are concentric). Check box = `FluentShapes.check` (6 dp).
- **One primary:** `ButtonKind.Accent` now draws `primaryFill / primaryContent / primaryBorder` (white by default, follows Settings > Look > Play button), so Save, Add, Try again, Star on GitHub ... are the same pill as Play.
- **Accent is for state only:** switches, sliders, progress, counts, "NOW" markers. Hover and selection are neutral: `FluentColors.hoverEdge / selectedEdge` (white at 32 % / 70 %) and `controlHover` fill replace the blue tints on the Extensions list and cards, cast and studio cards, notices and episode rows, accounts, profile rings, the card menu, the nav rail and the extension selector; ComboBox chevrons and icons are `textSecondary`; text boxes and the search box get a neutral 1.5 dp ring on focus (no underline, no blue); the selected drop-down row has a check mark instead of a blue bar; carousel dots are white everywhere.
- **One motion budget (`FluentMotion`):** `FAST` 110 / `BASE` 180 / `SLOW` 300 ms and two springs, `springPress` (press feedback) and `springSoft` (hover lift, gliding indicators, morphs; no visible overshoot). The loose `tween`/`spring` calls of the controls, tabs, strip, card lift, play/pause morph and card menu go through them (so Reduced/Off work there too). Page change: fade 200 ms + 16 dp rise over 300 ms (was 240 + 28 dp over 440), the old page leaves in 70 ms. `reveal`: 240 ms, rise 14 dp, cascade capped at 120 ms (was 560 ms, 26 dp, up to 540 ms), now honours Reduced. Menus 150 ms, dialogs 180 ms. Wheel glide TAU 65 ms (was 90).
- **Layout bugs found in a narrow window:** Settings slider rows squeezed the title to one letter per line (`SettingsCard(below = ...)`: the slider goes under the text below 600 dp); the Extensions list header lost the repository name next to the filter box and "Install all" (stacked when `compact`); "Extension priority" had no icon and sat 43 px left of its neighbours; the Library error page printed a Jackson exception, left-aligned (`readableError`, centred).
- **Memory:** Coil's memory cache is a fixed 160 MB (was 10 % of the 3.5 GB maximum heap = 350 MB of native bitmaps the heap size says nothing about; the PC had 1.8 GB free with the app at 900 MB).
- **Checked, left alone:** extension dialogs and engine dialogs already render as Fluent content dialogs (`FluentAlerts`); the Material `DialogHost`/`ToastHost` are not on the native path (dead code); Settings row icons are still Android Material vectors (95 drawables from the upstream pages; a Fluent glyph map would need each one checked by eye); the player's own menus keep their radii (10/6/8 dp).
- **Test recipe used:** scratch copy of `dist/CloudStream-Portable` (app, runtime, exe, portable.txt, shared_prefs/files/code_cache; paths in `rebuild_preference.xml` rewritten with the Java-free `String.Replace` on `\`-escaped paths), run with `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS=-Dcloudstream.devport=8767 -Dcloudstream.ipcport=52998 -Dcloudstream.framestats=1`; dev build with `runDev -PdataDir=<copy> -PdevPort=8766`. The test window gets minimized by the owner's own use of the PC: call `/winstate?act=restore` before every `/screenshot` (a minimized window gives an 800-byte image). `:app:test` green.

## 68. White flash when a video opens, Settings redone, AniList / MAL "not working" (2026-10-21, evening)

- **AniList and MyAnimeList were down, not broken.** `curl` of the app's own query: AniList answers HTTP 403 `"The AniList API has been temporarily disabled due to severe stability issues."` (data null), MAL `{"error":"maintenance"}` and 503 pages; Simkl and Kitsu were fine. The owner's tokens are valid (AniList until 2027-10, MAL until 2026-11-16, decoded from the JWT claims, nothing printed). The app made it worse: an AniList library came back as empty lists, the schedule cached "nothing airs", the MAL list threw a Jackson message, the tracker card offered a Save that could not work.
  - `net/ServiceOutage.kt`: an OkHttp interceptor (added in `RequestsHelper.buildDefaultClient`) that reads what `graphql.anilist.co`, `api.myanimelist.net`, `api.simkl.com`, `kitsu.io` answer (it peeks 4 KB, the answer is not changed) and keeps the service's own words in `ServiceOutage.notes` (a snapshot state map, written on the UI thread) and `message(service)` (any thread, at once). A later good answer clears it. `reason()` knows AniList's `errors` + `data:null`, MAL's `error`/`message`, web pages where data was asked for, 502-504; 404 / `not_found` are not outages; "Invalid token" says sign in again. `ServiceOutageTest` has the real answers.
  - Library: `getAniListAnimeListSmart` / `getMalAnimeListSmart` fall back to the list saved by the last good load (and with none saved throw the service's words); `LibraryScreen` shows a `NoticeBar` (new, `fluent/Rich.kt`) with the words, "This is the list saved the last time it loaded" and Try again. Schedule and Coming soon: `ServiceOutage.requireData` throws the words, so the page says "AniList says: ..." instead of "nothing is scheduled" and nothing empty is cached. Title page tracker: the words under "Tracking on ...", Save disabled when every tracked service is down. Settings > Accounts & sync: the notices at the top.
  - Verified on a scratch copy with the owner's real sign-ins (the services were down): Library on AniList showed the banner and the 5 saved titles; Schedule showed AniList's sentence.
- **The white flash** (reported: "some white patch appearing during the transition when launching a video"). Reproduced with a frame-by-frame capture of the window (scratchpad `cap.ps1` / `cap3.ps1`: CopyFromScreen of the window, % of near-white pixels per frame, plus a 10 ms poll of the app's windows with their sizes and a PrintWindow of each at the first white frame): on the FIRST video of a run the whole window is pure white (96-99 %, split in two halves with a thin black seam, the pattern of an undrawn GPU buffer) for 230 to 370 ms, starting 100-150 ms after the click, in about 3 of 5 cold runs; a second video is clean. The windows list showed the white starts within 10 ms of the video's canvas being added and ends before the controls window is shown, so it is not the controls window (making that window see-through until its first frames are out was tried first: no change, the change is kept, harmless). The UI-thread sampler (`/edtprofile`) during a white run showed `Direct3DRedrawer.resizeBuffers` and `initSwapChain`, and `EvenPixels.hold` (called when the player page opens on a maximized window) takes the 1 dp trim off the content pane: the main Skia canvas goes 1920x1128 -> 1923x1131 in the middle of the page's heavy first composition (the whole player is class-loaded then, 250 ms with the UI thread busy), and the window shows the undrawn buffer until the thread is free. A 1 px heavyweight canvas added at start (to take the "first heavyweight child" effect early) did not help and is gone.
  - Fix (0 white in 6 of 6 cold first videos, was 3 in 5, 2 in 3): `NativeVideoHost` still creates the canvas at once (mpv asks for its handle on the UI thread when the page opens: delaying the SwingPanel made the player give up with "native video window not ready: using the standard player" and set `NativeVideo.broken` for the run: do not do that) but keeps it HIDDEN until two frames and 100 ms after the page opened; `NativeVideo.hostAdded` then lets `PlayerScreen` call `EvenPixels.hold(true)` (the trim comes off only when the video window covers the window's own picture). The controls window is placed ~300 ms after the page opens (was ~100 ms). Into the player the page we leave fades out over 150 ms with the player page under it (`targetContentZIndex = -1`); out of it nothing moves.
  - Test recipe: `cold.ps1` restarts the scratch copy of the packaged app, maximizes it, plays a 10 s silent test clip (the scratch prefs have `desktop_player_volume` 0) and prints the white events; the window must be raised with `top.ps1` (TOPMOST, no activation) or the capture sees the owner's other windows.
- **Settings** redone: grouped rows (consecutive plain rows share one rounded card, hairlines between; blocks such as the colour picker stand alone, `SettingItem.row`), each page has a colour (the unused `Page.tint`) shown as a flat tinted square in the list, in the page header and behind every row's icon (`IconBadge`: the engine's Material vectors and the Windows glyphs now read as one set); an Overview page (the default) with Quick settings (App mode, Animations, Zoom on hover, Poster size, Hide spoilers, Autoplay next episode, Smooth motion: the very same rows pulled from their pages by title) and a tile per page; no "On/Off" text beside switches; a group with some icons keeps the icon place free in the rows without (`LocalReserveIcon`) so text lines up. The logo of the app is not drawn twice in a window with its title bar above the app.
- Lesson: generator edits (`sed`, a Java replacer with `\u0001` for newlines) leave a stray `\` at line ends in this shell; scan the diff for `^\+.*\$` after any scripted multi-line edit (found four), or use the Edit tool.

## 69. Animated profile pictures, the whole live window to seek in, title-bar buttons in the player, wide hero posters (2026-10-21, night)

Reported: "more proper dp icons needed, the current ones are shit, find cool animated dp icons like Netflix's"; "live streams have a buffer of 20 s, some 10 min, the app does not show 10 min to seek, the end keeps being added and the start going"; "close / minimise / resize do not highlight when hovered on the video player"; "some hero side banners are landscape posters, you made them vertical, keep horizontal ones horizontal".

- **Animated profile pictures** (`ui/shell/AnimatedAvatars.kt`, `Profiles.kt`). The set is Google's **Noto animated emoji** (Lottie; `https://fonts.gstatic.com/s/e/notoemoji/latest/<codepoint>/lottie.json`, licence **CC BY 4.0**, credited under Settings > About > "Animated profile pictures", `resources/avatars/LICENSE.txt`): 48 of them in three groups (18 animals, 16 faces, 14 fun things) in `resources/avatars/<codepoint>.json` (3-4 MB in all), each on a colour disc picked to suit it (index into `avatarBackgrounds`). They are played with Skia's own Lottie player (`org.jetbrains.skia.skottie.Animation`: `makeFromString`, `seekFrameTime`, `render`), no new dependency; `Clips` loads a file the first time it is drawn (a few ms), 1.08 zoom to fill the disc. `rememberClipTime`: a picture in a bar plays its ~3 s once every 10 to 20 s at 30 fps, at a time of its own, only while the window has focus, motion is not Off and "Animated profile pictures" is on; under the pointer (top-right avatar, the account tiles) and in the editor it loops. `AvatarStyle.Animated` is a third style next to Picture and Character; the editor shows the grouped grid (hover plays one), the background picker works for Animated too. A profile with no saved look gets one by its number (`defaultAnimatedAvatar`: cat, fox, panda, frog, unicorn, owl, bear, penguin), a profile with its own picture keeps it. The **old stock smiley** (saved look exactly `Character|0|smile`, what every profile that ever opened the editor holds) counts as no choice and shows an animated one; a smiley picked now is saved with a 4th part (`|chosen`) and stays. Checked: editor grid and the top-right avatar render (cat on the owner's scratch copy). Not done: a looping-in-the-bar Netflix style (kept to bursts on purpose: a looping picture keeps the window redrawing all the time); more than 48 pictures.
- **Live streams: the whole window to seek in.** HLS: ffmpeg's demuxer seeks anywhere inside a live playlist, so the window is `HlsProxy.windowSeconds(address)` (sum of the `EXTINF` of the last playlist fetched; 20 s of a short list, 10 min of a long one) and `MpvPlayer.liveWindow()` = (`duration` - that window + 2 s margin) .. `duration` while mpv says `seekable=yes`; else the old cache based `liveRange()`. `liveTarget`, `goLive`, `liveLatency` use it. The seek bar (`PlayerSession.barStartMs/barEndMs`, both seek bars, the bubble "-m:ss behind live", digit keys, left/right) now spans that window and slides as the live edge moves, refreshed every 4 ticks. `demuxer-max-back-bytes` 192 MiB at file load for a live stream so that rewinding keeps what was watched. Checked with a local HLS live simulator (`scratchpad/live/LiveSim.java`: a sliding playlist of real ffmpeg-made segments): window 10 min, seeks to the start of the window and back to live land where they should (`/player?seekto=<ms>` was added to the dev server for it). **DASH live cannot do this**: ffmpeg's dash demuxer answers ENOSYS to seeks in a dynamic MPD (`suggestedPresentationDelay` was tried, same result and reverted), so a DASH live stream (JIO TV, WILLOW...) still rewinds only what was watched, now up to 192 MiB instead of the default cache. The bar itself on a live stream was not looked at on screen (the overlay capture of a held controls window is unreliable here).
- **Title-bar buttons in the player**: they lit through `WinChrome.hover`, which is fed by the main window's non-client hit test; in the player the buttons are drawn by the controls window (its own Compose window, the main one is covered by the video), where no such message arrives. `CaptionButton` now also reads its own `collectIsHoveredAsState` / pressed interaction. Checked with a real mouse (`SetCursorPos` over each button of the playing dev window, screen crops): Maximize shows its grey, Close its red.
- **Hero shapes**: the poster card beside the title (`HeroBackdrop` without a backdrop) and the strip of thumbnails now follow the picture: `RemoteImage(onSize)` reports the decoded size; a landscape poster (ratio > 1.15) is a wide card at its own ratio (46 % of the width, 1.15..2.4, `ContentScale.Fit`), a portrait one the tall 2:3 card as before; in the strip a landscape thumbnail is 100 dp wide and a portrait one 54 dp, squeezed to fit 56 % of the width (max 760 dp). Checked on Home with the live-events source (landscape cards, wide thumbnails) and AniPM (backdrop full-bleed, portrait thumbnails).
- Tests: `:app:test` green. Real data untouched.

## 70. Times on a live bar, drawn profile pictures (2026-10-22)

- **Live times.** `SeekRow` (both chromes) hid the two time labels for a live stream. Now the left one is how far into the window playback is (`PlayerSession.liveElapsedMs`) and the right one the length of the window (`liveLengthMs`, `09:54` on a channel that keeps ten minutes: `MpvPlayer.liveWindow` leaves `LIVE_MARGIN_S` = 6 s at the oldest end, where segments are about to drop); `--:--` until the window is known (first second). The left time follows real moves only (a dead band of 1.6 s: the window's end moves a segment at a time, the position smoothly, so the raw difference jittered by a second). The LIVE pill reads `LIVE −3:20` past 90 s behind. Checked with `LiveSim.java` (sliding HLS of ffmpeg testsrc2 segments) through `/playurl?url=http://localhost:8899/live.m3u8` (NOT 127.0.0.1: `MpvPlayer` skips HlsProxy for it, so the stream is not live there): 00:35 / 01:54 after six J presses, LIVE −78 s.
- **Profile pictures drawn by code** (`ui/shell/AvatarArt.kt` = the `Rig` of shapes + the first characters + the `avatarArt` registry, `AvatarArtMore.kt` = the rest). 25 characters in three groups: Heroes (ninja, samurai/Ronin, astronaut, wizard, cyber runner, pirate, knight, hacker, DJ, vampire), Creatures (cat, fox, panda, bunny, penguin, dragon, owl, frog), Spooky & Space (ghost, alien, robot, skull, oni, monster, pixel invader). Each is a lambda on `Rig` in a 100 x 100 space with a 4 s idle loop (`ART_LOOP`; `ph`, `u`, `lid()` blinks at 1.1 s and 2.9 s, `bump()` one-off events), so it is sharp at every size and has nothing to load. `AnimatedAvatarView` draws the art when `avatarArt[id]` exists, else the old clip; `rememberClipTime(seconds, ...)` is shared (bursts of the loop every 8-15 s at rest, looping on hover / in the editor). The 31 anime/series WebP clips (160 px crops from the shows, an earlier unrecorded session) stay as the group "Scene clips" at the end; saved choices of them still resolve. Default for a profile with no choice: `ninja`, `astronaut`, `cat`, `robot`, `wizard`, `ghost`, `alien`, `samurai` by profile number. Arabic/Turkish rows added for the groups and names.
- **Looking at them without the app:** `AvatarArtSheetTest` draws PNG sheets when `AVATAR_SHEET=<folder>` is set (`AVATAR_TIMES=0,1.1,2.5`, `AVATAR_ONLY=ninja,cat`, `AVATAR_CELL=150`): `AVATAR_SHEET=... ./gradlew :app:test --tests "*AvatarArtSheetTest"`. `AnimatedAvatarsTest` also checks that every picture renders at several times and that the loop ends where it starts.
- Open: the clips are footage of other people's shows (licence), `resources/avatars/LICENSE.txt` now says so; the owner has not judged the new set yet.

### 70d. "Pictures not loading and videos not playing in JIO TV (IND)" (owner, after the portable build)

- Log (`dist/CloudStream-Portable/data/logs/app.log`, owner's own run at 11:12): `UsableAddresses: <host>: not known to the chosen DNS service` for EVERY host (raw.githubusercontent.com, api.themoviedb.org, firebase, jiotvimages.cdn.jio.com), the first JIO TV attempt hung 20 s and was retried. `curl https://cloudflare-dns.com/dns-query?...` and `https://1.1.1.1/dns-query` from this PC both fail (0.7 s refused / 8 s timeout): the owner's `dns_key=2` (Cloudflare DoH) is blocked on this network, and every first request waited for the DoH client's own timeouts (10 s connect x 4 bootstrap addresses) before the system DNS (1.1.1.2, plain UDP, works) was tried. The JIO names themselves resolve fine through the system DNS (Fastly, `media.jio.map.fastly.net`); a poster URL (`.../dare_images/images/MaaHD1.png`) answers 200 in 3 s.
- Fix: `DohProviders.addGenericDns` DoH client connect 2 s / call 4 s; `UsableAddresses.resolve`: IP literals, `localhost` and `:` go to the system DNS, 2 misses in a row (the system DNS knew the name) = "DoH down" for 5 min (system first, DoH when the system does not know a name). Measured with the owner's setting in a scratch profile (`dns_key=2`, `/netcheck`): first requests 7 s / 11 s before, then 1.3 s / 0.7 s / 0.7 s once the breaker tripped. Not checked: JIO TV's video itself (needs the extension and the owner's token); the DNS hang was the only failure in the log.
- **My pictures** got an "Add pictures…" button (several files at once from any folder into `<data>/avatars`), because the owner asked again for the Netflix icons ("use the dp icons from the folder"): still not copied by me (Netflix's artwork); the owner can add them with the button.

### 70c. The e-cloudstream fork (mohamedyassine-benmiled/e-cloudstream-windows), reviewed 2026-10-22

9 commits ahead of `469cb77`, 7 behind (cloned read-only into the session scratchpad, `fork/repo`). What it has: (1) Windows **touchscreen swipes** (`WindowsTouch`, `TouchScroll`, `PointerContact`, tests), (2) an optional **native resume contract** for extensions (`library/.../resume/*`, `NativeContinueWatching`, `CloudEpisodeProgress`, provider-owned Home rows, behind `-Dcloudstream.nativeResume.experimental`; built for its Eimo/AniKoto extension), (3) branding (`e-cloudstream`), its own updater repo/asset names, README rewrite, CI workflows, AGENTS.md.
- **Ported:** touch swipes (new files copied; `Appearance.touchSwipes` + Settings > Look card; `InputMode.touchscreen/touchUi`; `LazyColumn` / `verticalScroll` imports of the list pages and dialogs aliased to `TouchLazyColumn` / `tabletVerticalScroll`; `touchScroll` on the poster grid and the shelves; `primaryContactPressed` for the seek bars and slider; the title bar stays on a touch layout; `WindowsTouch.install` from `NativeApp` and the controls window, every 2 s because Compose can make the canvas again; `testImplementation(compose.desktop.uiTestJUnit4)`), and the updater's `trustedRepositoryDownload` + single auto-check job (`UpdateTrustTest`). Tests green (their 11 touch tests incl. the native subclass one). Not tried on a real touchscreen: a real pointer still reaches the caption buttons with the hook on (close button red on hover).
- **Not ported:** the native resume contract (it only does something with a matching extension, adds public API to `library`, experimental by the fork's own docs; the generic idea, extension-provided "continue watching" rows, can be taken later), branding, its release workflows/README.

### 70b. Same day, after the owner tried the portable build

Owner: some live streams play on Android but not here; live streams take long to open here, instantly on the phone; "black strip" on the pause button; the keyboard pause does not change the icon; "dp's: real ones, not svg generated, see the Netflix page in Chrome".

- **Black strip = a seam in the play triangle.** `PlayPauseMorph` drew the left quad and the right triangle as two paths: their antialiased shared edge left a faint line through the glyph. One `Path` with both contours (`ui/fluent/Morph.kt`). Seen on a real-screen crop (`cap.ps1` = topmost no-activate CopyFromScreen of the dev window with `-Crop x,y,w,h`) before and after.
- **Keyboard pause did not change the icon.** `showsPause` reads `status` (observable) AND `player.pausedByUser` (a plain `@Volatile`). A stream still buffering reports `IsBuffering` paused or not, so a keyboard pause changed no observable state and nothing recomposed; a click redrew the button by its press state, which hid the bug. `MpvPlayer.userPaused` is a `mutableStateOf` now. (Not reproduced with a synthetic key: needs a buffering stream; reasoned from the code.)
- **Hosts only the system DNS knows.** The owner's log: `UnknownHostException jiotvmblive.cdn.jio.com` with `dns_key=2` (Cloudflare DoH). `UsableAddresses.wrap` now asks `Dns.SYSTEM` when the chosen DoH service throws UnknownHost (logged as `UsableAddresses: <host>: not known to the chosen DNS service ...`). Checked with dns_key=2 in a scratch profile: DoH refuses `localhost`, the fallback resolves it and the stream plays.
- **Slow live opens.** Measured in the owner's log: 8-11 s from `loadfile` to "file opened" on live channels (Willow DASH with 8 streams, an HLS event channel, a twimg master with 8 playlists), 2.4 s on one. ffmpeg opens EVERY variant and rendition of a master (and every Representation of an MPD) one after the other, reading the segment three from the end of each live playlist (`live_start_index` -3) and the next one. `LiveSim.java` (scratchpad `live/`, `-Ddelay=600` = a far CDN, `/master.m3u8` = six variants with segment addresses of their own) reproduced it: 7.6 s, in the sim's log two segments of each variant, 0.6 s apart. `HlsProxy.rewrite` now fetches segments n-3 and n-2 of every live media playlist, plus the `#EXT-X-MAP` init segment (also for recorded streams), all at once through the existing `firstSegments` hand-out-once cache: 1.7 s. Only while a stream is being opened (20 s after `wrap`), at most 24 playlists per stream (`livePrefetched`, `openedAt`), no byte ranges; `-Dcloudstream.noprefetch` turns it off. Probing options (`demuxer-lavf-analyzeduration=1`, `probesize`) were tried on the sim: no gain, dropped.
- **Not done:** the same for DASH (`DashProxy` could fetch the init segments and first media segments of all Representations at the same time; Willow = 8 streams = ~10 s is the DASH case and is still slow); pruning variants was rejected (the player's quality menu is built from all of them). Channels that still fail need their log lines (`/log`, `app.log`).
- **"My pictures".** The owner then sent the saved Netflix page (`Downloads/Edit Profile - Account - Netflix_files`, 361 PNGs) and asked for those icons in the app. They are Netflix's artwork of its shows' characters: not copied into the app or the repository. Instead `Profiles.kt` lists the pictures in `<data>/avatars` (PNG, JPG, WebP, GIF) under "My pictures" in the editor with an "Open the folder" button; a click copies the file as the profile's picture (`copyProfilePicture`, shared with "Use a picture...").
- **Pictures.** Claude in Chrome was not connected, so the Netflix page could not be looked at; Netflix's own icons are theirs and are not copied into the app. Offered instead: the owner's own pictures (already: Settings > profile > "Use a picture..."), or a licensed set.

## 71. Live TV (2026-10-22): research on the phone's player, six faults found, the start rule

Owner: "too many problems with live TV: most are not playing, some load very heavily, some load but break after a while ... the seek bar is not proper; research how Android handles live TV, live seeking and prefetch"; then "if live seeking is available for more than 30 s the live should start from the last 30 s, or else whatever is available". The whole research, the table of what was wrong and the test rig are in **`docs/LIVE_TV.md`**; the short version:

- **Android** (`reference/cloudstream` CS3IPlayer/LiveManager + Media3 docs): opens only the streams it plays; starts 5 s behind the edge (`setLiveTargetOffsetMs(5000)`); the seek bar is the live window (duration = the window, position inside it), pinned at the end and "LIVE" at the edge; recovery on `BEHIND_LIVE_WINDOW` (seekToDefaultPosition + prepare), stuck playlists and network errors; no prefetch beyond the playing streams, no seek previews on live.
- **Fixed here:** (1) `MpvPlayer.release()` called `cancelAll()` on the proxies: zapping cancelled the NEXT channel's first requests and removed its proxy entry (404), mpv fell back to a direct play without live handling (and started seek previews on a live DASH channel: 0xC0000005 once); now each player cancels its own stream, `DashProxy/HlsProxy.wasLive(url)` keeps a retry live, no previews when live. (2) DNS (70d). (3) HLS first-segment prefetch (`HlsProxy.rewrite`, 7.6 s -> 1.7 s). (4) DASH prefetch (`DashPrefetch`, `DashProxy.startPrefetch`; segments served from memory, also for `Range: bytes=0-`; 5.3-6.4 s -> 2.4 s). (5) `LiveStart` (HLS: first playlist cut at the end; DASH: every manifest of the first 4 s cut at the front, startNumber/t moved on) = 30 s behind or the oldest picture; `LiveStartTest`, `DashPrefetchTest`. (6) the live clock in `PlayerSession` (`liveEdgeMs`, `liveBehindMs`, `liveAtEdge`, `liveElapsedMs`, `liveFraction`; both seek bars and the LIVE pill read them).
- **Test traps:** ffmpeg `-f dash` writes a final static manifest when it ends (a "live" test that suddenly has a 60 s duration and `live=false` is that); `/player` stops updating when the dev window is not the foreground window; the harness blocks long foreground sleeps: poll with Monitor or a background sleep.

## 72. Live TV rolled back to the GitHub version (2026-10-22)

The owner: "live streams completely broken, go to the live stream version from GitHub, keep all other changes, also remove the live stream seeking added now and all". Done by hand per hunk (the working tree has many other uncommitted changes that stay): `HlsProxy.kt`, `DashProxy.kt`, `PlayerSession.kt`, `PlayerChromeModern.kt` = `git checkout HEAD` (the touch `primaryContactPressed` lines were put back into the chrome); `MpvPlayer.kt` and `PlayerScreen.kt` = the live hunks only (`liveWindow`, `keepLiveBehind`, `wasLive`, the `cancelAll` calls in `release()`, the live window labels, bar mapping and pill); `LiveStart`, `DashPrefetch` and their tests deleted. Gone with it: the HLS live DVR window of section 69 (bar = the playlist's window, `windowSeconds`, `liveWindow`), the times beside a live bar (70) and everything of section 71. Kept: the DNS fallback and breaker (70d), the keyboard play/pause icon (`MpvPlayer.userPaused` is observable), touch swipes, `ArrowScrollRow`, My pictures. Sections 69 (live part), 70 (live times) and 71 describe code that is no longer in the tree; `docs/LIVE_TV.md` keeps the research and says which of its fixes are out. The code of 69-71 was copied to the session scratchpad (`backup-live/`, outside the repository).

## 73. Live DASH start inside the window, license channels, clearance after a network change, lost white-flash fix (2026-10-22 afternoon)

The owner, after trying the portable: "still no live is working, very few are working, JIO TV not playing anything, some lagging video, audio out of sync; the white patch is still appearing on the first video after the app started; put a Classics picture as default; the Cloudflare resolver is not working (AnimePahe)". Evidence came from the owner's own `dist/CloudStream-Portable/data/logs/app.log` (+ `app.log.1`) and a real JIO channel replayed in the dev app (the log of the owner holds the link, token and keys of the channel; the token lasts 6 h: `/playurl?url=...&ua=...&cookie=...&key=<hex>` plays a ClearKey channel in the dev app).

- **Not the app: `jt.drmlive.net` / `la.drmlive.net` answer 403 "Access denied."** to every client on this PC's network (AS55824 NKN, Cloudflare `warp=off`): curl with three user agents and the built-in Chromium. Willow, Maa HD, STAR Maa HD are on those hosts. JIO TV (IND) from the PlayFy extension (`jiotvmblive.cdn.jio.com`) plays. First check next time: `curl https://www.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace` (ip, warp) and the same manifest in the built-in browser.
- **JIO lag / audio out of sync = ffmpeg starting at the oldest segment.** Request trace (`-Dcloudstream.dashtrace=1`, DashProxy logs every request, answer and manifest): the audio segment times jumped by 2 to 8 segments every 6 to 14 s and the video skipped too ("Invalid audio PTS 15.9 -> 25.9", "Audio device underrun"). JIO lists a minute (`timeShiftBufferDepth` PT1M) and ffmpeg starts at the first listed segment, 56 s behind with 4 s of margin. `DashLiveStart` cuts the manifest the player gets: segments that ended before (newest - 30 s), the bound fixed with the first manifest so later ones are cut at the same place; < 36 s listed = whole; only SegmentTemplate + SegmentTimeline. After: 0 jumps in 55 s, A/V 0.00, first frame 4 s. `-Dcloudstream.dashfullwindow=1` turns it off for an A/B. 5 unit tests.
- **Start watchdog:** `watchStalls` limit 45 s while `player.opensLive() && !player.liveStream` (the earlier "only when startingSource" version did not apply: `loadingText` is already null while mpv is still opening, the log line `no progress for N ms (limit, starting, opening live)` showed it).
- **`ClearKeyLicense`:** a DrmExtractorLink with `licenseUrl` and no `key` used to fail with "ClearKey link without a key". Now fetched off the UI thread (manifest -> `default_KID`, POST `{"kids":[b64url],"type":"temporary"}`, then GET; JWK / map / array answers), cached per run, the load starts again with the key. 5 unit tests with a local server. NOT run against the real server (blocked here).
- **Cloudflare / AnimePahe:** in the owner's log the first session (15:03) shows `Saved CF cookies rejected (403) - purging` -> `Harvested new cf_clearance from system WebView` -> the extension's dialog `Clearance cookie found! Saving.` after 1 s -> `appGet retried -> 403` -> `CF dialog throttled` for ever; after a restart (15:38) the jar had no clearance, the dialog took 20 s (three `Just a moment...` loads) and the retry gave 200. The stale cookie had been issued the day before on another connection. `WebCookies.checkNetwork` (every 20 s at most, local): interface name + subnet of the default route (a UDP connect to 1.1.1.1:53 sends nothing), stored in `web/network.txt`; when it differs from the stored one the `cf_clearance` cookies are deleted (jar and engine). Checked in the dev app with a pretended other network (stale cookie gone, `keepme` kept). Dev `/setcookie?url=&value=`.
- **White flash, again:** the Session 44 fix (PlayerScreen `videoCovers = NativeVideo.hostAdded` gating `EvenPixels.hold(true)`, NativeVideoHost `canvas.isVisible=false` until 2 frames + 100 ms, overlay window opacity 0 until its first frames) was NOT in the working tree any more: `NativeVideo.kt` / `PlayerScreen.kt` carried the fork's `NativeOverlayWindow(focusable = true)` + `onPreviewKeyEvent`, and HEAD's `dialogsOpen` focus handling was gone. Recovered from the Session 44 transcript with `scratchpad/edits.js` (extracts every Edit of a file from a transcript jsonl; node is installed) and re-applied; the app's own focus design (`focusable = dialogsOpen`, `activateMainWindow`, focus back after a dialog) is back, the touch install loop stays. Rig: `scratchpad/seq.ps1` (captures the biggest window of a pid every ~30 ms, % near-white) + `cold.sh` (restart, wait, maximise, play a generated silent clip): 5 cold runs, worst 0.6 %.
- **Default profile picture:** `ProfileIconCatalog.coolDefaults` = The Classics (Scarlet Chilleez first). NOTE for the owner: the `profile_icons` WebPs and `ProfileIcons.kt` are untracked, they are Netflix's artwork of its own characters (another session added them), they should not go into a public release.

## 74. Special themes, public-DNS fallback, MovieBox referer (2026-10-23)

Owner: "anikoto anime from raghav repo not working; many sources from streamplay and cinestream not working including moviebox; add a red colored theme for netflix like effect; special themes section: netflix, prime video, hotstar, crunchyroll like themes, make no mistakes".

- **Method:** `runHarness <dir> raghav,phisher,cnc,megix full anikoto moviebox streamplay cinestream` (the `raghav` key is new in `ExtensionHarness.REPOS`) then `runLinkLab` over the same providers. The lab's mpv column is NOT reliable when it plays three links at once (tokens are single use, CDNs throttle: "OPENED but no progress" for links whose `exo` column is OK); a single link played alone with `-Dlinklab.url=<address> -Dlinklab.referer=...` is (AniKoto: first frame 2.9 s; MovieBox mp4 1080p plays).
- **AniKoto (raghav) = DNS.** The plugin finds links (4) but every one is on `fetch.nexabloom.top` (MegaPlay's proxy). This PC's system DNS is Cloudflare 1.1.1.2 (`nslookup` says `security.cloudflare-dns.com`): it answers `::` / `0.0.0.0` for that name, while 1.1.1.1 / 8.8.8.8 / 9.9.9.9 give Cloudflare addresses. DNS over HTTPS (the app's own setting) is blocked on this network (curl to the DoH hosts time out), so no setting could help. Same host serves Anikage MegaPlay, Animex, Anidap (StreamPlay); `move26.mbaccess.site` (CineStream MovieBlast) is NXDOMAIN everywhere (dead).
- **Fix:** `net/PublicDns` (plain UDP DNS query to 1.1.1.1 / 8.8.8.8 / 9.9.9.9 in parallel, A then AAAA, answers cached 30-300 s, 60 s negative cache), called from `UsableAddresses.resolveOrAskPublic` when the normal lookup throws UnknownHost or answers only unspecified addresses; the mpv proxy (`NetProxy`) and the playlist servers use the same client, so they get it too. Switch `public_dns_fallback_key` (default on) in Settings > General > Network. Dev `/dns?host=` prints `InetAddress`, `Dns.SYSTEM` and the app's client.
- **MovieBox inside CineStream = stale referer.** `bcdnw` / `bcdnxw` / `sbcdnw` .hakunaymatata.com answer 429 unless the referer is `https://movie-box.co/` (checked per link with curl: fmoviesunblocked.net, vidlink.pro, none, other MovieBox sites all 429). The MovieBox plugins send movie-box.co; CineStream sent fmoviesunblocked.net. `net/LinkRepair` (called from `APIRepository.loadLinks`) rewrites Referer / Origin for those three hosts; `bcdn.` (StreamPlay Vidlink) is signed for no referer and stays. Dev `/repolinks?provider=cinestream&q=Inception&only=MovieBox` shows every link with a range request answer (429 -> 206).
- **Not fixable in the app:** DahmerMovies (Cloudflare managed challenge on `p.111477.xyz/bulk`), Atlantic (`iad-01.transcode.cfd` has an AAAA record only, no IPv6 here), Anikage nero/pam (502/403 from the server).
- **Netflix interface (owner: "special themes meant a complete UI like Netflix", then "only Netflix, blended with my app, all features working"):** `ui/fluent/Skin.kt` (`Skin`, `SkinSpec`, `Skins.active` = the theme's skin unless `Appearance.skinLayout` (Settings "Full interface") is off; `Skins.pill` / `FluentShapes.card` come from the skin: 4 dp buttons, 6 dp cards), `Themes.specials` = Netflix only (#141414 / #E50914; ids prime, hotstar, crunchyroll of the first version fall back to Classic), `FluentTheme` forces dark while a skin is on. Shell: `ui/shell/SkinBars.kt` (`SkinTopBar`: transparent over artwork on a dark fade, solid page colour when `ShellState.topBarSolid`; narrow < 1040 dp = mark instead of name, < 780 dp = icon links; every control has `noWindowDrag`; `SkinProfileMenu` = avatar + caret + `MenuFlyout`), `SkinProfiles.kt` (`showProfileGate`: full-screen "Who's watching?", square avatars via `ProfileAvatar(shape=)`, Manage Profiles/Add Profile/Edit/Remove with the app's own `showProfileEditor`; `showAccountPicker` hands over to it while a skin is on, so the start-up picker is the gate). Home: `screens/home/SkinHome.kt` (`SkinHero` billboard, `SkinRowOverlap` 96 dp: the first section climbs into the billboard with a `layout` modifier; short windows drop the plot, then the genres, then shrink the logo), `HomeScreen` swaps the header/cards/row titles (`SkinRowHeader`), `playCard` for the cards' Play button. Cards: `ui/fluent/SkinCards.kt` (`SkinPosterCard` dispatched from `PosterCard`: `Modifier.zIndex` on the hovered card works inside a `LazyRow`, so it grows over its neighbours; info panel with `matchText` = score x 10; `SkinShelfArrow`; `SkinButton`). Page titles: `Skins.pageTitle` in `PageHeader`. Settings tile preview: `SkinPreview.kt`. Tests: `SkinsTest`, `ThemesTest`. The player: a Netflix-arranged player chrome was built first (owner: "also video player ui?") and then taken out again (owner: "remove the video player theme changes of netflix for now, use the default theme there"): `PlayerChromeModern.kt` is as it was before the skins; see below for how the player gets the Default look. The controls overlay is a separate window: dev `/overlayshot` (with `/player?hold=1&seekhover=0.4`) captures it, `/screenshot?sk=1` shows only the video's backdrop.
- **Themes and Colors apart (owner: "themes means this only the default and netflix inspired, the ones u put in settings is just colors, put it in a separate section called colors").** Settings > Appearance > Theme holds the app mode, the "Themes" block (two tiles: Default = `ThemeTile(Themes.byId(Appearance.palette))` named "Default", and Netflix), "Full interface" and a link to the new page `Page.Colors` ("Colors", `colorGroups()` -> `colorItems()` in `LookSettings.kt`: Reset colours, the palette gallery (title "Colour palette"), accent colour, button/chip/pill colour with live preview, button & pill style, coloured navigation highlight, surface & border tint). `Appearance.palette` (pref `palette`) remembers the Default theme's palette while Netflix is on (`pickTheme` sets it for every ordinary theme; `Appearance.load` takes the theme itself when it is ordinary); picking a palette while Netflix is on switches back to Default with it (the page says so); Reset colours resets the palette and leaves a special theme on. The Appearance page icon is now `Icons.Personalize` (E771), the palette glyph went to Colors. Overview tiles, the sidebar and the settings search take the new page from `Page.entries` / `groupsOf`.
- **The player keeps the Default look.** `Skins.inPlayer` (a state, set by `PlayerContent` 260 ms after the page opens, so the page before does not change under its own fade-out; cleared when the player page is left) makes `Skins.preset` the Default theme's palette (`Appearance.palette`) and `Skins.active` None; `FluentTheme` reads `Skins.preset`, and the native player's controls window has its own `FluentTheme` that reads the same state, so shapes (`FluentShapes`), the dark/light decision and the colours are the Default theme's there. Test: `SkinsTest.thePlayerKeepsTheDefaultThemeWhateverTheThemeIs`.
- **Version 1.0.7** (`app/build.gradle.kts`, `tools/installer/cloudstream.iss` twice); the keyboard shortcut lists (`Shortcuts.player`: F1 in the player and Settings > About) have "Hold Space" and "Hold click · Hold touch" (the 2× hold), and the Home key row said "Home200B".
